```
1→"""Scorecard badge image generator — produces a visual health summary PNG."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import importlib
     6→import logging
     7→import os
     8→from importlib import metadata as importlib_metadata
     9→from pathlib import Path
    10→
    11→from desloppify.app.output.scorecard_parts.dimensions import (
    12→    collapse_elegance_dimensions,
    13→    limit_scorecard_dimensions,
    14→    prepare_scorecard_dimensions,
    15→    resolve_scorecard_lang,
    16→)
    17→from desloppify.app.output.scorecard_parts.meta import (
    18→    resolve_package_version,
    19→    resolve_project_name,
    20→)
    21→from desloppify.app.output.scorecard_parts.theme import (
    22→    ACCENT,
    23→    BG,
    24→    BG_ROW_ALT,
    25→    BG_SCORE,
    26→    BG_TABLE,
    27→    BORDER,
    28→    DIM,
    29→    FRAME,
    30→    SCALE,
    31→    TEXT,
    32→    fmt_score,
    33→    load_font,
    34→    scale,
    35→    score_color,
    36→)
    37→from desloppify.core._internal.text_utils import PROJECT_ROOT
    38→from desloppify.state import get_overall_score, get_strict_score
    39→
    40→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    41→
    42→
    43→def generate_scorecard(state: dict, output_path: str | Path) -> Path:
    44→    """Render a landscape scorecard PNG from scan state. Returns the output path."""
    45→    image_mod = importlib.import_module("PIL.Image")  # deferred: optional dependency
    46→    image_draw_mod = importlib.import_module("PIL.ImageDraw")  # deferred: optional dependency
    47→    scorecard_draw_mod = importlib.import_module("desloppify.app.output.scorecard_parts.draw")  # deferred: depends on PIL
    48→
    49→    output_path = Path(output_path)
    50→
    51→    main_score = get_overall_score(state) or 0
    52→    strict_score = get_strict_score(state) or 0
    53→
    54→    project_name = resolve_project_name(PROJECT_ROOT)
    55→    package_version = resolve_package_version(
    56→        PROJECT_ROOT,
    57→        version_getter=importlib_metadata.version,
    58→        package_not_found_error=importlib_metadata.PackageNotFoundError,
    59→    )
    60→
    61→    # Layout — landscape (wide), File health first
    62→    active_dims = prepare_scorecard_dimensions(state)
    63→    row_count = len(active_dims)
    64→    row_h = scale(20)
    65→    width = scale(780)
    66→    divider_x = scale(260)
    67→    frame_inset = scale(5)
    68→
    69→    cols = 2
    70→    rows_per_col = (row_count + cols - 1) // cols
    71→    table_content_h = scale(14) + scale(4) + scale(6) + rows_per_col * row_h
    72→    content_h = max(table_content_h + scale(28), scale(150))
    73→    height = scale(12) + content_h
    74→
    75→    img = image_mod.new("RGB", (width, height), BG)
    76→    draw = image_draw_mod.Draw(img)
    77→
    78→    # Double frame
    79→    draw.rectangle((0, 0, width - 1, height - 1), outline=FRAME, width=scale(2))
    80→    draw.rectangle(
    81→        (frame_inset, frame_inset, width - frame_inset - 1, height - frame_inset - 1),
    82→        outline=BORDER,
    83→        width=1,
    84→    )
    85→
    86→    content_top = frame_inset + scale(1)
    87→    content_bot = height - frame_inset - scale(1)
    88→    content_mid_y = (content_top + content_bot) // 2
    89→
    90→    # Left panel: title + score + project name
    91→    scorecard_draw_mod.draw_left_panel(
    92→        draw,
    93→        main_score,
    94→        strict_score,
    95→        project_name,
    96→        package_version,
    97→        lp_left=frame_inset + scale(11),
    98→        lp_right=divider_x - scale(11),
    99→        lp_top=content_top + scale(4),
   100→        lp_bot=content_bot - scale(4),
   101→    )
   102→
   103→    # Vertical divider with ornament
   104→    scorecard_draw_mod.draw_vert_rule_with_ornament(
   105→        draw,
   106→        divider_x,
   107→        content_top + scale(12),
   108→        content_bot - scale(12),
   109→        content_mid_y,
   110→        BORDER,
   111→        ACCENT,
   112→    )
   113→
   114→    # Right panel: dimension table
   115→    scorecard_draw_mod.draw_right_panel(
   116→        draw,
   117→        active_dims,
   118→        row_h,
   119→        table_x1=divider_x + scale(11),
   120→        table_x2=width - frame_inset - scale(11),
   121→        table_top=content_top + scale(4),
   122→        table_bot=content_bot - scale(4),
   123→    )
   124→    output_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   125→    img.save(str(output_path), "PNG", optimize=True)
   126→    return output_path
   127→
   128→
   129→def get_badge_config(args, config: dict | None = None) -> tuple[Path | None, bool]:
   130→    """Resolve badge output path and whether badge generation is disabled.
   131→
   132→    Returns (output_path, disabled). Checks CLI args, then config, then env vars.
   133→    """
   134→    cfg = config or {}
   135→    disabled = getattr(args, "no_badge", False)
   136→    if not disabled:
   137→        disabled = not cfg.get("generate_scorecard", True)
   138→    if not disabled:
   139→        disabled = os.environ.get("DESLOPPIFY_NO_BADGE", "").lower() in (
   140→            "1",
   141→            "true",
   142→            "yes",
   143→        )
   144→    if disabled:
   145→        return None, True
   146→
   147→    path_str = (
   148→        getattr(args, "badge_path", None)
   149→        or cfg.get("badge_path")
   150→        or os.environ.get("DESLOPPIFY_BADGE_PATH", "scorecard.png")
   151→    )
   152→    path = Path(path_str)
   153→    # On Windows, "/tmp/foo.png" is root-anchored but drive-relative.
   154→    # Treat any rooted path as user-intended absolute-like input.
   155→    is_root_anchored = bool(path.root)
   156→    if not path.is_absolute() and not is_root_anchored:
   157→        path = PROJECT_ROOT / path
   158→    return path, False
   159→
   160→
   161→def _scorecard_ignore_warning(state: dict) -> str | None:
   162→    """Return an ignore-suppression warning line for scorecard context."""
   163→    info = state.get("ignore_integrity", {}) if isinstance(state, dict) else {}
   164→    if not isinstance(info, dict):
   165→        return None
   166→    ignored = int(info.get("ignored", 0) or 0)
   167→    if ignored <= 0:
   168→        return None
   169→
   170→    suppressed_pct = float(info.get("suppressed_pct", 0.0) or 0.0)
   171→    rounded = round(suppressed_pct)
   172→    level = "high" if suppressed_pct >= 50 else "moderate"
   173→    return (
   174→        f"Ignore suppression is {rounded}% ({level}) "
   175→        f"across {ignored} findings."
   176→    )
   177→
   178→
   179→__all__ = [
   180→    "ACCENT",
   181→    "BG",
   182→    "BG_ROW_ALT",
   183→    "BG_SCORE",
   184→    "BG_TABLE",
   185→    "BORDER",
   186→    "DIM",
   187→    "FRAME",

   195→    "score_color",
   196→    "generate_scorecard",

   199→]
```

```
1→"""Scorecard panel drawing helpers used by the renderer."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.app.output.scorecard_parts.left_panel import draw_left_panel
     6→from desloppify.app.output.scorecard_parts.ornaments import (
     7→    draw_ornament,
     8→    draw_rule_with_ornament,
     9→    draw_vert_rule_with_ornament,
    10→)
    11→from desloppify.app.output.scorecard_parts.theme import (
    12→    BG_ROW_ALT,
    13→    BG_TABLE,
    14→    BORDER,
    15→    TEXT,
    16→    fmt_score,
    17→    load_font,
    18→    scale,
    19→    score_color,
    20→)
    21→
    22→
    23→def draw_right_panel(
    24→    draw,
    25→    active_dims: list,
    26→    row_h: int,
    27→    table_x1: int,
    28→    table_x2: int,
    29→    table_top: int,
    30→    table_bot: int,
    31→) -> None:
    32→    """Draw the right panel: two separate dimension tables side by side."""
    33→    font_row = load_font(11, mono=True)
    34→    font_strict = load_font(9, mono=True)
    35→    row_count = len(active_dims)
    36→
    37→    cols = 2
    38→    rows_per_col = (row_count + cols - 1) // cols
    39→    table_width = table_x2 - table_x1
    40→    grid_gap = scale(8)
    41→    grid_width = (table_width - grid_gap) // cols
    42→
    43→    for col_index in range(cols):
    44→        grid_x1 = table_x1 + col_index * (grid_width + grid_gap)
    45→        grid_x2 = grid_x1 + grid_width
    46→        draw.rounded_rectangle(
    47→            (grid_x1, table_top, grid_x2, table_bot),
    48→            radius=scale(4),
    49→            fill=BG_TABLE,
    50→            outline=BORDER,
    51→            width=1,
    52→        )
    53→
    54→        name_col_width = scale(132)
    55→        value_col_gap = scale(4)
    56→        value_col_width = scale(34)
    57→        total_content_width = (
    58→            name_col_width
    59→            + value_col_gap
    60→            + value_col_width
    61→            + value_col_gap
    62→            + value_col_width
    63→        )
    64→        block_left = grid_x1 + (grid_width - total_content_width) // 2
    65→        name_col_x = block_left
    66→        health_col_x = name_col_x + name_col_width + value_col_gap
    67→        strict_col_x = health_col_x + value_col_width + value_col_gap + scale(4)
    68→
    69→        rows_this_col = min(rows_per_col, row_count - col_index * rows_per_col)
    70→        content_height = rows_this_col * row_h
    71→        content_top = (table_top + table_bot) // 2 - content_height // 2
    72→
    73→        sample_bbox = draw.textbbox((0, 0), "Xg", font=font_row)
    74→        row_text_height = sample_bbox[3] - sample_bbox[1]
    75→        row_text_offset = sample_bbox[1]
    76→        start_idx = col_index * rows_per_col
    77→
    78→        for row_index in range(rows_this_col):
    79→            dim_idx = start_idx + row_index
    80→            if dim_idx >= row_count:
    81→                break
    82→            name, data = active_dims[dim_idx]
    83→            band_top = content_top + row_index * row_h
    84→            band_bottom = band_top + row_h
    85→            if row_index % 2 == 1:
    86→                draw.rectangle(
    87→                    (grid_x1 + 1, band_top, grid_x2 - 1, band_bottom), fill=BG_ROW_ALT
    88→                )
    89→            text_y = band_top + (row_h - row_text_height) // 2 - row_text_offset + scale(1)
    90→            score = data.get("score", 100)
    91→            strict = data.get("strict", score)
    92→
    93→            max_name_width = name_col_width - scale(2)
    94→            while (
    95→                name
    96→                and draw.textlength(name + "\u2026", font=font_row) > max_name_width
    97→            ):
    98→                name = name[:-1]
    99→            if draw.textlength(name, font=font_row) > max_name_width:
   100→                name = name.rstrip() + "\u2026"
   101→
   102→            draw.text((name_col_x, text_y), name, fill=TEXT, font=font_row)
   103→            draw.text(
   104→                (health_col_x, text_y),
   105→                f"{fmt_score(score)}%",
   106→                fill=score_color(score),
   107→                font=font_row,
   108→            )
   109→
   110→            strict_text = f"{fmt_score(strict)}%"
   111→            strict_bbox = draw.textbbox((0, 0), strict_text, font=font_strict)
   112→            strict_text_height = strict_bbox[3] - strict_bbox[1]
   113→            strict_y = band_top + (row_h - strict_text_height) // 2 - strict_bbox[1]
   114→            draw.text(
   115→                (strict_col_x, strict_y),
   116→                strict_text,
   117→                fill=score_color(strict, muted=True),
   118→                font=font_strict,
   119→            )
   120→
   121→__all__ = [
   122→    "draw_left_panel",
   123→    "draw_right_panel",
   124→    "draw_ornament",
   125→    "draw_rule_with_ornament",
   126→    "draw_vert_rule_with_ornament",
   127→]
   128→
```

```
1→"""Visual style primitives shared by scorecard rendering modules."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import importlib
     6→import logging
     7→
     8→from desloppify.core.fallbacks import log_best_effort_failure
     9→
    10→# Render at 2x for retina/high-DPI crispness
    11→SCALE = 2
    12→
    13→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    14→
    15→
    16→def score_color(score: float, *, muted: bool = False) -> tuple[int, int, int]:
    17→    """Color-code a score: deep sage >= 90, mustard 70-90, dusty rose < 70.
    18→
    19→    muted=True returns a desaturated variant for secondary display (strict column).
    20→    """
    21→    if score >= 90:
    22→        base = (68, 120, 68)  # deep sage
    23→    elif score >= 70:
    24→        base = (120, 140, 72)  # olive green
    25→    else:
    26→        base = (145, 155, 80)  # yellow-green
    27→    if not muted:
    28→        return base
    29→    # Pastel orange shades for strict column
    30→    if score >= 90:
    31→        return (195, 160, 115)  # light sandy peach
    32→    if score >= 70:
    33→        return (200, 148, 100)  # warm apricot
    34→    return (195, 125, 95)  # soft coral
    35→
    36→
    37→def load_font(
    38→    size: int, *, serif: bool = False, bold: bool = False, mono: bool = False
    39→):
    40→    """Load a font with cross-platform fallback."""
    41→    image_font_mod = importlib.import_module("PIL.ImageFont")  # deferred: optional dependency
    42→
    43→    size = size * SCALE
    44→    candidates = []
    45→    if mono:
    46→        candidates = [
    47→            "/System/Library/Fonts/SFNSMono.ttf",
    48→            "/usr/share/fonts/truetype/dejavu/DejaVuSansMono.ttf",
    49→            "/usr/share/fonts/truetype/liberation/LiberationMono-Regular.ttf",
    50→        ]
    51→    elif serif and bold:
    52→        candidates = [
    53→            "/System/Library/Fonts/Supplemental/Georgia Bold.ttf",
    54→            "/System/Library/Fonts/NewYork.ttf",
    55→            "/usr/share/fonts/truetype/dejavu/DejaVuSerif-Bold.ttf",
    56→            "/usr/share/fonts/truetype/liberation/LiberationSerif-Bold.ttf",
    57→        ]
    58→    elif serif:
    59→        candidates = [
    60→            "/System/Library/Fonts/Supplemental/Georgia.ttf",
    61→            "/System/Library/Fonts/NewYork.ttf",
    62→            "/usr/share/fonts/truetype/dejavu/DejaVuSerif.ttf",
    63→            "/usr/share/fonts/truetype/liberation/LiberationSerif-Regular.ttf",
    64→        ]
    65→    elif bold:
    66→        candidates = [
    67→            "/System/Library/Fonts/SFCompact.ttf",
    68→            "/System/Library/Fonts/HelveticaNeue.ttc",
    69→            "/usr/share/fonts/truetype/dejavu/DejaVuSans-Bold.ttf",
    70→        ]
    71→    else:
    72→        candidates = [
    73→            "/System/Library/Fonts/SFCompact.ttf",
    74→            "/System/Library/Fonts/HelveticaNeue.ttc",
    75→            "/usr/share/fonts/truetype/dejavu/DejaVuSans.ttf",
    76→        ]
    77→    for path in candidates:
    78→        try:
    79→            return image_font_mod.truetype(path, size)
    80→        except OSError as exc:
    81→            log_best_effort_failure(
    82→                logger, f"load scorecard font candidate {path}", exc
    83→            )
    84→            continue
    85→    return image_font_mod.load_default()
    86→
    87→
    88→def scale(v: int | float) -> int:
    89→    """Scale a layout value."""
    90→    return int(v * SCALE)
    91→
    92→
    93→def fmt_score(score: float) -> str:
    94→    """Format score without .0 for whole numbers."""
    95→    if score == int(score):
    96→        return f"{int(score)}"
    97→    return f"{score:.1f}"
    98→
    99→
   100→# -- Palette used by all drawing functions --
   101→BG = (247, 240, 228)
   102→BG_SCORE = (240, 232, 217)
   103→BG_TABLE = (240, 233, 220)
   104→BG_ROW_ALT = (234, 226, 212)
   105→TEXT = (58, 48, 38)
   106→DIM = (138, 122, 102)
   107→BORDER = (192, 176, 152)
   108→ACCENT = (148, 112, 82)
   109→FRAME = (172, 152, 126)
   110→
   111→
   112→__all__ = [
   113→    "ACCENT",
   114→    "BG",
   115→    "BG_ROW_ALT",
   116→    "BG_SCORE",
   117→    "BG_TABLE",

   127→]
```

```
1→"""Dimension projection policy for scorecard rendering."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.app.output.scorecard_parts.dimension_policy import (
     6→    _DEFAULT_ELEGANCE_COMPONENTS,
     7→    _ELEGANCE_COMPONENTS_BY_LANG,
     8→    _SUBJECTIVE_SCORECARD_ORDER_BY_LANG,
     9→    _SUBJECTIVE_SCORECARD_ORDER_DEFAULT,
    10→)
    11→from desloppify.app.output.scorecard_parts.dimension_policy import (
    12→    _SCORECARD_MAX_DIMENSIONS as SCORECARD_MAX_DIMENSIONS,
    13→)
    14→
    15→
    16→def resolve_scorecard_lang(state: dict) -> str | None:
    17→    """Best-effort current scan language key for scorecard display policy."""
    18→    history = state.get("scan_history")
    19→    if isinstance(history, list):
    20→        for entry in reversed(history):
    21→            if not isinstance(entry, dict):
    22→                continue
    23→            lang = entry.get("lang")
    24→            if isinstance(lang, str) and lang.strip():
    25→                return lang.strip().lower()
    26→
    27→    capabilities = state.get("lang_capabilities")
    28→    if isinstance(capabilities, dict) and len(capabilities) == 1:
    29→        only_lang = next(iter(capabilities.keys()))
    30→        if isinstance(only_lang, str) and only_lang.strip():
    31→            return only_lang.strip().lower()
    32→
    33→    findings = state.get("findings")
    34→    if isinstance(findings, dict):
    35→        counts: dict[str, int] = {}
    36→        for finding in findings.values():
    37→            if not isinstance(finding, dict):
    38→                continue
    39→            lang = finding.get("lang")
    40→            if isinstance(lang, str) and lang.strip():
    41→                key = lang.strip().lower()
    42→                counts[key] = counts.get(key, 0) + 1
    43→        if counts:
    44→            return max(counts, key=counts.get)
    45→    return None
    46→
    47→
    48→def is_unassessed_subjective_placeholder(data: dict) -> bool:
    49→    return (
    50→        "subjective_assessment" in data.get("detectors", {})
    51→        and data.get("score", 0) == 0
    52→        and data.get("issues", 0) == 0
    53→    )
    54→
    55→
    56→def collapse_elegance_dimensions(
    57→    active_dims: list[tuple[str, dict]],
    58→    *,
    59→    lang_key: str | None,
    60→) -> list[tuple[str, dict]]:
    61→    """Collapse High/Mid/Low elegance rows into one aggregate display row."""
    62→    component_names = set(
    63→        _ELEGANCE_COMPONENTS_BY_LANG.get(lang_key or "", _DEFAULT_ELEGANCE_COMPONENTS)
    64→    )
    65→    elegance_rows = [
    66→        (name, data) for name, data in active_dims if name in component_names
    67→    ]
    68→    if not elegance_rows:
    69→        return active_dims
    70→
    71→    remaining_rows = [
    72→        (name, data) for name, data in active_dims if name not in component_names
    73→    ]
    74→    count = len(elegance_rows)
    75→    score_avg = round(
    76→        sum(float(data.get("score", 100.0)) for _, data in elegance_rows) / count, 1
    77→    )
    78→    strict_avg = round(
    79→        sum(
    80→            float(data.get("strict", data.get("score", 100.0)))
    81→            for _, data in elegance_rows
    82→        )
    83→        / count,
    84→        1,
    85→    )
    86→    checks_total = sum(int(data.get("checks", 0)) for _, data in elegance_rows)
    87→    issues_total = sum(int(data.get("issues", 0)) for _, data in elegance_rows)
    88→    tier = max(int(data.get("tier", 4)) for _, data in elegance_rows)
    89→    placeholder_flags = [
    90→        bool(
    91→            data.get("detectors", {})
    92→            .get("subjective_assessment", {})
    93→            .get("placeholder")
    94→        )
    95→        for _, data in elegance_rows
    96→    ]
    97→
    98→    label = "Elegance"
    99→    if any(name.lower() == label.lower() for name, _ in remaining_rows):
   100→        label = "Elegance (combined)"
   101→
   102→    pass_rate = round(score_avg / 100.0, 4)
   103→    return [
   104→        *remaining_rows,
   105→        (
   106→            label,
   107→            {
   108→                "score": score_avg,
   109→                "strict": strict_avg,
   110→                "checks": checks_total,
   111→                "issues": issues_total,
   112→                "tier": tier,
   113→                "detectors": {
   114→                    "subjective_assessment": {
   115→                        "potential": checks_total,
   116→                        "pass_rate": pass_rate,
   117→                        "issues": issues_total,
   118→                        "weighted_failures": round(checks_total * (1 - pass_rate), 4),
   119→                        "components": [name for name, _ in elegance_rows],
   120→                        "placeholder": any(placeholder_flags),
   121→                    }
   122→                },
   123→            },
   124→        ),
   125→    ]
   126→
   127→
   128→def limit_scorecard_dimensions(
   129→    active_dims: list[tuple[str, dict]],
   130→    *,
   131→    lang_key: str | None,
   132→    max_rows: int = SCORECARD_MAX_DIMENSIONS,
   133→) -> list[tuple[str, dict]]:
   134→    """Limit scorecard rows with language-specific subjective priority."""
   135→    if len(active_dims) <= max_rows:
   136→        return active_dims
   137→
   138→    mechanical = [
   139→        (name, data)
   140→        for name, data in active_dims
   141→        if "subjective_assessment" not in data.get("detectors", {})
   142→    ]
   143→    subjective = [
   144→        (name, data)
   145→        for name, data in active_dims
   146→        if "subjective_assessment" in data.get("detectors", {})
   147→    ]
   148→    if len(mechanical) >= max_rows:
   149→        return mechanical[:max_rows]
   150→
   151→    budget = max_rows - len(mechanical)
   152→    preferred_order = _SUBJECTIVE_SCORECARD_ORDER_BY_LANG.get(
   153→        lang_key or "",
   154→        _SUBJECTIVE_SCORECARD_ORDER_DEFAULT,
   155→    )
   156→
   157→    remaining = {name: (name, data) for name, data in subjective}
   158→    selected: list[tuple[str, dict]] = []
   159→    for name in preferred_order:
   160→        row = remaining.pop(name, None)
   161→        if row is None:
   162→            continue
   163→        selected.append(row)
   164→        if len(selected) >= budget:
   165→            break
   166→
   167→    if len(selected) < budget and remaining:
   168→        extras = sorted(
   169→            remaining.values(),
   170→            key=lambda item: (
   171→                float(item[1].get("strict", item[1].get("score", 100.0))),
   172→                item[0],
   173→            ),
   174→        )
   175→        selected.extend(extras[: budget - len(selected)])
   176→
   177→    return [*mechanical, *selected]
   178→
   179→
   180→def prepare_scorecard_dimensions(state: dict) -> list[tuple[str, dict]]:
   181→    """Prepare scorecard rows (active, elegance-collapsed, capped).
   182→
   183→    Dimensions are derived dynamically from what the scoring engine produced
   184→    in ``dimension_scores`` — no per-language hard-coded dimension list.
   185→    Unassessed subjective placeholders (score 0, no issues) are excluded so
   186→    the scorecard only shows dimensions that have real data.
   187→    """
   188→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {})
   189→    if not isinstance(dim_scores, dict):
   190→        return []
   191→
   192→    all_dims = [
   193→        (name, data) for name, data in dim_scores.items() if isinstance(data, dict)
   194→    ]
   195→
   196→    [REDACTED](state)
   197→    all_dims = collapse_elegance_dimensions(all_dims, lang_key=lang_key)
   198→
   199→    # Show dimensions with real data; skip unassessed subjective placeholders.
   200→    active_dims = [
   201→        (name, data)
   202→        for name, data in all_dims
   203→        if data.get("checks", 0) > 0 and not is_unassessed_subjective_placeholder(data)
   204→    ]
   205→    active_dims = limit_scorecard_dimensions(active_dims, lang_key=lang_key)
   206→    active_dims.sort(key=lambda x: (0 if x[0] == "File health" else 1, x[0]))
   207→    return active_dims
   208→
   209→
   210→__all__ = [
   211→    "SCORECARD_MAX_DIMENSIONS",
   212→    "collapse_elegance_dimensions",
   213→    "prepare_scorecard_dimensions",
   214→    "limit_scorecard_dimensions",
   215→    "resolve_scorecard_lang",
   216→]
   217→
```

```
1→"""Left panel drawing helpers for the scorecard."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.app.output.scorecard_parts.ornaments import draw_rule_with_ornament
     6→from desloppify.app.output.scorecard_parts.theme import (
     7→    ACCENT,
     8→    BG,
     9→    BG_SCORE,
    10→    BORDER,
    11→    DIM,
    12→    TEXT,
    13→    fmt_score,
    14→    load_font,

    18→
    19→
    20→def _draw_left_panel_version(
    21→    draw,
    22→    *,
    23→    center_x: int,
    24→    baseline_y: int,
    25→    version_text: str,
    26→    version_bbox,
    27→    version_width: float,
    28→    font_version,
    29→) -> None:
    30→    draw.text(
    31→        (center_x - version_width / 2, baseline_y - version_bbox[1]),
    32→        version_text,
    33→        fill=DIM,
    34→        font=font_version,
    35→    )
    36→
    37→
    38→def _draw_left_panel_title(
    39→    draw,
    40→    *,
    41→    center_x: int,
    42→    title_y: int,
    43→    title: str,
    44→    title_bbox,
    45→    title_width: float,
    46→    font_title,
    47→) -> None:
    48→    draw.text(
    49→        (center_x - title_width / 2, title_y - title_bbox[1]),
    50→        title,
    51→        fill=TEXT,
    52→        font=font_title,
    53→    )
    54→
    55→
    56→def _draw_left_panel_score(
    57→    draw,
    58→    *,
    59→    center_x: int,
    60→    score_y: int,
    61→    score_text: str,
    62→    score_bbox,
    63→    score_value: float,
    64→    font_big,
    65→) -> None:
    66→    score_width = draw.textlength(score_text, font=font_big)
    67→    draw.text(
    68→        (center_x - score_width / 2, score_y - score_bbox[1]),
    69→        score_text,
    70→        fill=score_color(score_value),
    71→        font=font_big,
    72→    )
    73→
    74→
    75→def _draw_left_panel_strict(
    76→    draw,
    77→    *,
    78→    center_x: int,
    79→    strict_y: int,
    80→    strict_value: float,
    81→    strict_text: str,
    82→    strict_label_bbox,
    83→    strict_value_bbox,
    84→    font_strict_label,
    85→    font_strict_val,
    86→) -> None:
    87→    strict_label = "strict"
    88→    label_width = draw.textlength(strict_label, font=font_strict_label)
    89→    value_width = draw.textlength(strict_text, font=font_strict_val)
    90→    gap = scale(5)
    91→    strict_x = center_x - (label_width + gap + value_width) / 2
    92→    draw.text(
    93→        (strict_x, strict_y - strict_label_bbox[1]),
    94→        strict_label,
    95→        fill=DIM,
    96→        font=font_strict_label,
    97→    )
    98→    draw.text(
    99→        (strict_x + label_width + gap, strict_y - strict_value_bbox[1]),
   100→        strict_text,
   101→        fill=score_color(strict_value, muted=True),
   102→        font=font_strict_val,
   103→    )
   104→
   105→
   106→def _draw_left_panel_project_pill(
   107→    draw,
   108→    *,
   109→    center_x: int,
   110→    strict_y: int,
   111→    strict_height: int,
   112→    project_gap: int,
   113→    project_pill_height: int,
   114→    pill_pad_y: int,
   115→    pill_pad_x: int,
   116→    project_name: str,
   117→    project_bbox,
   118→    font_project,
   119→) -> None:
   120→    pill_top = strict_y + strict_height + project_gap
   121→    project_y = pill_top + pill_pad_y
   122→    project_width = draw.textlength(project_name, font=font_project)
   123→    pill_left = center_x - project_width / 2 - pill_pad_x
   124→    pill_right = center_x + project_width / 2 + pill_pad_x
   125→    pill_bottom = pill_top + project_pill_height
   126→    draw.rounded_rectangle(
   127→        (pill_left, pill_top, pill_right, pill_bottom),
   128→        radius=scale(3),
   129→        fill=BG,
   130→        outline=BORDER,
   131→        width=1,
   132→    )
   133→    draw.text(
   134→        (center_x - project_width / 2, project_y - project_bbox[1]),
   135→        project_name,
   136→        fill=DIM,
   137→        font=font_project,
   138→    )
   139→
   140→
   141→def _left_panel_measurements(
   142→    draw,
   143→    *,
   144→    main_score: float,
   145→    strict_score: float,
   146→    project_name: str,
   147→    package_version: str,
   148→) -> dict:
   149→    font_version = load_font(9, mono=True)
   150→    font_title = load_font(15, serif=True, bold=True)
   151→    font_big = load_font(42, serif=True, bold=True)
   152→    font_strict_label = load_font(12, serif=True)
   153→    font_strict_val = load_font(19, serif=True, bold=True)
   154→    font_project = load_font(9, serif=True)
   155→    version_text = (
   156→        f"v{package_version}"
   157→        if package_version and package_version != "unknown"
   158→        else "version unknown"
   159→    )
   160→    title = "DESLOPPIFY SCORE"
   161→    score_text = fmt_score(main_score)
   162→    strict_text = fmt_score(strict_score)
   163→    version_bbox = draw.textbbox((0, 0), version_text, font=font_version)
   164→    title_bbox = draw.textbbox((0, 0), title, font=font_title)
   165→    score_bbox = draw.textbbox((0, 0), score_text, font=font_big)
   166→    strict_label_bbox = draw.textbbox((0, 0), "strict", font=font_strict_label)
   167→    strict_value_bbox = draw.textbbox((0, 0), strict_text, font=font_strict_val)
   168→    project_bbox = draw.textbbox((0, 0), project_name, font=font_project)
   169→    version_h = version_bbox[3] - version_bbox[1]
   170→    title_h = title_bbox[3] - title_bbox[1]
   171→    score_h = score_bbox[3] - score_bbox[1]
   172→    strict_h = max(
   173→        strict_label_bbox[3] - strict_label_bbox[1],
   174→        strict_value_bbox[3] - strict_value_bbox[1],
   175→    )
   176→    project_h = project_bbox[3] - project_bbox[1]
   177→    return {
   178→        "font_version": font_version,
   179→        "font_title": font_title,
   180→        "font_big": font_big,
   181→        "font_strict_label": font_strict_label,
   182→        "font_strict_val": font_strict_val,
   183→        "font_project": font_project,
   184→        "version_text": version_text,
   185→        "title": title,
   186→        "score_text": score_text,
   187→        "strict_text": strict_text,
   188→        "version_bbox": version_bbox,
   189→        "title_bbox": title_bbox,
   190→        "score_bbox": score_bbox,
   191→        "strict_label_bbox": strict_label_bbox,
   192→        "strict_value_bbox": strict_value_bbox,
   193→        "project_bbox": project_bbox,
   194→        "version_h": version_h,
   195→        "title_h": title_h,
   196→        "score_h": score_h,
   197→        "strict_h": strict_h,
   198→        "project_h": project_h,
   199→        "version_width": draw.textlength(version_text, font=font_version),
   200→        "title_width": draw.textlength(title, font=font_title),
   201→    }
   202→
   203→
   204→def draw_left_panel(
   205→    draw,
   206→    main_score: float,
   207→    strict_score: float,
   208→    project_name: str,
   209→    package_version: str,
   210→    lp_left: int,
   211→    lp_right: int,
   212→    lp_top: int,
   213→    lp_bot: int,
   214→) -> None:
   215→    measurements = _left_panel_measurements(
   216→        draw,
   217→        main_score=main_score,
   218→        strict_score=strict_score,
   219→        project_name=project_name,
   220→        package_version=package_version,
   221→    )
   222→    font_version = measurements["font_version"]
   223→    font_title = measurements["font_title"]
   224→    font_big = measurements["font_big"]
   225→    font_strict_label = measurements["font_strict_label"]
   226→    font_strict_val = measurements["font_strict_val"]
   227→    font_project = measurements["font_project"]
   228→    version_text = measurements["version_text"]
   229→    title = measurements["title"]
   230→    score_text = measurements["score_text"]
   231→    strict_text = measurements["strict_text"]
   232→    version_bbox = measurements["version_bbox"]
   233→    title_bbox = measurements["title_bbox"]
   234→    score_bbox = measurements["score_bbox"]
   235→    strict_label_bbox = measurements["strict_label_bbox"]
   236→    strict_value_bbox = measurements["strict_value_bbox"]
   237→    project_bbox = measurements["project_bbox"]
   238→    version_h = measurements["version_h"]
   239→    title_h = measurements["title_h"]
   240→    score_h = measurements["score_h"]
   241→    strict_h = measurements["strict_h"]
   242→    project_h = measurements["project_h"]
   243→    version_width = measurements["version_width"]
   244→    title_width = measurements["title_width"]
   245→    lp_center = (lp_left + lp_right) // 2
   246→
   247→    draw.rounded_rectangle(
   248→        (lp_left, lp_top, lp_right, lp_bot),
   249→        radius=scale(4),
   250→        fill=BG_SCORE,
   251→        outline=BORDER,
   252→        width=1,
   253→    )
   254→
   255→    version_gap = scale(4)
   256→    ornament_gap = scale(7)
   257→    score_gap = scale(6)
   258→    project_gap = scale(8)
   259→    pill_pad_y = scale(3)
   260→    pill_pad_x = scale(8)
   261→    project_pill_height = project_h + 2 * pill_pad_y
   262→    total_h = (
   263→        version_h
   264→        + version_gap
   265→        + title_h
   266→        + ornament_gap
   267→        + scale(6)
   268→        + ornament_gap
   269→        + score_h
   270→        + score_gap
   271→        + strict_h
   272→        + project_gap
   273→        + project_pill_height
   274→    )
   275→    base_y = (lp_top + lp_bot) // 2 - total_h // 2 + scale(3)
   276→    _draw_left_panel_version(
   277→        draw,
   278→        center_x=lp_center,
   279→        baseline_y=base_y,
   280→        version_text=version_text,
   281→        version_bbox=version_bbox,
   282→        version_width=version_width,
   283→        font_version=font_version,
   284→    )
   285→    title_y = base_y + version_h + version_gap
   286→    _draw_left_panel_title(
   287→        draw,
   288→        center_x=lp_center,
   289→        title_y=title_y,
   290→        title=title,
   291→        title_bbox=title_bbox,
   292→        title_width=title_width,
   293→        font_title=font_title,
   294→    )
   295→    rule_y = title_y + title_h + ornament_gap
   296→    draw_rule_with_ornament(
   297→        draw,
   298→        rule_y,
   299→        lp_left + scale(28),
   300→        lp_right - scale(28),
   301→        lp_center,
   302→        BORDER,
   303→        ACCENT,
   304→    )
   305→    score_y = rule_y + scale(6) + ornament_gap
   306→    _draw_left_panel_score(
   307→        draw,
   308→        center_x=lp_center,
   309→        score_y=score_y,
   310→        score_text=score_text,
   311→        score_bbox=score_bbox,
   312→        score_value=main_score,
   313→        font_big=font_big,
   314→    )
   315→    strict_y = score_y + score_h + score_gap
   316→    _draw_left_panel_strict(
   317→        draw,
   318→        center_x=lp_center,
   319→        strict_y=strict_y,
   320→        strict_value=strict_score,
   321→        strict_text=strict_text,
   322→        strict_label_bbox=strict_label_bbox,
   323→        strict_value_bbox=strict_value_bbox,
   324→        font_strict_label=font_strict_label,
   325→        font_strict_val=font_strict_val,
   326→    )
   327→    _draw_left_panel_project_pill(
   328→        draw,
   329→        center_x=lp_center,
   330→        strict_y=strict_y,
   331→        strict_height=strict_h,
   332→        project_gap=project_gap,
   333→        project_pill_height=project_pill_height,
   334→        pill_pad_y=pill_pad_y,
   335→        pill_pad_x=pill_pad_x,
   336→        project_name=project_name,
   337→        project_bbox=project_bbox,
   338→        font_project=font_project,
   339→    )
   340→__all__ = ["draw_left_panel"]
   341→
```

```
1→"""Metadata helpers for scorecard rendering."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import logging
     6→import re
     7→import subprocess
     8→from collections.abc import Callable
     9→from pathlib import Path
    10→
    11→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    12→
    13→
    14→def resolve_project_name(project_root: Path) -> str:
    15→    """Resolve owner/repo display name from GitHub CLI, git remote, or folder."""
    16→    try:
    17→        name = subprocess.check_output(
    18→            ["gh", "repo", "view", "--json", "nameWithOwner", "-q", ".nameWithOwner"],
    19→            cwd=str(project_root),
    20→            stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL,
    21→            text=True,
    22→            timeout=5,
    23→        ).strip()
    24→        if "/" in name:
    25→            return name
    26→    except (
    27→        subprocess.CalledProcessError,
    28→        FileNotFoundError,
    29→        subprocess.TimeoutExpired,
    30→    ) as exc:
    31→        logger.debug("gh repo view failed, falling back to git remote: %s", exc)
    32→
    33→    try:
    34→        url = subprocess.check_output(
    35→            ["git", "config", "--get", "remote.origin.url"],
    36→            cwd=str(project_root),
    37→            stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL,
    38→            text=True,
    39→            timeout=5,
    40→        ).strip()
    41→        if url.startswith("git@") and ":" in url:
    42→            path = url.split(":")[-1]
    43→        else:
    44→            path = "/".join(url.split("/")[-2:])
    45→        return path.removesuffix(".git")
    46→    except (
    47→        subprocess.CalledProcessError,
    48→        FileNotFoundError,
    49→        IndexError,
    50→        subprocess.TimeoutExpired,
    51→    ):
    52→        return project_root.name
    53→
    54→
    55→def resolve_package_version(
    56→    project_root: Path,
    57→    *,
    58→    version_getter: Callable[[str], str],
    59→    package_not_found_error: type[Exception],
    60→) -> str:
    61→    """Resolve package version from installed metadata or local pyproject."""
    62→    try:
    63→        return version_getter("desloppify")
    64→    except package_not_found_error as exc:
    65→        logger.debug("Package metadata lookup failed, trying pyproject.toml: %s", exc)
    66→
    67→    pyproject_path = project_root / "pyproject.toml"
    68→    try:
    69→        text = pyproject_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    70→        match = re.search(r'^\s*version\s*=\s*"([^"]+)"\s*$', text, re.MULTILINE)
    71→        if match:
    72→            return match.group(1)
    73→    except OSError as exc:
    74→        logger.debug("Failed to read pyproject.toml for version: %s", exc)
    75→
    76→    return "unknown"
    77→
```

```
1→"""CLI entry point: parse args, load shared context, dispatch command handlers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import logging
     6→import sys
     7→
     8→from desloppify import file_discovery as file_discovery_mod
     9→from desloppify import utils as utils_mod
    10→from desloppify.app.cli_support.parser import create_parser as _create_parser
    11→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.lang import LangResolutionError, resolve_lang
    12→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import CommandRuntime
    13→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import state_path
    14→from desloppify.core._internal.text_utils import PROJECT_ROOT
    15→from desloppify.core.config import load_config
    16→from desloppify.core.runtime_state import runtime_scope
    17→from desloppify.languages import available_langs
    18→from desloppify.state import load_state
    19→from desloppify.utils import DEFAULT_PATH, colorize
    20→
    21→_DETECTOR_NAMES: list[str] | None = None
    22→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    23→
    24→
    25→def _get_detector_names() -> list[str]:
    26→    """Return cached detector names, computing on first access."""
    27→    global _DETECTOR_NAMES
    28→    if _DETECTOR_NAMES is None:
    29→        from desloppify.core.registry import detector_names
    30→
    31→        _DETECTOR_NAMES = detector_names()
    32→    return _DETECTOR_NAMES
    33→
    34→def create_parser():
    35→    """Return the top-level argparse parser."""
    36→    return _create_parser(langs=available_langs(), detector_names=_get_detector_names())
    37→
    38→
    39→def _apply_persisted_exclusions(args, config: dict):
    40→    """Merge CLI --exclude with persisted config.exclude and apply globally."""
    41→    cli_exclusions = getattr(args, "exclude", None) or []
    42→    persisted = config.get("exclude", [])
    43→    combined = list(cli_exclusions) + [e for e in persisted if e not in cli_exclusions]
    44→    if not combined:
    45→        return
    46→    file_discovery_mod.set_exclusions(combined)
    47→    if cli_exclusions:
    48→        print(
    49→            utils_mod.colorize(f"  Excluding: {', '.join(combined)}", "dim"),
    50→            file=sys.stderr,
    51→        )
    52→        return
    53→    print(
    54→        utils_mod.colorize(
    55→            f"  Excluding (from config): {', '.join(combined)}", "dim"
    56→        ),
    57→        file=sys.stderr,
    58→    )
    59→
    60→
    61→def _resolve_default_path(args) -> None:
    62→    """Fill args.path from detected language or default source path.
    63→
    64→    For the review command, the last scan path (stored in state) is used as the
    65→    default so that ``desloppify review --prepare`` works on the same scope as
    66→    the preceding scan even when the project files are not under ``src/``.
    67→    """
    68→    if getattr(args, "path", None) is not None:
    69→        return
    70→    if getattr(args, "command", None) == "review":
    71→        try:
    72→            state_file = state_path(args)
    73→            if state_file:
    74→                saved = load_state(state_file)
    75→                saved_path = saved.get("scan_path")
    76→                if saved_path:
    77→                    args.path = str((PROJECT_ROOT / saved_path).resolve())
    78→                    return
    79→        except (OSError, KeyError, ValueError, TypeError, AttributeError) as exc:
    80→            logger.debug("Failed to resolve default path from saved state: %s", exc)
    81→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
    82→    if lang:
    83→        args.path = str(PROJECT_ROOT / lang.default_src)
    84→    else:
    85→        args.path = str(DEFAULT_PATH)
    86→
    87→
    88→def _load_shared_runtime(args) -> None:
    89→    """Load config/state and attach shared objects to parsed args."""
    90→    config = load_config()
    91→
    92→    state_file = state_path(args)
    93→    state = load_state(state_file)
    94→    _apply_persisted_exclusions(args, config)
    95→
    96→    args.runtime = CommandRuntime(config=config, state=state, state_path=state_file)
    97→
    98→
    99→def _resolve_handler(command: str):
   100→    from desloppify.app.commands.registry import get_command_handlers
   101→
   102→    return get_command_handlers()[command]
   103→
   104→
   105→def _handle_help_command(args, parser) -> None:
   106→    """Handle explicit help command when present in parser config."""
   107→    topic = list(getattr(args, "topic", []) or [])
   108→    try:
   109→        parser.parse_args([*topic, "--help"])
   110→    except SystemExit:
   111→        return
   112→
   113→
   114→def main() -> None:
   115→    # Ensure Unicode output works on Windows terminals (cp1252 etc.)
   116→    for stream in (sys.stdout, sys.stderr):
   117→        if hasattr(stream, "reconfigure"):
   118→            try:
   119→                stream.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
   120→            except (AttributeError, OSError):
   121→                logger.debug(
   122→                    "Skipping stream reconfigure for %s (not supported)",
   123→                    getattr(stream, "name", "<stream>"),
   124→                )
   125→
   126→    parser = create_parser()
   127→    args = parser.parse_args()
   128→    if args.command == "help":
   129→        _handle_help_command(args, parser)
   130→        return
   131→
   132→    try:
   133→        with runtime_scope():
   134→            _resolve_default_path(args)
   135→            _load_shared_runtime(args)
   136→
   137→            handler = _resolve_handler(args.command)
   138→            handler(args)
   139→    except LangResolutionError as exc:
   140→        print(colorize(f"  {exc.message}", "red"), file=sys.stderr)
   141→        sys.exit(1)
   142→    except KeyboardInterrupt:
   143→        print("\nInterrupted.")
   144→        sys.exit(1)
   145→
   146→
   147→if __name__ == "__main__":
   148→    main()
   149→
```

```
1→"""Language registry: plugin registration and language resolution."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import inspect
     6→from collections.abc import Callable
     7→from pathlib import Path
     8→from typing import TypeVar
     9→
    10→from desloppify.languages._framework import (
    11→    discovery,
    12→    registry_state,
    13→    resolution,
    14→    runtime,
    15→)
    16→from desloppify.languages._framework.base.types import LangConfig
    17→from desloppify.languages._framework.contract_validation import validate_lang_contract
    18→from desloppify.languages._framework.policy import REQUIRED_DIRS, REQUIRED_FILES
    19→from desloppify.languages._framework.resolution import (
    20→    auto_detect_lang,
    21→    available_langs,
    22→    get_lang,
    23→    make_lang_config,
    24→)
    25→from desloppify.languages._framework.structure_validation import validate_lang_structure
    26→
    27→T = TypeVar("T")
    28→
    29→
    30→def register_lang(name: str) -> Callable[[T], T]:
    31→    """Decorator to register a language config class.
    32→
    33→    Validates structure, instantiates the class, validates the contract,
    34→    and stores the *instance* in the registry.
    35→    """
    36→
    37→    def decorator(cls: T) -> T:
    38→        module = inspect.getmodule(cls)
    39→        if module and hasattr(module, "__file__"):
    40→            validate_lang_structure(Path(module.__file__).parent, name)
    41→        if isinstance(cls, type) and issubclass(cls, LangConfig):
    42→            cfg = make_lang_config(name, cls)  # instantiate + validate
    43→            registry_state.register(name, cfg)  # store instance
    44→        else:
    45→            registry_state.register(name, cls)  # test doubles
    46→        return cls
    47→
    48→    return decorator
    49→
    50→
    51→def register_generic_lang(name: str, cfg: LangConfig) -> None:
    52→    """Register a pre-built language plugin instance (no package structure required)."""
    53→    validate_lang_contract(name, cfg)
    54→    registry_state.register(name, cfg)
    55→
    56→
    57→__all__ = [
    58→    "REQUIRED_FILES",
    59→    "REQUIRED_DIRS",
    60→    "register_lang",
    61→    "register_generic_lang",
    62→    "get_lang",
    63→    "available_langs",
    64→    "auto_detect_lang",
    65→    "make_lang_config",
    66→    "validate_lang_structure",
    67→    "validate_lang_contract",
```

```
1→"""Language-resolution helpers for command modules."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import logging
     6→from collections.abc import Callable
     7→from functools import lru_cache
     8→from pathlib import Path
     9→from typing import TYPE_CHECKING
    10→
    11→from desloppify import languages as lang_api
    12→from desloppify.core._internal.text_utils import PROJECT_ROOT
    13→
    14→if TYPE_CHECKING:
    15→    from desloppify.languages._framework.base.types import LangConfig
    16→
    17→
    18→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    19→
    20→
    21→class LangResolutionError(SystemExit):
    22→    """Raised when language resolution fails with a user-facing message.
    23→
    24→    Inherits from SystemExit so that callers which don't catch it explicitly
    25→    will still terminate with a non-zero exit code, matching the previous
    26→    sys.exit(1) behaviour.  The CLI top-level catches it to print a clean
    27→    error without a traceback.
    28→    """
    29→
    30→    def __init__(self, message: str) -> None:
    31→        self.message = message
    32→        super().__init__(1)
    33→
    34→
    35→EXTRA_ROOT_MARKERS = (
    36→    "package.json",
    37→    "pyproject.toml",
    38→    "setup.py",
    39→    "setup.cfg",
    40→    "go.mod",
    41→    "Cargo.toml",
    42→)
    43→
    44→
    45→def _cache_once(fn: Callable[[], tuple[str, ...]]) -> Callable[[], tuple[str, ...]]:
    46→    """Cache a zero-arg function once while preserving cache_clear for tests."""
    47→    cached = lru_cache(maxsize=1)(fn)
    48→    return cached
    49→
    50→
    51→@_cache_once
    52→def _lang_config_markers() -> tuple[str, ...]:
    53→    """Collect project-root marker files from language plugins + fallback markers."""
    54→    markers = set(EXTRA_ROOT_MARKERS)
    55→
    56→    for lang_name in lang_api.available_langs():
    57→        try:
    58→            cfg = lang_api.get_lang(lang_name)
    59→        except (ImportError, ValueError, TypeError, AttributeError) as exc:
    60→            logger.debug(
    61→                "Skipping language marker collection for %s: %s", lang_name, exc
    62→            )
    63→            continue
    64→        for marker in getattr(cfg, "detect_markers", []) or []:
    65→            if not isinstance(marker, str):
    66→                continue
    67→            cleaned = marker.strip()
    68→            if cleaned:
    69→                markers.add(cleaned)
    70→    return tuple(sorted(markers))
    71→
    72→
    73→def resolve_detection_root(
    74→    args,
    75→    *,
    76→    project_root: Path = PROJECT_ROOT,
    77→    marker_provider: Callable[[], tuple[str, ...]] | None = None,
    78→) -> Path:
    79→    """Best root to auto-detect language from."""
    80→    marker_provider = marker_provider or _lang_config_markers
    81→    markers = marker_provider()
    82→
    83→    raw_path = getattr(args, "path", None)
    84→    if not raw_path:
    85→        return project_root
    86→
    87→    candidate = Path(raw_path)
    88→    if not candidate.is_absolute():
    89→        candidate = project_root / candidate
    90→    candidate = candidate.resolve()
    91→    search_root = candidate if candidate.is_dir() else candidate.parent
    92→
    93→    for root in (search_root, *search_root.parents):
    94→        if any((root / marker).exists() for marker in markers):
    95→            return root
    96→    return search_root
    97→
    98→
    99→def auto_detect_lang_name(args) -> str | None:
   100→    """Auto-detect language using the most relevant root for this command."""
   101→    root = resolve_detection_root(args)
   102→    detected = lang_api.auto_detect_lang(root)
   103→    if detected is None and root != PROJECT_ROOT:
   104→        detected = lang_api.auto_detect_lang(PROJECT_ROOT)
   105→    return detected
   106→
   107→
   108→def resolve_lang(args) -> LangConfig | None:
   109→    """Resolve language config from args, with auto-detection fallback."""
   110→    lang_name = getattr(args, "lang", None)
   111→    if lang_name is None:
   112→        lang_name = auto_detect_lang_name(args)
   113→    if lang_name is None:
   114→        return None
   115→    try:
   116→        return lang_api.get_lang(lang_name)
   117→    except ValueError as exc:
   118→        langs = lang_api.available_langs()
   119→        langs_str = ", ".join(langs) if langs else "registered language plugins"
   120→        raise LangResolutionError(
   121→            f"{exc}\n  Hint: use --lang to select manually (available: {langs_str})"
   122→        ) from exc
   123→
   124→
   125→def resolve_lang_settings(config: dict, lang: LangConfig) -> dict[str, object]:
   126→    """Resolve persisted per-language settings from config.languages.<lang>."""
   127→    if not isinstance(config, dict):
   128→        return lang.normalize_settings({})
   129→    languages = config.get("languages", {})
   130→    if not isinstance(languages, dict):
   131→        return lang.normalize_settings({})
   132→    raw = languages.get(lang.name, {})
   133→    return lang.normalize_settings(raw if isinstance(raw, dict) else {})
   134→
```

```
    69→def _show_scan_visibility(noise, effective_include_slow: bool) -> None:

    73→    if noise.budget_warning:

    77→            f", {noise.global_noise_budget} global"
    78→            if noise.global_noise_budget > 0

    80→        )
    81→        print(

   100→        summary = str(entry.get("summary", "")).strip()

   102→        remediation = str(entry.get("remediation", "")).strip()
   103→        detector = str(entry.get("detector", "")).strip() or "detector"

   113→def cmd_scan(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
```

```
1→"""CLI entry point: parse args, load shared context, dispatch command handlers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import logging
     6→import sys
     7→
     8→from desloppify import file_discovery as file_discovery_mod
     9→from desloppify import utils as utils_mod
    10→from desloppify.app.cli_support.parser import create_parser as _create_parser
    11→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.lang import LangResolutionError, resolve_lang
    12→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import CommandRuntime
    13→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import state_path
    14→from desloppify.core._internal.text_utils import PROJECT_ROOT
    15→from desloppify.core.config import load_config
    16→from desloppify.core.runtime_state import runtime_scope
    17→from desloppify.languages import available_langs
    18→from desloppify.state import load_state
    19→from desloppify.utils import DEFAULT_PATH, colorize
    20→
    21→_DETECTOR_NAMES: list[str] | None = None
    22→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    23→
    24→
    25→def _get_detector_names() -> list[str]:
    26→    """Return cached detector names, computing on first access."""
    27→    global _DETECTOR_NAMES
    28→    if _DETECTOR_NAMES is None:
    29→        from desloppify.core.registry import detector_names
    30→
    31→        _DETECTOR_NAMES = detector_names()
    32→    return _DETECTOR_NAMES
    33→
    34→def create_parser():
    35→    """Return the top-level argparse parser."""
    36→    return _create_parser(langs=available_langs(), detector_names=_get_detector_names())
    37→
    38→
    39→def _apply_persisted_exclusions(args, config: dict):
    40→    """Merge CLI --exclude with persisted config.exclude and apply globally."""
    41→    cli_exclusions = getattr(args, "exclude", None) or []
    42→    persisted = config.get("exclude", [])
    43→    combined = list(cli_exclusions) + [e for e in persisted if e not in cli_exclusions]
    44→    if not combined:
    45→        return
    46→    file_discovery_mod.set_exclusions(combined)
    47→    if cli_exclusions:
    48→        print(
    49→            utils_mod.colorize(f"  Excluding: {', '.join(combined)}", "dim"),
    50→            file=sys.stderr,
    51→        )
    52→        return
    53→    print(
    54→        utils_mod.colorize(
    55→            f"  Excluding (from config): {', '.join(combined)}", "dim"
    56→        ),
    57→        file=sys.stderr,
    58→    )
    59→
    60→
    61→def _resolve_default_path(args) -> None:
    62→    """Fill args.path from detected language or default source path.
    63→
    64→    For the review command, the last scan path (stored in state) is used as the
    65→    default so that ``desloppify review --prepare`` works on the same scope as
    66→    the preceding scan even when the project files are not under ``src/``.
    67→    """
    68→    if getattr(args, "path", None) is not None:
    69→        return
    70→    if getattr(args, "command", None) == "review":
    71→        try:
    72→            state_file = state_path(args)
    73→            if state_file:
    74→                saved = load_state(state_file)
    75→                saved_path = saved.get("scan_path")
    76→                if saved_path:
    77→                    args.path = str((PROJECT_ROOT / saved_path).resolve())
    78→                    return
    79→        except (OSError, KeyError, ValueError, TypeError, AttributeError) as exc:
    80→            logger.debug("Failed to resolve default path from saved state: %s", exc)
    81→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
    82→    if lang:
    83→        args.path = str(PROJECT_ROOT / lang.default_src)
    84→    else:
    85→        args.path = str(DEFAULT_PATH)
    86→
    87→
    88→def _load_shared_runtime(args) -> None:
    89→    """Load config/state and attach shared objects to parsed args."""
    90→    config = load_config()
    91→
    92→    state_file = state_path(args)
    93→    state = load_state(state_file)
    94→    _apply_persisted_exclusions(args, config)
    95→
    96→    args.runtime = CommandRuntime(config=config, state=state, state_path=state_file)
    97→
    98→
    99→def _resolve_handler(command: str):
   100→    from desloppify.app.commands.registry import get_command_handlers
   101→
   102→    return get_command_handlers()[command]
   103→
   104→
   105→def _handle_help_command(args, parser) -> None:
   106→    """Handle explicit help command when present in parser config."""
   107→    topic = list(getattr(args, "topic", []) or [])
   108→    try:
   109→        parser.parse_args([*topic, "--help"])
   110→    except SystemExit:
   111→        return
   112→
   113→
   114→def main() -> None:
   115→    # Ensure Unicode output works on Windows terminals (cp1252 etc.)
   116→    for stream in (sys.stdout, sys.stderr):
   117→        if hasattr(stream, "reconfigure"):
   118→            try:
   119→                stream.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
   120→            except (AttributeError, OSError):
   121→                logger.debug(
   122→                    "Skipping stream reconfigure for %s (not supported)",
   123→                    getattr(stream, "name", "<stream>"),
   124→                )
   125→
   126→    parser = create_parser()
   127→    args = parser.parse_args()
   128→    if args.command == "help":
   129→        _handle_help_command(args, parser)
   130→        return
   131→
   132→    try:
   133→        with runtime_scope():
   134→            _resolve_default_path(args)
   135→            _load_shared_runtime(args)
   136→
   137→            handler = _resolve_handler(args.command)
   138→            handler(args)
   139→    except LangResolutionError as exc:
   140→        print(colorize(f"  {exc.message}", "red"), file=sys.stderr)
   141→        sys.exit(1)
   142→    except KeyboardInterrupt:
   143→        print("\nInterrupted.")
   144→        sys.exit(1)
   145→
   146→
   147→if __name__ == "__main__":
   148→    main()
   149→
```
