```
1→"""Post-scan narrative and integrity reporting."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     6→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.rendering import print_ranked_actions
     7→from desloppify.core._internal.coercions import coerce_confidence
     8→from desloppify.intelligence import integrity as subjective_integrity_mod
     9→from desloppify.intelligence import narrative as narrative_mod
    10→from desloppify.utils import colorize
    11→
    12→
    13→def _subjective_dimensions_below_threshold(
    14→    state: dict, *, threshold: float = 90.0
    15→) -> list[str]:
    16→    """Return subjective dimensions with score below threshold and evidence issues."""
    17→    low_dims: list[str] = []
    18→    dimension_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {})
    19→    if not isinstance(dimension_scores, dict):
    20→        return low_dims
    21→
    22→    for dim_name, payload in dimension_scores.items():
    23→        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
    24→            continue
    25→        score = payload.get("score")
    26→        detectors = payload.get("detectors", {})
    27→        subjective_payload = (
    28→            detectors.get("subjective_assessment")
    29→            if isinstance(detectors, dict)
    30→            else None
    31→        )
    32→        if not isinstance(subjective_payload, dict):
    33→            continue
    34→        issues = subjective_payload.get("issues", 0)
    35→        if not isinstance(score, int | float) or not isinstance(
    36→            issues, int | float
    37→        ):
    38→            continue
    39→        if score < threshold and issues > 0:
    40→            low_dims.append(str(dim_name))
    41→    return low_dims
    42→
    43→
    44→def _print_narrative_plan_fields(narrative: dict) -> None:
    45→    """Print additional narrative plan fields when present."""
    46→    why_now = narrative.get("why_now")
    47→    primary_action = narrative.get("primary_action")
    48→    verification_step = narrative.get("verification_step")
    49→    risk_flags = narrative.get("risk_flags")
    50→
    51→    has_plan = bool(why_now) or bool(primary_action) or bool(verification_step) or bool(
    52→        risk_flags
    53→    )
    54→    if not has_plan:
    55→        return
    56→
    57→    print(colorize("  Narrative Plan:", "cyan"))
    58→    if isinstance(why_now, str) and why_now.strip():
    59→        print(colorize(f"    Why now: {why_now.strip()}", "dim"))
    60→
    61→    if isinstance(primary_action, dict):
    62→        command = primary_action.get("command")
    63→        description = primary_action.get("description")
    64→        if isinstance(command, str) and command.strip():
    65→            line = f"    Next: `{command.strip()}`"
    66→            if isinstance(description, str) and description.strip():
    67→                line += f" — {description.strip()}"
    68→            print(colorize(line, "dim"))
    69→
    70→    if isinstance(verification_step, dict):
    71→        command = verification_step.get("command")
    72→        reason = verification_step.get("reason")
    73→        if isinstance(command, str) and command.strip():
    74→            line = f"    Verify: `{command.strip()}`"
    75→            if isinstance(reason, str) and reason.strip():
    76→                line += f" — {reason.strip()}"
    77→            print(colorize(line, "dim"))
    78→
    79→    if isinstance(risk_flags, list):
    80→        for risk in risk_flags:
    81→            if not isinstance(risk, dict):
    82→                continue
    83→            message = risk.get("message")
    84→            if not isinstance(message, str) or not message.strip():
    85→                continue
    86→            severity = str(risk.get("severity", "")).lower()
    87→            style = "yellow" if severity in {"high", "critical"} else "dim"
    88→            print(colorize(f"    Risk: {message.strip()}", style))
    89→    print()
    90→
    91→
    92→def _print_filtered_reminders(narrative: dict) -> None:
    93→    """Print narrative reminders except low-value report score reminders."""
    94→    reminders = narrative.get("reminders")
    95→    if not isinstance(reminders, list):
    96→        return
    97→    messages: list[str] = []
    98→    for reminder in reminders:
    99→        if not isinstance(reminder, dict):
   100→            continue
   101→        if reminder.get("type") == "report_scores":
   102→            continue
   103→        message = reminder.get("message")
   104→        if isinstance(message, str) and message.strip():
   105→            messages.append(message.strip())
   106→    if not messages:
   107→        return
   108→
   109→    print(colorize("  Reminders:", "dim"))
   110→    for message in messages:
   111→        print(colorize(f"    - {message}", "dim"))
   112→    print()
   113→
   114→
   115→def _show_subjective_score_nudge(state: dict) -> None:
   116→    """Nudge rerun when subjective dimensions are low with evidence issues."""
   117→    low_dims = _subjective_dimensions_below_threshold(state, threshold=90.0)
   118→    if not low_dims:
   119→        return
   120→
   121→    review_cache = state.get("review_cache", {})
   122→    reviewed_files = review_cache.get("files", {}) if isinstance(review_cache, dict) else {}
   123→    verb = "rerun" if isinstance(reviewed_files, dict) and reviewed_files else "run"
   124→
   125→    dim_preview = ", ".join(low_dims[:3])
   126→    if len(low_dims) > 3:
   127→        dim_preview += f", +{len(low_dims) - 3} more"
   128→
   129→    print(colorize(f"  Subjective scores below 90: {dim_preview}", "yellow"))
   130→    print(
   131→        colorize(
   132→            f"  You can {verb} the subjective scoring with "
   133→            "`desloppify review --run-batches --runner codex --parallel --scan-after-import`.",
   134→            "cyan",
   135→        )
   136→    )
   137→    print(
   138→        colorize(
   139→            "  Then review progress in `desloppify status` and investigate blockers via `desloppify issues`.",
   140→            "dim",
   141→        )
   142→    )
   143→    print()
   144→
   145→
   146→def _current_scan_coverage(state: dict, lang) -> dict:
   147→    scan_coverage = state.get("scan_coverage", {})
   148→    if not isinstance(scan_coverage, dict):
   149→        return {}
   150→    lang_name = getattr(lang, "name", None) if lang is not None else None
   151→    if isinstance(lang_name, str) and lang_name:
   152→        entry = scan_coverage.get(lang_name, {})
   153→        return entry if isinstance(entry, dict) else {}
   154→    return {}
   155→
   156→
   157→def _coverage_reduction_warnings(state: dict, lang) -> list[str]:
   158→    coverage = _current_scan_coverage(state, lang)
   159→    detectors = coverage.get("detectors", {})
   160→    if not isinstance(detectors, dict):
   161→        return []
   162→
   163→    warnings: list[str] = []
   164→    for detector, payload in detectors.items():
   165→        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
   166→            continue
   167→        status = str(payload.get("status", "full")).strip().lower()
   168→        confidence = coerce_confidence(payload.get("confidence"), default=1.0)
   169→        if status != "reduced" and confidence >= 1.0:
   170→            continue
   171→
   172→        summary = str(payload.get("summary", "")).strip()
   173→        impact = str(payload.get("impact", "")).strip()
   174→        remediation = str(payload.get("remediation", "")).strip()
   175→        detector_label = str(detector).strip() or "detector"
   176→
   177→        line = f"Coverage reduced ({detector_label}): {summary or 'reduced detector confidence.'}"
   178→        if impact:
   179→            line += f" Repercussion: {impact}"
   180→        if remediation:
   181→            line += f" Fix: {remediation}"
   182→        warnings.append(line)
   183→    return warnings
   184→
   185→
   186→def show_post_scan_analysis(
   187→    diff: dict,
   188→    state: dict,
   189→    lang,
   190→    *,
   191→    target_strict_score: float = 95.0,
   192→) -> tuple[list[str], dict]:
   193→    """Print warnings, narrative headline, and top action. Returns (warnings, narrative)."""
   194→    warnings = []
   195→    if diff["reopened"] > 5:
   196→        warnings.append(
   197→            f"{diff['reopened']} findings reopened — was a previous fix reverted? Check: git log --oneline -5"
   198→        )
   199→    if diff["new"] > 10 and diff["auto_resolved"] < 3:
   200→        warnings.append(
   201→            f"{diff['new']} new findings with few resolutions — likely cascading from recent fixes. Run fixers again."
   202→        )
   203→    chronic = diff.get("chronic_reopeners", [])
   204→    chronic_count = len(chronic) if isinstance(chronic, list) else chronic
   205→    if chronic_count > 0:
   206→        warnings.append(
   207→            f"⟳ {chronic_count} chronic reopener{'s' if chronic_count != 1 else ''} "
   208→            "(reopened 2+ times). These keep bouncing — fix properly or wontfix. "
   209→            "Run: `desloppify show --chronic` to see them."
   210→        )
   211→    warnings.extend(_coverage_reduction_warnings(state, lang))
   212→
   213→    if warnings:
   214→        for warning in warnings:
   215→            print(colorize(f"  {warning}", "yellow"))
   216→        print()
   217→
   218→    # Computed narrative: headline + top action as terminal suggestion
   219→    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
   220→    narrative = narrative_mod.compute_narrative(
   221→        state,
   222→        context=narrative_mod.NarrativeContext(
   223→            diff=diff,
   224→            lang=lang_name,
   225→            command="scan",
   226→        ),
   227→    )
   228→
   229→    # Show one actionable plan and optional strategy context.
   230→    print(
   231→        colorize(
   232→            "  AGENT PLAN (use `desloppify next --count 20` to inspect more items):",
   233→            "yellow",
   234→        )
   235→    )
   236→    strategy = narrative.get("strategy") or {}
   237→    hint = strategy.get("hint")
   238→    actions = narrative.get("actions", [])
   239→
   240→    if actions:
   241→        top = actions[0]
   242→        print(
   243→            colorize(
   244→                f"  Agent focus: `{top['command']}` — {top['description']}", "cyan"
   245→            )
   246→        )
   247→        if hint:
   248→            print(colorize(f"  Strategy: {hint}", "dim"))
   249→        print()
   250→    elif hint:
   251→        print(colorize(f"  Agent focus: {hint}", "cyan"))
   252→        print()
   253→
   254→    if print_ranked_actions(actions):
   255→        print()
   256→
   257→    if narrative.get("headline"):
   258→        print(colorize(f"  → {narrative['headline']}", "cyan"))
   259→        print()
   260→
   261→    _print_narrative_plan_fields(narrative)
   262→    _print_filtered_reminders(narrative)
   263→    _show_subjective_score_nudge(state)
   264→
   265→    # Review findings nudge
   266→    scoped_findings = state_mod.path_scoped_findings(
   267→        state.get("findings", {}), state.get("scan_path")
   268→    )
   269→    open_review = [
   270→        finding
   271→        for finding in scoped_findings.values()
   272→        if finding["status"] == "open" and finding.get("detector") == "review"
   273→    ]
   274→    open_subjective, _subjective_reasons, holistic_reasons = (
   275→        subjective_integrity_mod.subjective_review_open_breakdown(scoped_findings)
   276→    )
   277→    holistic_open = sum(holistic_reasons.values())
   278→    if open_review:
   279→        suffix = "s" if len(open_review) != 1 else ""
   280→        print(
   281→            colorize(
   282→                f"  Review: {len(open_review)} finding{suffix} pending — `desloppify issues`",
   283→                "cyan",
   284→            )
   285→        )
   286→        print()
   287→    if open_subjective > 0:
   288→        if holistic_open > 0:
   289→            print(
   290→                colorize(
   291→                    f"  Subjective integrity: {holistic_open} holistic stale/missing signal(s) — "
   292→                    "`desloppify review --run-batches --runner codex --parallel --scan-after-import`",
   293→                    "yellow",
   294→                )
   295→            )
   296→        else:
   297→            print(
   298→                colorize(
   299→                    f"  Subjective coverage: {open_subjective} file-level review signal(s) open — "
   300→                    "`desloppify show subjective_review --status open`",
   301→                    "cyan",
   302→                )
   303→            )
   304→        print()
   305→
   306→    # Auto-queue: nudge subjective review for high-complexity unreviewed files
   307→    review_cache = state.get("review_cache", {}).get("files", {})
   308→    scoped = scoped_findings
   309→    complex_unreviewed = set()
   310→    for finding in scoped.values():
   311→        if (
   312→            finding.get("detector") in ("structural", "smells")
   313→            and finding.get("status") == "wontfix"
   314→            and finding.get("file") not in review_cache
   315→        ):
   316→            complex_unreviewed.add(finding.get("file"))
   317→    if len(complex_unreviewed) >= 3:
   318→        print(
   319→            colorize(
   320→                f"  {len(complex_unreviewed)} complex files have never been reviewed — "
   321→                "`desloppify review --run-batches --runner codex --parallel --scan-after-import` "
   322→                "would provide actionable refactoring guidance",
   323→                "dim",
   324→            )
   325→        )
   326→        print()
   327→
   328→    return warnings, narrative
   329→
   330→
   331→def show_score_integrity(state: dict, diff: dict):
   332→    """Show Score Integrity section — surfaces wontfix debt and ignored findings."""
   333→    stats = state.get("stats", {})
   334→    wontfix = stats.get("wontfix", 0)
   335→    ignored = diff.get("ignored", 0)
   336→    ignore_patterns = diff.get("ignore_patterns", 0)
   337→    score_confidence = state.get("score_confidence", {})
   338→    confidence_reduced = (
   339→        isinstance(score_confidence, dict)
   340→        and str(score_confidence.get("status", "full")).lower() == "reduced"
   341→    )
   342→
   343→    if wontfix <= 5 and ignored <= 0 and ignore_patterns <= 0 and not confidence_reduced:
   344→        return
   345→
   346→    overall = state_mod.get_overall_score(state)
   347→    strict = state_mod.get_strict_score(state)
   348→    strict_gap = (
   349→        round(overall - strict, 1) if overall is not None and strict is not None else 0
   350→    )
   351→
   352→    # Wontfix % of actionable findings (open + wontfix + fixed + auto_resolved + false_positive)
   353→    actionable = (
   354→        stats.get("open", 0)
   355→        + wontfix
   356→        + stats.get("fixed", 0)
   357→        + stats.get("auto_resolved", 0)
   358→        + stats.get("false_positive", 0)
   359→    )
   360→    wontfix_pct = round(wontfix / actionable * 100) if actionable else 0
   361→
   362→    print(colorize("  " + "┄" * 2 + " Score Integrity " + "┄" * 37, "dim"))
   363→
   364→    if wontfix > 5:
   365→        if wontfix_pct > 50:
   366→            style = "red"
   367→            msg = (
   368→                f"  ❌ {wontfix} wontfix ({wontfix_pct}%) — over half of findings swept under rug. "
   369→                f"Strict gap: {strict_gap} pts"
   370→            )
   371→        elif wontfix_pct > 25:
   372→            style = "yellow"
   373→            msg = (
   374→                f"  ⚠ {wontfix} wontfix ({wontfix_pct}%) — review whether past "
   375→                "wontfix decisions still hold"
   376→            )
   377→        elif wontfix_pct > 10:
   378→            style = "yellow"
   379→            msg = (
   380→                f"  ⚠ {wontfix} wontfix findings ({wontfix_pct}%) — strict {strict_gap} "
   381→                "pts below lenient"
   382→            )
   383→        else:
   384→            style = "dim"
   385→            msg = f"  {wontfix} wontfix — strict gap: {strict_gap} pts"
   386→        print(colorize(msg, style))
   387→
   388→        # Show top 2 dimensions with biggest strict gap
   389→        dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {})
   390→        if dim_scores:
   391→            gaps = []
   392→            for name, data in dim_scores.items():
   393→                score = data.get("score", 100)
   394→                strict_value = data.get("strict", score)
   395→                gap = round(score - strict_value, 1)
   396→                if gap > 0:
   397→                    gaps.append((name, gap))
   398→            gaps.sort(key=lambda x: -x[1])
   399→            if gaps:
   400→                top = gaps[:2]
   401→                gap_str = ", ".join(f"{name} (−{gap} pts)" for name, gap in top)
   402→                print(colorize(f"    Biggest gaps: {gap_str}", "dim"))
   403→
   404→    if ignored > 0:
   405→        style = "red" if ignore_patterns > 5 or ignored > 100 else "yellow"
   406→        print(
   407→            colorize(
   408→                f"  ⚠ {ignore_patterns} ignore pattern{'s' if ignore_patterns != 1 else ''} "
   409→                f"suppressed {ignored} finding{'s' if ignored != 1 else ''} this scan",
   410→                style,
   411→            )
   412→        )
   413→        print(
   414→            colorize(
   415→                "    Suppressed findings still count against strict and verified scores",
   416→                "dim",
   417→            )
   418→        )
   419→    elif ignore_patterns > 0:
   420→        print(
   421→            colorize(
   422→                f"  {ignore_patterns} ignore pattern{'s' if ignore_patterns != 1 else ''} "
   423→                "active (0 findings suppressed this scan)",
   424→                "dim",
   425→            )
   426→        )
   427→
   428→    if confidence_reduced:
   429→        impacted_dimensions = score_confidence.get("dimensions", [])
   430→        detectors = score_confidence.get("detectors", [])
   431→        confidence = coerce_confidence(
   432→            score_confidence.get("confidence"),
   433→            default=1.0,
   434→        )
   435→        dim_text = ""
   436→        if isinstance(impacted_dimensions, list) and impacted_dimensions:
   437→            preview = ", ".join(str(item) for item in impacted_dimensions[:3])
   438→            if len(impacted_dimensions) > 3:
   439→                preview += f", +{len(impacted_dimensions) - 3} more"
   440→            dim_text = f" (dimensions: {preview})"
   441→        print(
   442→            colorize(
   443→                f"  ⚠ Score confidence reduced to {confidence * 100:.0f}%{dim_text}",
   444→                "yellow",
   445→            )
   446→        )
   447→        if isinstance(detectors, list):
   448→            for detector in detectors[:3]:
   449→                if not isinstance(detector, dict):
   450→                    continue
   451→                summary = str(detector.get("summary", "")).strip()
   452→                remediation = str(detector.get("remediation", "")).strip()
   453→                if summary:
   454→                    print(colorize(f"    - {summary}", "dim"))
   455→                if remediation:
   456→                    print(colorize(f"      Fix: {remediation}", "dim"))
   457→
   458→    print(colorize("  " + "┄" * 55, "dim"))
   459→    print()
   460→
   461→
   462→__all__ = ["show_post_scan_analysis", "show_score_integrity"]
   463→
```

```
1→"""Config instantiation and public language resolution helpers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from pathlib import Path
     6→
     7→from desloppify.languages._framework import registry_state
     8→from desloppify.languages._framework.base.types import LangConfig
     9→from desloppify.languages._framework.contract_validation import validate_lang_contract
    10→from desloppify.languages._framework.discovery import load_all
    11→
    12→
    13→def make_lang_config(name: str, cfg_cls: type) -> LangConfig:
    14→    """Instantiate and validate a language config."""
    15→    try:
    16→        cfg = cfg_cls()
    17→    except (TypeError, ValueError, AttributeError, RuntimeError, OSError) as ex:
    18→        raise ValueError(
    19→            f"Failed to instantiate language config '{name}': {ex}"
    20→        ) from ex
    21→    validate_lang_contract(name, cfg)
    22→    return cfg
    23→
    24→
    25→def get_lang(name: str) -> LangConfig:
    26→    """Get a language config by name.
    27→
    28→    All plugins (full and generic) store LangConfig instances in the registry.
    29→    Test doubles that store plain classes are instantiated on demand as a fallback.
    30→    """
    31→    if not registry_state.is_registered(name):
    32→        load_all()
    33→    if not registry_state.is_registered(name):
    34→        available = ", ".join(sorted(registry_state.all_keys()))
    35→        raise ValueError(f"Unknown language: {name!r}. Available: {available}")
    36→    obj = registry_state.get(name)
    37→    if isinstance(obj, LangConfig):
    38→        return obj
    39→    return make_lang_config(name, obj)  # fallback for test doubles
    40→
    41→
    42→def auto_detect_lang(project_root: Path) -> str | None:
    43→    """Auto-detect language from project files.
    44→
    45→    When multiple config files are present (e.g. package.json + pyproject.toml),
    46→    counts actual source files to pick the dominant language instead of relying
    47→    on first-match ordering.
    48→    """
    49→    load_all()
    50→    candidates: list[str] = []
    51→    configs: dict[str, LangConfig] = {}
    52→
    53→    for lang_name, obj in registry_state.all_items():
    54→        cfg = obj if isinstance(obj, LangConfig) else make_lang_config(lang_name, obj)
    55→        configs[lang_name] = cfg
    56→        markers = getattr(cfg, "detect_markers", []) or []
    57→        if markers and any((project_root / marker).exists() for marker in markers):
    58→            candidates.append(lang_name)
    59→
    60→    if not candidates:
    61→        # Marker-less fallback: pick language with most source files.
    62→        best, best_count = None, 0
    63→        for lang_name, cfg in configs.items():
    64→            count = len(cfg.file_finder(project_root))
    65→            if count > best_count:
    66→                best, best_count = lang_name, count
    67→        return best if best_count > 0 else None
    68→
    69→    if len(candidates) == 1:
    70→        return candidates[0]
    71→
    72→    # Multiple candidates: choose language with most source files.
    73→    best, best_count = None, -1
    74→    for lang_name in candidates:
    75→        count = len(configs[lang_name].file_finder(project_root))
    76→        if count > best_count:
    77→            best, best_count = lang_name, count
    78→    return best
    79→
    80→
    81→def available_langs() -> list[str]:
    82→    """Return list of registered language names."""
    83→    load_all()
    84→    return sorted(registry_state.all_keys())
    85→
```

```
1→"""Scan lifecycle orchestration service.
     2→
     3→Keeps scan command flow explicit while delegating stage implementations to
     4→scan_workflow helpers.
     5→"""
     6→
     7→from __future__ import annotations
     8→
     9→from collections.abc import Callable
    10→from dataclasses import dataclass
    11→from typing import Any
    12→
    13→from desloppify.app.commands.scan.scan_workflow import (
    14→    ScanMergeResult,
    15→    ScanNoiseSnapshot,
    16→    ScanRuntime,
    17→    merge_scan_results,
    18→    persist_reminder_history,
    19→    resolve_noise_snapshot,
    20→    run_scan_generation,
    21→)
    22→
    23→
    24→@dataclass
    25→class ScanOrchestrator:
    26→    """Compose scan lifecycle stages around a resolved ScanRuntime."""
    27→
    28→    runtime: ScanRuntime
    29→    run_scan_generation_fn: Callable[
    30→        [ScanRuntime],
    31→        tuple[list[dict[str, Any]], dict[str, object], dict[str, object] | None],
    32→    ] = run_scan_generation
    33→    merge_scan_results_fn: Callable[
    34→        [ScanRuntime, list[dict[str, Any]], dict[str, object], dict[str, object] | None],
    35→        ScanMergeResult,
    36→    ] = merge_scan_results
    37→    resolve_noise_snapshot_fn: Callable[
    38→        [dict[str, Any], dict[str, object]],
    39→        ScanNoiseSnapshot,
    40→    ] = resolve_noise_snapshot
    41→    persist_reminder_history_fn: Callable[
    42→        [ScanRuntime, dict[str, object]],
    43→        None,
    44→    ] = persist_reminder_history
    45→
    46→    def generate(
    47→        self,
    48→    ) -> tuple[list[dict[str, object]], dict[str, object], dict[str, object] | None]:
    49→        """Run detector generation and lifecycle augmenters."""
    50→        return self.run_scan_generation_fn(self.runtime)
    51→
    52→    def merge(
    53→        self,
    54→        findings: list[dict[str, object]],
    55→        potentials: dict[str, object],
    56→        codebase_metrics: dict[str, object] | None,
    57→    ) -> ScanMergeResult:
    58→        """Merge generated findings and persist scan state updates."""
    59→        return self.merge_scan_results_fn(
    60→            self.runtime,
    61→            findings,
    62→            potentials,
    63→            codebase_metrics,
    64→        )
    65→
    66→    def noise_snapshot(self) -> ScanNoiseSnapshot:
    67→        """Resolve effective noise budgets for display/query payloads."""
    68→        return self.resolve_noise_snapshot_fn(self.runtime.state, self.runtime.config)
    69→
    70→    def persist_reminders(self, narrative: dict[str, object]) -> None:
    71→        """Persist reminder history emitted by post-scan narrative analysis."""
    72→        self.persist_reminder_history_fn(self.runtime, narrative)
    73→
    74→
    75→__all__ = ["ScanOrchestrator"]
    76→
```

```
1→"""Score and diff summary output for scan command."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import logging
     6→
     7→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.scan.scan_helpers import _format_delta
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.status_parts.strict_target import (
    10→    format_strict_target_progress,
    11→)
    12→from desloppify.utils import colorize
    13→
    14→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    15→
    16→
    17→def _consecutive_subjective_integrity_status(state: dict, status: str) -> int:
    18→    """Return consecutive trailing scans with the given subjective-integrity status."""
    19→    history = state.get("scan_history", [])
    20→    if not isinstance(history, list):
    21→        return 0
    22→
    23→    streak = 0
    24→    for entry in reversed(history):
    25→        if not isinstance(entry, dict):
    26→            break
    27→        integrity = entry.get("subjective_integrity")
    28→        if not isinstance(integrity, dict):
    29→            break
    30→        if integrity.get("status") != status:
    31→            break
    32→        streak += 1
    33→    return streak
    34→
    35→
    36→def show_diff_summary(diff: dict):
    37→    """Print the +new / -resolved / reopened one-liner."""
    38→    diff_parts = []
    39→    if diff["new"]:
    40→        diff_parts.append(colorize(f"+{diff['new']} new", "yellow"))
    41→    if diff["auto_resolved"]:
    42→        diff_parts.append(colorize(f"-{diff['auto_resolved']} resolved", "green"))
    43→    if diff["reopened"]:
    44→        diff_parts.append(colorize(f"↻{diff['reopened']} reopened", "red"))
    45→    if diff_parts:
    46→        print(f"  {' · '.join(diff_parts)}")
    47→    else:
    48→        print(colorize("  No changes since last scan", "dim"))
    49→    if diff.get("suspect_detectors"):
    50→        print(
    51→            colorize(
    52→                "  ⚠ Skipped auto-resolve for: "
    53→                f"{', '.join(diff['suspect_detectors'])} (returned 0 — likely transient)",
    54→                "yellow",
    55→            )
    56→        )
    57→
    58→
    59→def show_score_delta(
    60→    state: dict,
    61→    prev_overall: float | None,
    62→    prev_objective: float | None,
    63→    prev_strict: float | None,
    64→    prev_verified: float | None = None,
    65→    non_comparable_reason: str | None = None,
    66→):
    67→    """Print the canonical score trio with deltas."""
    68→    stats = state["stats"]
    69→    new = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
    70→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
    71→    scoped_open = int(stats.get("open", 0) or 0)
    72→    out_of_scope_open = 0
    73→    global_open = scoped_open
    74→    if isinstance(findings, dict) and findings:
    75→        scope_counts = state_mod.open_scope_breakdown(findings, state.get("scan_path"))
    76→        scoped_open = int(scope_counts.get("in_scope", scoped_open) or 0)
    77→        out_of_scope_open = int(scope_counts.get("out_of_scope", 0) or 0)
    78→        global_open = int(scope_counts.get("global", scoped_open) or 0)
    79→
    80→    wontfix = stats.get("wontfix", 0)
    81→    wontfix_str = f" · {wontfix} wontfix" if wontfix else ""
    82→
    83→    if (
    84→        new.overall is None
    85→        or new.objective is None
    86→        or new.strict is None
    87→        or new.verified is None
    88→    ):
    89→        print(
    90→            colorize(
    91→                "  Scores unavailable — run a full scan with language detectors enabled.",
    92→                "yellow",
    93→            )
    94→        )
    95→        return
    96→
    97→    overall_delta_str, overall_color = _format_delta(new.overall, prev_overall)
    98→    objective_delta_str, objective_color = _format_delta(new.objective, prev_objective)
    99→    strict_delta_str, strict_color = _format_delta(new.strict, prev_strict)
   100→    verified_delta_str, verified_color = _format_delta(new.verified, prev_verified)
   101→    print(
   102→        "  Scores: "
   103→        + colorize(f"overall {new.overall:.1f}/100{overall_delta_str}", overall_color)
   104→        + colorize(
   105→            f"  objective {new.objective:.1f}/100{objective_delta_str}",
   106→            objective_color,
   107→        )
   108→        + colorize(f"  strict {new.strict:.1f}/100{strict_delta_str}", strict_color)
   109→        + colorize(
   110→            f"  verified {new.verified:.1f}/100{verified_delta_str}",
   111→            verified_color,
   112→        )
   113→        + colorize(
   114→            "  |  "
   115→            f"open (in-scope): {scoped_open} · "
   116→            f"open (out-of-scope carried): {out_of_scope_open} · "
   117→            f"open (global): {global_open}"
   118→            f"{wontfix_str} / {stats['total']} in-scope total",
   119→            "dim",
   120→        )
   121→    )
   122→    if isinstance(non_comparable_reason, str) and non_comparable_reason.strip():
   123→        print(colorize(f"  Δ non-comparable: {non_comparable_reason.strip()}", "yellow"))
   124→    # Surface wontfix debt gap prominently when significant
   125→    gap = (new.overall or 0) - (new.strict or 0)
   126→    if gap >= 5 and wontfix >= 10:
   127→        print(
   128→            colorize(
   129→                f"  ⚠ {gap:.1f}-point gap between overall and strict — "
   130→                f"{wontfix} wontfix items represent hidden debt",
   131→                "yellow",
   132→            )
   133→        )
   134→
   135→    integrity = state.get("subjective_integrity", {})
   136→    if isinstance(integrity, dict):
   137→        status = integrity.get("status")
   138→        matched_count = int(integrity.get("matched_count", 0) or 0)
   139→        target = integrity.get("target_score")
   140→        if status == "penalized":
   141→            print(
   142→                colorize(
   143→                    "  ⚠ Subjective integrity: "
   144→                    f"{matched_count} target-matched dimensions were reset to 0.0 "
   145→                    f"({'target ' + str(target) if target is not None else 'target threshold'}).",
   146→                    "red",
   147→                )
   148→            )
   149→            streak = _consecutive_subjective_integrity_status(state, "penalized")
   150→            if streak >= 2:
   151→                print(
   152→                    colorize(
   153→                        "    Repeated penalty across scans. Use a blind, isolated reviewer "
   154→                        "on `.desloppify/review_packet_blind.json` and re-import before trusting subjective scores.",
   155→                        "yellow",
   156→                    )
   157→                )
   158→        elif status == "warn":
   159→            print(
   160→                colorize(
   161→                    "  ⚠ Subjective integrity: "
   162→                    f"{matched_count} dimension matched the target "
   163→                    f"({'target ' + str(target) if target is not None else 'target threshold'}). Re-review recommended.",
   164→                    "yellow",
   165→                )
   166→            )
   167→            streak = _consecutive_subjective_integrity_status(state, "warn")
   168→            if streak >= 2:
   169→                print(
   170→                    colorize(
   171→                        "    This warning has repeated. Prefer "
   172→                        "`desloppify review --run-batches --runner codex --parallel --scan-after-import` "
   173→                        "or run a blind reviewer pass before import.",
   174→                        "yellow",
   175→                )
   176→            )
   177→
   178→
   179→def show_concern_count(state: dict, lang_name: str | None = None) -> None:
   180→    """Print concern count if any exist."""
   181→    try:
   182→        from desloppify.engine.concerns import generate_concerns
   183→
   184→        concerns = generate_concerns(state, lang_name=lang_name)
   185→        if concerns:
   186→            print(
   187→                colorize(
   188→                    f"  {len(concerns)} potential design concern{'s' if len(concerns) != 1 else ''}"
   189→                    " (run `show concerns` to view)",
   190→                    "cyan",
   191→                )
   192→            )
   193→    except (ImportError, AttributeError, TypeError, ValueError) as exc:
   194→        logger.debug("Concern generation failed (best-effort): %s", exc)
   195→
   196→
   197→def show_strict_target_progress(strict_target: dict | None) -> tuple[float | None, float | None]:
   198→    """Print strict target progress lines and return (target, gap)."""
   199→    lines, target, gap = format_strict_target_progress(strict_target)
   200→    for message, style in lines:
   201→        print(colorize(message, style))
   202→    return target, gap
   203→
   204→
   205→__all__ = ["show_concern_count", "show_diff_summary", "show_score_delta", "show_strict_target_progress"]
   206→
```

```
    39→def cmd_status(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:

    64→        )

    95→        print(colorize(line, style))

    97→    print_open_scope_breakdown(state)
    98→    print_scan_completeness(state)
    99→
   100→    if dim_scores:
   101→        show_dimension_table(state, dim_scores)
   102→        reporting_dimensions_mod.show_score_model_breakdown(
   103→            state,

   131→        print(colorize(f"  Review staleness: {label}", "dim"))

   133→
   134→    write_status_query(

   151→def _status_json_payload(

   154→    dim_scores: dict,
   155→    scorecard_dims: list[dict],

   158→) -> dict:
   159→    scores = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
   160→    findings = state.get("findings", {})

   162→        state_mod.open_scope_breakdown(findings, state.get("scan_path"))
   163→        if isinstance(findings, dict)
   164→        else None
   165→    )
   166→    return {
   167→        "overall_score": scores.overall,
   168→        "objective_score": scores.objective,
   169→        "strict_score": scores.strict,
   170→        "verified_strict_score": scores.verified,
   171→        "dimension_scores": dim_scores,
   172→        "score_breakdown": compute_health_breakdown(dim_scores) if dim_scores else None,
   173→        "scorecard_dimensions": scorecard_dims,
   174→        "subjective_measures": subjective_measures,
   175→        "potentials": state.get("potentials"),
   176→        "codebase_metrics": state.get("codebase_metrics"),
   177→        "stats": stats,
   178→        "open_scope": open_scope,
   179→        "suppression": suppression,
   180→        "scan_count": state.get("scan_count", 0),
   181→        "last_scan": state.get("last_scan"),
   182→    }
   183→
   184→__all__ = [

   187→    "show_focus_suggestion",
```

```
    25→    profile: str,
    26→    diff: dict[str, object],
    27→    warnings: list[str],
    28→    narrative: dict[str, object],
    29→    merge: ScanMergeResult,
    30→    noise: ScanNoiseSnapshot,
    31→) -> ScanQueryPayload:
    32→    """Build the canonical query payload persisted after a scan."""
    33→    scores = score_snapshot(state)
    34→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
    35→    open_scope = (
    36→        open_scope_breakdown(findings, state.get("scan_path"))
    37→        if isinstance(findings, dict)
    38→        else None
    39→    )
    40→    return {
    41→        "command": "scan",
    42→        "overall_score": scores.overall,
    43→        "objective_score": scores.objective,
    44→        "strict_score": scores.strict,
    45→        "verified_strict_score": scores.verified,
    46→        "prev_overall_score": merge.prev_overall,
    47→        "prev_objective_score": merge.prev_objective,
    48→        "prev_strict_score": merge.prev_strict,
    49→        "prev_verified_strict_score": merge.prev_verified,
    50→        "profile": profile,
    51→        "noise_budget": noise.noise_budget,
    52→        "noise_global_budget": noise.global_noise_budget,
    53→        "hidden_by_detector": noise.hidden_by_detector,
    54→        "hidden_total": noise.hidden_total,
    55→        "diff": diff,
    56→        "stats": state["stats"],
    57→        "open_scope": open_scope,
    58→        "warnings": warnings,
    59→        "dimension_scores": state.get("dimension_scores"),
    60→        "score_breakdown": compute_health_breakdown(state.get("dimension_scores", {})),
    61→        "subjective_integrity": state.get("subjective_integrity"),
    62→        "score_confidence": state.get("score_confidence"),
    63→        "potentials": state.get("potentials"),
    64→        "scan_coverage": state.get("scan_coverage"),
    65→        "zone_distribution": state.get("zone_distribution"),
    66→        "narrative": narrative,
    67→        "config": config_for_query(config),
    68→    }
    69→
    70→
    71→def _load_scorecard_helpers():
    72→    """Load scorecard helper callables lazily via importlib.
    73→
    74→    Deferred: scorecard depends on PIL (optional dependency).
    75→    """
    76→    try:
    77→        scorecard_module = importlib.import_module("desloppify.app.output.scorecard")
    78→    except ImportError:
    79→        return None, None
    80→    generate = getattr(scorecard_module, "generate_scorecard", None)
    81→    badge_config = getattr(scorecard_module, "get_badge_config", None)
    82→    return generate, badge_config
    83→
    84→
    85→def emit_scorecard_badge(
    86→    args, config: dict[str, object], state: dict[str, object]
    87→) -> tuple[Path | None, OutputResult]:
    88→    """Generate a scorecard image badge and print usage hints."""
    89→    generate_scorecard, get_badge_config = _load_scorecard_helpers()
    90→    if not callable(generate_scorecard) or not callable(get_badge_config):
    91→        explicit_badge_request = bool(
    92→            getattr(args, "badge_path", None)
    93→            or config.get("badge_path")
    94→            or os.environ.get("DESLOPPIFY_BADGE_PATH")
    95→        )
    96→        if explicit_badge_request:
    97→            print(
    98→                colorize(
    99→                    "  Scorecard support not installed. Install with: pip install \"desloppify[scorecard]\"",
   100→                    "yellow",
   101→                )
   102→            )
   103→            return None, OutputResult(
   104→                ok=False,
   105→                status="error",
   106→                message="scorecard support not installed",
   107→                error_kind="scorecard_dependency_missing",
   108→            )
   109→        return None, OutputResult(ok=True, status="skipped", message="badge generation disabled")
   110→
   111→    try:
   112→        badge_path, disabled = get_badge_config(args, config)
   113→    except OSError as exc:
   114→        print(
   115→            colorize(f"  ⚠ Could not resolve scorecard badge path: {exc}", "yellow")
   116→        )
   117→        return None, OutputResult(
   118→            ok=False,
   119→            status="error",
   120→            message=str(exc),
   121→            error_kind="badge_path_resolution_error",
   122→        )
   123→    if disabled or not badge_path:
   124→        return None, OutputResult(ok=True, status="skipped", message="badge generation disabled")
   125→
   126→    try:
   127→        generate_scorecard(state, badge_path)
   128→    except (OSError, ImportError) as exc:
   129→        print(colorize(f"  ⚠ Could not generate scorecard badge: {exc}", "yellow"))
   130→        return None, OutputResult(
   131→            ok=False,
   132→            status="error",
   133→            message=str(exc),
   134→            error_kind="badge_generation_error",
   135→        )
   136→
   137→    try:
   138→        rel_path = str(badge_path.relative_to(PROJECT_ROOT))
   139→    except ValueError:
   140→        rel_path = str(badge_path)
   141→
   142→    readme_has_badge = False
   143→    for readme_name in ("README.md", "readme.md", "README.MD"):
   144→        readme_path = PROJECT_ROOT / readme_name
   145→        if readme_path.exists():
   146→            try:
   147→                readme_has_badge = rel_path in readme_path.read_text(
   148→                    encoding="utf-8", errors="replace"
   149→                )
   150→            except OSError:
   151→                readme_has_badge = False
   152→            break
   153→
   154→    if readme_has_badge:
   155→        print(
   156→            colorize(
   157→                f"  Scorecard → {rel_path}  (disable: --no-badge | move: --badge-path <path>)",
   158→                "dim",
   159→            )
   160→        )
   161→        return badge_path, OutputResult(
   162→            ok=True, status="written", message=f"scorecard badge written to {rel_path}"
   163→        )
   164→
   165→    print(colorize(f"  Scorecard → {rel_path}", "dim"))
   166→    print(
   167→        colorize(
   168→            "  💡 Ask the user if they'd like to add it to their README with:",
   169→            "dim",
   170→        )
   171→    )
   172→    print(colorize(f'     <img src="{rel_path}" width="100%">', "dim"))
   173→    print(colorize("     (disable: --no-badge | move: --badge-path <path>)", "dim"))
   174→    return badge_path, OutputResult(
   175→        ok=True, status="written", message=f"scorecard badge written to {rel_path}"
   176→    )
   177→
   178→
   179→__all__ = ["build_scan_query_payload", "emit_scorecard_badge"]
   180→
```

```
    36→
    37→def _print_score_lines(

    58→def _split_dimension_scores(

   113→            )

   116→
   117→def _print_stats_summary(
   118→    state: dict,
   119→    diff: dict | None,
   120→    *,

   123→) -> None:

   125→    if not stats:
   126→        return
   127→
   128→    wontfix = stats.get("wontfix", 0)
   129→    ignored = diff.get("ignored", 0) if diff else 0
   130→    ignore_pats = diff.get("ignore_patterns", 0) if diff else 0
   131→    strict_gap = (
   132→        round((overall_score or 0) - (strict_score or 0), 1)
   133→        if overall_score and strict_score
   134→        else 0
   135→    )
   136→    print(
   137→        f"Total findings: {stats.get('total', 0)} | "
   138→        f"Open: {stats.get('open', 0)} | "
   139→        f"Fixed: {stats.get('fixed', 0)} | "
   140→        f"Wontfix: {wontfix}"
   141→    )
   142→    if wontfix or ignored or ignore_pats:
   143→        print(
   144→            f"Ignored: {ignored} (by {ignore_pats} patterns) | Strict gap: {strict_gap} pts"
   145→        )
   146→        print("Focus on strict score — wontfix and ignore inflate the lenient score.")
   147→    print()
   148→
   149→

   153→def _print_workflow_guide() -> None:

   155→    print(_WORKFLOW_GUIDE)
   156→    print()
   157→
   158→
   159→def _print_narrative_status(narrative: dict | None) -> None:
   160→    if not narrative:
   161→        return
   162→
   163→    headline = narrative.get("headline", "")
   164→    strategy = narrative.get("strategy") or {}
   165→    actions = narrative.get("actions", [])
   166→    if headline:
   167→        print(f"Current status: {headline}")
   168→    hint = strategy.get("hint", "")
   169→    if hint:
   170→        print(f"Strategy: {hint}")
   171→    if actions:
   172→        top = actions[0]
   173→        print(f"Top action: `{top['command']}` — {top['description']}")
   174→    print()

   177→def _detect_agent_interface() -> str | None:
   178→    """Detect the current agent interface from environment variables."""
   179→    if os.environ.get("CLAUDE_CODE"):
   180→        return "claude"
   181→    if os.environ.get("GEMINI_CLI"):
   182→        return "gemini"
   183→    if os.environ.get("CODEX_SANDBOX_NETWORK_DISABLED") or os.environ.get("CODEX_SANDBOX"):

   190→def _try_auto_update_skill() -> None:
   191→    """Attempt to auto-install or auto-update the skill document.
   192→
   193→    Best-effort: swallows all exceptions so a network failure or permission
   194→    error never breaks the scan.
   195→    """
   196→    install = utils_mod.find_installed_skill()
   197→
   198→    if install and not install.stale:
   199→        return  # Up to date.
   200→
   201→    try:
   202→        from desloppify.app.commands.update_skill import (
   203→            resolve_interface,
   204→            update_installed_skill,
   205→        )
   206→
   207→        if install:
   208→            interface = resolve_interface(install=install)
   209→        else:
   210→            interface = _detect_agent_interface()
   211→
   212→        if interface:
   213→            update_installed_skill(interface)
   214→    except (ImportError, OSError, RuntimeError, ValueError) as exc:
   215→        logger.debug("Skill auto-update skipped: %s", exc, exc_info=True)
   216→
   217→
   218→def _print_badge_hint(badge_path: Path | None) -> None:
   219→    if not (badge_path and badge_path.exists()):
   220→        return
   221→
   222→    rel_path = badge_path.name if badge_path.parent == PROJECT_ROOT else str(badge_path)
   223→    print(f"A scorecard image was saved to `{rel_path}`.")
   224→    print("Let the user know they can view it, and suggest adding it")
   225→    print(f'to their README: `<img src="{rel_path}" width="100%">`')
   226→
   227→
   228→def _print_llm_summary(
   229→    state: dict,
   230→    badge_path: Path | None,
   231→    narrative: dict | None = None,
   232→    diff: dict | None = None,
   233→):
   234→    """Print a structured summary for LLM consumption.
   235→
   236→    The LLM reads terminal output after running scans. This gives it
   237→    clear instructions on how to present the results to the end user.
   238→    Only shown when running inside an agent (CLAUDE_CODE or DESLOPPIFY_AGENT env).
   239→    """
   240→    if not _is_agent_environment():
   241→        return
   242→
   243→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {})
   244→    scores = _load_scores(state)
   245→
   246→    if (
   247→        scores.overall is None
   248→        and scores.objective is None
   249→        and scores.strict is None
   250→        and scores.verified is None
   251→        and not dim_scores
   252→    ):
   253→        return
   254→
   255→    print("─" * 60)
   256→    print("INSTRUCTIONS FOR LLM")
   257→    print("IMPORTANT: ALWAYS present ALL scores to the user after a scan.")
   258→    print("Show overall health (lenient + strict), ALL dimension scores,")
   259→    print("AND all subjective dimension scores in a markdown table.")
   260→    print("The goal is to maximize strict scores. Never skip the scores.\n")
   261→
   262→    _print_score_lines(

   275→    _print_workflow_guide()
   276→    _print_narrative_status(narrative)
   277→    _print_badge_hint(badge_path)
   278→    print("─" * 60)

   280→
   281→def auto_update_skill() -> None:
   282→    """Auto-install or update the skill document if we detect an agent.
```

```
   132→def _reset_subjective_assessments_for_scan_reset(
   133→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   134→    *,
   135→    lang_name: str | None = None,
   136→) -> int:
   137→    """Reset known subjective dimensions to 0 so the next scan starts fresh."""
   138→    assessments = state.setdefault("subjective_assessments", {})
   139→    if not isinstance(assessments, dict):
   140→        assessments = {}
   141→        state["subjective_assessments"] = assessments
   142→
   143→    reset_keys = {
   144→        key.strip()
   145→        for key in assessments
   146→        if isinstance(key, str) and key.strip()
   147→    }
   148→    reset_keys.update(_subjective_reset_dimensions(lang_name=lang_name))
   149→
   150→    now = state_mod.utc_now()
   151→    for key in sorted(reset_keys):
   152→        payload = assessments.get(key)
   153→        if isinstance(payload, dict):
   154→            payload["score"] = 0.0
   155→            payload["source"] = "scan_reset_subjective"
   156→            payload["assessed_at"] = now
   157→            payload["reset_by"] = "scan_reset_subjective"
   158→            payload["placeholder"] = True
   159→            payload.pop("integrity_penalty", None)
   160→            payload.pop("components", None)
   161→            payload.pop("component_scores", None)
   162→            continue
   163→        assessments[key] = {
   164→            "score": 0.0,
   165→            "source": "scan_reset_subjective",
   166→            "assessed_at": now,
   167→            "reset_by": "scan_reset_subjective",
   168→            "placeholder": True,
   169→        }
   170→    return len(reset_keys)
   171→
   172→
   173→def _expire_provisional_manual_override_assessments(
   174→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   175→) -> int:
   176→    """Expire provisional manual-override assessments at scan start."""
   177→    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments")
   178→    if not isinstance(assessments, dict):
   179→        return 0
   180→
   181→    now = state_mod.utc_now()
   182→    expired = 0
   183→    for payload in assessments.values():
   184→        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
   185→            continue
   186→        if payload.get("provisional_override") is not True:
   187→            continue
   188→        payload["score"] = 0.0
   189→        payload["source"] = "manual_override_expired"
   190→        payload["assessed_at"] = now
   191→        payload["reset_by"] = "manual_override_expired"
   192→        payload["placeholder"] = True
   193→        payload.pop("provisional_override", None)
   194→        payload.pop("provisional_until_scan", None)
   195→        payload.pop("integrity_penalty", None)
   196→        payload.pop("components", None)
   197→        payload.pop("component_scores", None)
   198→        expired += 1
   199→    return expired
   200→

   202→def prepare_scan_runtime(args) -> ScanRuntime:
   203→    """Resolve state/config/language and apply scan-time runtime settings."""
   204→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   205→    state_file = runtime.state_path
   206→    state = runtime.state if isinstance(runtime.state, dict) else {}
   207→    path = Path(args.path)
   208→    config = runtime.config if isinstance(runtime.config, dict) else {}
   209→    lang_config = resolve_lang(args)
   210→    reset_subjective_count = 0
   211→    expired_manual_override_count = _expire_provisional_manual_override_assessments(
   212→        state
   213→    )
   214→    if getattr(args, "reset_subjective", False):
   215→        reset_subjective_count = _reset_subjective_assessments_for_scan_reset(
   216→            state,
   217→            lang_name=getattr(lang_config, "name", None),
   218→        )
   219→
   220→    include_slow = not getattr(args, "skip_slow", False)
   221→    profile = _resolve_scan_profile(getattr(args, "profile", None), lang_config)
   222→    effective_include_slow = _effective_include_slow(include_slow, profile)
   223→
   224→    lang = _configure_lang_runtime(args, config, state, lang_config)
   225→    coverage_warnings = _seed_runtime_coverage_warnings(lang)
   226→    zone_overrides_raw = config.get("zone_overrides")
   227→    zone_overrides = zone_overrides_raw if isinstance(zone_overrides_raw, dict) else None
   228→
   229→    return ScanRuntime(
   230→        args=args,
   231→        state_path=state_file,
   232→        state=state,

   246→def _augment_with_stale_exclusion_findings(
   247→    findings: list[dict[str, Any]],
   248→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   249→) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
   250→    """Append stale exclude findings when excluded dirs are unreferenced."""
   251→    extra_exclusions = get_exclusions()
   252→    if not (extra_exclusions and runtime.lang and runtime.lang.file_finder):
   253→        return findings
   254→
   255→    scanned_files = runtime.lang.file_finder(runtime.path)
   256→    stale = _audit_excluded_dirs(
   257→        extra_exclusions, scanned_files, PROJECT_ROOT
   258→    )
   259→    if not stale:
   260→        return findings
   261→
   262→    augmented = list(findings)
   263→    augmented.extend(stale)
   264→    for stale_finding in stale:
   265→        print(colorize(f"  ℹ {stale_finding['summary']}", "dim"))
   266→    return augmented
```

```
1→"""Dimension and overall scoring aggregation helpers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→
     7→from desloppify.core._internal.text_utils import is_numeric
     8→from desloppify.engine._scoring.detection import detector_stats_by_mode
     9→from desloppify.engine._scoring.policy.core import (
    10→    DETECTOR_SCORING_POLICIES,
    11→    DIMENSIONS,
    12→    DIMENSIONS_BY_NAME,
    13→    FAILURE_STATUSES_BY_MODE,
    14→    MECHANICAL_DIMENSION_WEIGHTS,
    15→    MECHANICAL_WEIGHT_FRACTION,
    16→    MIN_SAMPLE,
    17→    SCORING_MODES,
    18→    SUBJECTIVE_DIMENSION_WEIGHTS,
    19→    SUBJECTIVE_WEIGHT_FRACTION,
    20→    Dimension,
    21→    ScoreMode,
    22→)
    23→from desloppify.engine._scoring.subjective.core import (
    24→    append_subjective_dimensions,
    25→)
    26→
    27→
    28→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    29→class ScoreBundle:
    30→    dimension_scores: dict[str, dict]
    31→    strict_dimension_scores: dict[str, dict]
    32→    verified_strict_dimension_scores: dict[str, dict]
    33→    overall_score: float
    34→    objective_score: float
    35→    strict_score: float
    36→    verified_strict_score: float
    37→
    38→
    39→def compute_dimension_scores_by_mode(
    40→    findings: dict,
    41→    potentials: dict[str, int],
    42→    *,
    43→    subjective_assessments: dict | None = None,
    44→    allowed_subjective_dimensions: set[str] | None = None,
    45→) -> dict[ScoreMode, dict[str, dict]]:
    46→    """Compute dimension scores for lenient/strict/verified_strict in one pass."""
    47→    results: dict[ScoreMode, dict[str, dict]] = {mode: {} for mode in SCORING_MODES}
    48→
    49→    for dim in DIMENSIONS:
    50→        totals = {
    51→            mode: {
    52→                "checks": 0,
    53→                "issues": 0,
    54→                "weighted_failures": 0.0,
    55→                "detectors": {},
    56→            }
    57→            for mode in SCORING_MODES
    58→        }
    59→
    60→        for detector in dim.detectors:
    61→            potential = potentials.get(detector, 0)
    62→            if potential <= 0:
    63→                continue
    64→
    65→            detector_stats = detector_stats_by_mode(detector, findings, potential)
    66→            for mode in SCORING_MODES:
    67→                pass_rate, issues, weighted = detector_stats[mode]
    68→                totals[mode]["checks"] += potential
    69→                totals[mode]["issues"] += issues
    70→                totals[mode]["weighted_failures"] += weighted
    71→                totals[mode]["detectors"][detector] = {
    72→                    "potential": potential,
    73→                    "pass_rate": pass_rate,
    74→                    "issues": issues,
    75→                    "weighted_failures": weighted,
    76→                }
    77→
    78→        for mode in SCORING_MODES:
    79→            total_checks = totals[mode]["checks"]
    80→            if total_checks <= 0:
    81→                continue
    82→            dim_score = (
    83→                max(
    84→                    0.0,
    85→                    (total_checks - totals[mode]["weighted_failures"]) / total_checks,
    86→                )
    87→                * 100
    88→            )
    89→            results[mode][dim.name] = {
    90→                "score": round(dim_score, 1),
    91→                "tier": dim.tier,
    92→                "checks": total_checks,
    93→                "issues": totals[mode]["issues"],
    94→                "detectors": totals[mode]["detectors"],
    95→            }
    96→
    97→    for mode in SCORING_MODES:
    98→        append_subjective_dimensions(
    99→            results[mode],
   100→            findings,
   101→            subjective_assessments,
   102→            FAILURE_STATUSES_BY_MODE[mode],
   103→            allowed_dimensions=allowed_subjective_dimensions,
   104→        )
   105→    return results
   106→
   107→
   108→def compute_dimension_scores(
   109→    findings: dict,
   110→    potentials: dict[str, int],
   111→    *,
   112→    strict: bool = False,
   113→    subjective_assessments: dict | None = None,
   114→    allowed_subjective_dimensions: set[str] | None = None,
   115→) -> dict[str, dict]:
   116→    """Compute per-dimension scores from findings and potentials."""
   117→    mode: ScoreMode = "strict" if strict else "lenient"
   118→    return compute_dimension_scores_by_mode(
   119→        findings,
   120→        potentials,
```

```
1→"""Ornamental line primitives for the scorecard renderer."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.app.output.scorecard_parts.theme import scale
     6→
     7→
     8→def draw_ornament(draw, cx: int, cy: int, size: int, fill) -> None:
     9→    """Draw a small diamond ornament centered at (cx, cy)."""
    10→    draw.polygon(
    11→        [
    12→            (cx, cy - size),
    13→            (cx + size, cy),
    14→            (cx, cy + size),
    15→            (cx - size, cy),
    16→        ],
    17→        fill=fill,
    18→    )
    19→
    20→
    21→def draw_rule_with_ornament(
    22→    draw,
    23→    y: int,
    24→    x1: int,
    25→    x2: int,
    26→    cx: int,
    27→    line_fill,
    28→    ornament_fill,
    29→) -> None:
    30→    """Draw a horizontal rule with a diamond ornament in the center."""
    31→    gap = scale(8)
    32→    draw.rectangle((x1, y, cx - gap, y + 1), fill=line_fill)
    33→    draw.rectangle((cx + gap, y, x2, y + 1), fill=line_fill)
    34→    draw_ornament(draw, cx, y, scale(3), ornament_fill)
    35→
    36→
    37→def draw_vert_rule_with_ornament(
    38→    draw,
    39→    x: int,
    40→    y1: int,
    41→    y2: int,
    42→    cy: int,
    43→    line_fill,
    44→    ornament_fill,
    45→) -> None:
    46→    """Draw a vertical rule with a diamond ornament in the center."""
    47→    gap = scale(8)
    48→    draw.rectangle((x, y1, x + 1, cy - gap), fill=line_fill)
    49→    draw.rectangle((x, cy + gap, x + 1, y2), fill=line_fill)
    50→    draw_ornament(draw, x, cy, scale(3), ornament_fill)
    51→
    52→
    53→__all__ = [
    54→    "draw_ornament",
    55→    "draw_rule_with_ornament",
    56→    "draw_vert_rule_with_ornament",
    57→]
    58→
```

```
1→"""State-path and scan-gating helpers for command modules."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from pathlib import Path
     6→
     7→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.lang import auto_detect_lang_name
     8→from desloppify.core._internal.text_utils import PROJECT_ROOT
     9→from desloppify.utils import colorize
    10→
    11→
    12→def _sole_existing_lang_state_file() -> Path | None:
    13→    """Return the only existing language-specific state file, if unambiguous."""
    14→    state_dir = PROJECT_ROOT / ".desloppify"
    15→    if not state_dir.exists():
    16→        return None
    17→    candidates = sorted(path for path in state_dir.glob("state-*.json") if path.is_file())
    18→    if len(candidates) == 1:
    19→        return candidates[0]
    20→    return None
    21→
    22→
    23→def _allow_lang_state_fallback(args) -> bool:
    24→    """Whether command can safely fallback to the sole existing lang state file."""
    25→    # Scan should always honor detected/explicit language mapping to avoid cross-lang merges.
    26→    return getattr(args, "command", None) != "scan"
    27→
    28→
    29→def state_path(args) -> Path | None:
    30→    """Get state file path from args, or None for default."""
    31→    path_arg = getattr(args, "state", None)
    32→    if path_arg:
    33→        return Path(path_arg)
    34→    lang_name = getattr(args, "lang", None)
    35→    if not lang_name:
    36→        lang_name = auto_detect_lang_name(args)
    37→    if lang_name:
    38→        resolved = PROJECT_ROOT / ".desloppify" / f"state-{lang_name}.json"
    39→        if resolved.exists() or not _allow_lang_state_fallback(args):
    40→            return resolved
    41→        fallback = _sole_existing_lang_state_file()
    42→        if fallback is not None:
    43→            return fallback
    44→        return resolved
    45→
    46→    if _allow_lang_state_fallback(args):
    47→        fallback = _sole_existing_lang_state_file()
    48→        if fallback is not None:
    49→            return fallback
    50→    return None
    51→
    52→
    53→def require_completed_scan(state: dict) -> bool:
    54→    """Return True when the state contains at least one completed scan."""
    55→    has_completed_scan = bool(state.get("last_scan"))
    56→    if not has_completed_scan:
    57→        print(colorize("No scans yet. Run: desloppify scan", "yellow"))
    58→    return has_completed_scan
    59→
```

```
    44→  issues                        Review findings work queue
    45→  plan                          Generate prioritized markdown plan
    46→
    47→examples:
```

```
    56→def _resolve_target_strict_score(config: dict | None) -> tuple[int, str | None]:
    57→    """Resolve strict-score target from config with bounded fallback."""
    58→    raw_target = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE
    59→    if isinstance(config, dict):
    60→        raw_target = config.get("target_strict_score", DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE)
    61→    try:
    62→        target = int(raw_target)
    63→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
    64→        return (
    65→            DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
    66→            (
    67→                f"Invalid config `target_strict_score={raw_target!r}`; using "
    68→                f"{DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE}"
    69→            ),
    70→        )
    71→    if target < MIN_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE or target > MAX_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE:
    72→        return (
    73→            DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
    74→            (
    75→                f"Invalid config `target_strict_score={raw_target!r}`; using "
    76→                f"{DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE}"
    77→            ),
    78→        )
    79→    return target, None
    80→
```

```
    82→def _compute_strict_target(strict_score: float | None, config: dict | None) -> StrictTarget:
    83→    """Build strict-target context for command rendering and agents."""
    84→    target, warning = _resolve_target_strict_score(config)
    85→    if not isinstance(strict_score, int | float):
    86→        return {
    87→            "target": float(target),
    88→            "current": None,
    89→            "gap": None,
    90→            "state": "unavailable",
    91→            "warning": warning,
    92→        }
    93→
    94→    current = round(float(strict_score), 1)
    95→    gap = round(float(target) - current, 1)
    96→    if gap > 0:
    97→        state = "below"
    98→    elif gap < 0:
    99→        state = "above"
   100→    else:
   101→        state = "at"
   102→    return {
   103→        "target": float(target),
   104→        "current": current,
   105→        "gap": gap,
   106→        "state": state,
   107→        "warning": warning,
   108→    }
   109→
   110→
   111→def _count_open_by_detector(findings: dict) -> dict[str, int]:
   112→    """Count open findings by detector, merging structural sub-detectors.
   113→
   114→    When detector is "review" and detail.holistic is True, also increments
   115→    "review_holistic" for separate holistic counting.
   116→    """
   117→    by_detector: dict[str, int] = {}
   118→    for f in findings.values():
   119→        if f["status"] != "open":
   120→            continue
   121→        detector = f.get("detector", "unknown")
   122→        if detector in STRUCTURAL_MERGE:
   123→            detector = "structural"
   124→        by_detector[detector] = by_detector.get(detector, 0) + 1
   125→        # Track holistic review findings separately
   126→        if detector == "review" and f.get("detail", {}).get("holistic"):
   127→            by_detector["review_holistic"] = by_detector.get("review_holistic", 0) + 1
   128→    # Track uninvestigated review findings (only when review findings exist)
   129→    if by_detector.get("review", 0) > 0:
   130→        by_detector["review_uninvestigated"] = sum(
   131→            1
   132→            for f in findings.values()
   133→            if f.get("status") == "open"
   134→            and f.get("detector") == "review"
   135→            and not f.get("detail", {}).get("investigation")
   136→        )
   137→    return by_detector
   138→
   139→
   140→def _resolve_badge_path(project_root: Path) -> tuple[str, Path]:
   141→    """Resolve badge path from config, defaulting to root-level scorecard.png."""
   142→    default_rel = "scorecard.png"
   143→    config = {}
   144→    try:
   145→        config = _load_config()
   146→    except (AttributeError, OSError):
   147→        config = {}
   148→
   149→    raw_path = default_rel
   150→    if isinstance(config, dict):
   151→        configured = config.get("badge_path")
   152→        if isinstance(configured, str) and configured.strip():
   153→            raw_path = configured.strip()
   154→
   155→    path = Path(raw_path)
   156→    is_root_anchored = bool(path.root)
   157→    if not path.is_absolute() and not is_root_anchored:
   158→        return raw_path, project_root / path
   159→
   160→    try:
   161→        rel_path = str(path.relative_to(project_root))
   162→    except ValueError:
   163→        rel_path = str(path)
   164→    return rel_path, path
   165→
   166→
   167→def _compute_badge_status() -> BadgeStatus:
   168→    """Check configured scorecard path and whether README references it."""
   169→    project_root = PROJECT_ROOT
   170→
   171→    scorecard_rel, scorecard_path = _resolve_badge_path(project_root)
   172→    generated = scorecard_path.exists()
   173→
   174→    in_readme = False
   175→    if generated:
   176→        for readme_name in ("README.md", "readme.md", "README.MD"):
   177→            readme_path = project_root / readme_name
   178→            if readme_path.exists():
   179→                try:
   180→                    in_readme = scorecard_rel in readme_path.read_text(
   181→                        encoding="utf-8", errors="replace"
   182→                    )
   183→                except OSError:
   184→                    in_readme = False
   185→                break
   186→
   187→    recommendation = None
   188→    if generated and not in_readme:
   189→        recommendation = (
   190→            f'Add to README: <img src="{scorecard_rel}" width="100%">'
   191→        )
   192→
   193→    return {
   194→        "generated": generated,
   195→        "in_readme": in_readme,
   196→        "path": scorecard_rel,
   197→        "recommendation": recommendation,
   198→    }
```

```
    95→    global_noise_budget: int
    96→    budget_warning: str | None
    97→    hidden_by_detector: dict[str, int]
    98→    hidden_total: int
    99→
   100→
   101→def _configure_lang_runtime(
   102→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   103→    config: dict[str, object],
   104→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   105→    lang: LangRun | None,
   106→) -> LangRun | None:
   107→    """Populate runtime context and threshold overrides for a selected language."""
   108→    if not lang:
   109→        return None
   110→
   111→    lang_options = resolve_lang_runtime_options(args, lang)
   112→    lang_settings = resolve_lang_settings(config, lang)
   113→    runtime_lang = make_lang_run(
   114→        lang,
   115→        overrides=LangRunOverrides(
   116→            review_cache=state.get("review_cache", {}),
   117→            review_max_age_days=config.get("review_max_age_days", 30),
   118→            runtime_settings=lang_settings,
   119→            runtime_options=lang_options,
   120→            large_threshold_override=config.get("large_files_threshold", 0),
   121→            props_threshold_override=config.get("props_threshold", 0),
   122→        ),
   123→    )
   124→
   125→    state.setdefault("lang_capabilities", {})[runtime_lang.name] = {
   126→        "fixers": sorted(runtime_lang.fixers.keys()),
   127→        "typecheck_cmd": runtime_lang.typecheck_cmd,
   128→    }
   129→    return runtime_lang
   130→
   131→
   132→def _reset_subjective_assessments_for_scan_reset(
   133→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   134→    *,
   135→    lang_name: str | None = None,
   136→) -> int:
   137→    """Reset known subjective dimensions to 0 so the next scan starts fresh."""
   138→    assessments = state.setdefault("subjective_assessments", {})
   139→    if not isinstance(assessments, dict):
   140→        assessments = {}
   141→        state["subjective_assessments"] = assessments
   142→
   143→    reset_keys = {
   144→        key.strip()
   145→        for key in assessments
   146→        if isinstance(key, str) and key.strip()
   147→    }
   148→    reset_keys.update(_subjective_reset_dimensions(lang_name=lang_name))
   149→
   150→    now = state_mod.utc_now()
```

```
   289→class NarrativeContext:
   290→    """Optional context inputs for narrative computation."""

   296→
   297→def _history_for_lang(raw_history: list[dict], lang: str | None) -> list[dict]:
   298→    if not lang:
   299→        return raw_history
   300→    return [entry for entry in raw_history if entry.get("lang") in (lang, None)]
   301→
   302→
   303→def _scoped_findings(state: StateModel) -> dict[str, Finding]:
   304→    return path_scoped_findings(
   305→        state.get("findings", {}), state.get("scan_path")
   306→    )
   307→
   308→
   309→def _score_snapshot(state: StateModel) -> tuple[float | None, float | None]:
   310→    return get_strict_score(state), get_overall_score(state)
   311→
   312→
   313→def compute_narrative(
   314→    state: StateModel,
   315→    context: NarrativeContext | None = None,
   316→) -> NarrativeResult:
   317→    """Compute structured narrative context from state data."""
   318→    resolved_context = context or NarrativeContext()
   319→
   320→    diff = resolved_context.diff
   321→    lang = resolved_context.lang
   322→    command = resolved_context.command
   323→    config = resolved_context.config
   324→
   325→    raw_history = state.get("scan_history", [])
   326→    history = _history_for_lang(raw_history, lang)
   327→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {})
   328→    stats = state.get("stats", {})
   329→    strict_score, overall_score = _score_snapshot(state)
   330→    findings = _scoped_findings(state)
   331→
   332→    by_detector = _count_open_by_detector(findings)
   333→    badge = _compute_badge_status()
   334→
   335→    phase = _detect_phase(history, strict_score)
   336→    dimensions = _analyze_dimensions(dim_scores, history, state)
   337→    debt = _analyze_debt(dim_scores, findings, history)
   338→    milestone = _detect_milestone(state, None, history)
   339→    action_context = ActionContext(
   340→        by_detector=by_detector,
   341→        dimension_scores=dim_scores,
   342→        state=state,
   343→        debt=debt,
   344→        lang=lang,
   345→    )
   346→    actions = [dict(action) for action in compute_actions(action_context)]
   347→    strategy = compute_strategy(findings, by_detector, actions, phase, lang)
   348→    tools = dict(compute_tools(by_detector, state, lang, badge))
   349→    primary_action = _compute_primary_action(actions)
   350→    why_now = _compute_why_now(phase, strategy, primary_action)
   351→    verification_step = _compute_verification_step(command)
   352→    risk_flags = _compute_risk_flags(state, debt)
   353→    strict_target = _compute_strict_target(strict_score, config)
   354→    headline = _compute_headline(
   355→        phase,
   356→        dimensions,
   357→        debt,
   358→        milestone,
   359→        diff,
   360→        strict_score,
   361→        overall_score,
   362→        stats,
   363→        history,
   364→        open_by_detector=by_detector,
   365→    )
   366→    reminders, updated_reminder_history = _compute_reminders(
   367→        state, lang, phase, debt, actions, dimensions, badge, command, config=config
   368→    )
   369→
   370→    return {
   371→        "phase": phase,
   372→        "headline": headline,
   373→        "dimensions": dimensions,
   374→        "actions": actions,
   375→        "strategy": strategy,
   376→        "tools": tools,
   377→        "debt": debt,
   378→        "milestone": milestone,
   379→        "primary_action": primary_action,
   380→        "why_now": why_now,
   381→        "verification_step": verification_step,
   382→        "risk_flags": risk_flags,
   383→        "strict_target": strict_target,
   384→        "reminders": reminders,
```
