```
   202→def prepare_scan_runtime(args) -> ScanRuntime:
   203→    """Resolve state/config/language and apply scan-time runtime settings."""
   204→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   205→    state_file = runtime.state_path
   206→    state = runtime.state if isinstance(runtime.state, dict) else {}
   207→    path = Path(args.path)
   208→    config = runtime.config if isinstance(runtime.config, dict) else {}
   209→    lang_config = resolve_lang(args)
   210→    reset_subjective_count = 0
   211→    expired_manual_override_count = _expire_provisional_manual_override_assessments(
   212→        state
   213→    )
   214→    if getattr(args, "reset_subjective", False):
   215→        reset_subjective_count = _reset_subjective_assessments_for_scan_reset(
   216→            state,
   217→            lang_name=getattr(lang_config, "name", None),
   218→        )
   219→
   220→    include_slow = not getattr(args, "skip_slow", False)
   221→    profile = _resolve_scan_profile(getattr(args, "profile", None), lang_config)
   222→    effective_include_slow = _effective_include_slow(include_slow, profile)
   223→
   224→    lang = _configure_lang_runtime(args, config, state, lang_config)
   225→    coverage_warnings = _seed_runtime_coverage_warnings(lang)
   226→    zone_overrides_raw = config.get("zone_overrides")
   227→    zone_overrides = zone_overrides_raw if isinstance(zone_overrides_raw, dict) else None
   228→
   229→    return ScanRuntime(
   230→        args=args,
   231→        state_path=state_file,
   232→        state=state,
   233→        path=path,
   234→        config=config,
   235→        lang=lang,
   236→        lang_label=f" ({lang.name})" if lang else "",
   237→        profile=profile,
   238→        effective_include_slow=effective_include_slow,
   239→        zone_overrides=zone_overrides,
   240→        reset_subjective_count=reset_subjective_count,
   241→        expired_manual_override_count=expired_manual_override_count,
   242→        coverage_warnings=coverage_warnings,
   243→    )
   244→
   245→
   246→def _augment_with_stale_exclusion_findings(
   247→    findings: list[dict[str, Any]],
   248→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   249→) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
   250→    """Append stale exclude findings when excluded dirs are unreferenced."""
   251→    extra_exclusions = get_exclusions()
   252→    if not (extra_exclusions and runtime.lang and runtime.lang.file_finder):
   253→        return findings
   254→
   255→    scanned_files = runtime.lang.file_finder(runtime.path)
   256→    stale = _audit_excluded_dirs(
   257→        extra_exclusions, scanned_files, PROJECT_ROOT
   258→    )
   259→    if not stale:
   260→        return findings
   261→
   262→    augmented = list(findings)
   263→    augmented.extend(stale)
   264→    for stale_finding in stale:
   265→        print(colorize(f"  ℹ {stale_finding['summary']}", "dim"))
   266→    return augmented
   267→
   268→
   269→def _augment_with_stale_wontfix_findings(
   270→    findings: list[dict[str, Any]],
   271→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   272→    *,
   273→    decay_scans: int,
   274→) -> tuple[list[dict[str, Any]], int]:
   275→    """Append re-triage findings for stale/worsening wontfix debt."""
   276→    return _augment_stale_wontfix_impl(
   277→        findings,
   278→        state=runtime.state,
   279→        scan_path=runtime.path,
   280→        project_root=PROJECT_ROOT,
   281→        decay_scans=decay_scans,
   282→    )
   283→
   284→
   285→def run_scan_generation(
   286→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   287→) -> tuple[list[dict[str, Any]], dict[str, object], dict[str, object] | None]:
   288→    """Run detector pipeline and return findings, potentials, and codebase metrics."""
   289→    from desloppify.languages._framework.treesitter import (
   290→        disable_parse_cache,
   291→        enable_parse_cache,
   292→    )
   293→
   294→    enable_file_cache()
   295→    enable_parse_cache()
   296→    try:
   297→        findings, potentials = plan_mod.generate_findings(
   298→            runtime.path,
   299→            lang=runtime.lang,
   300→            options=PlanScanOptions(
   301→                include_slow=runtime.effective_include_slow,
   302→                zone_overrides=runtime.zone_overrides,
   303→                profile=runtime.profile,
   304→            ),
   305→        )
   306→    finally:
   307→        disable_parse_cache()
   308→        disable_file_cache()
   309→
   310→    codebase_metrics = _collect_codebase_metrics(runtime.lang, runtime.path)
   311→    _warn_explicit_lang_with_no_files(
   312→        runtime.args, runtime.lang, runtime.path, codebase_metrics
   313→    )
   314→    findings = _augment_with_stale_exclusion_findings(findings, runtime)
   315→    decay_scans = _coerce_int(
   316→        runtime.config.get("wontfix_decay_scans"),
   317→        default=_WONTFIX_DECAY_SCANS_DEFAULT,
   318→    )
   319→    findings, monitored_wontfix = _augment_with_stale_wontfix_findings(
   320→        findings,
   321→        runtime,
   322→        decay_scans=max(decay_scans, 0),
   323→    )
   324→    potentials["stale_wontfix"] = monitored_wontfix
   325→    return findings, potentials, codebase_metrics
   326→
   327→
   328→def merge_scan_results(
   329→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   330→    findings: list[dict[str, Any]],
   331→    potentials: dict[str, object],
   332→    codebase_metrics: dict[str, object] | None,
   333→) -> ScanMergeResult:
   334→    """Merge findings into persistent state and return diff + previous score snapshot."""
   335→    scan_path_rel = rel(str(runtime.path))
   336→    prev_scan_path = runtime.state.get("scan_path")
   337→    path_changed = prev_scan_path is not None and prev_scan_path != scan_path_rel
   338→
   339→    if not path_changed:
   340→        prev = state_mod.score_snapshot(runtime.state)
   341→    else:
   342→        prev = state_mod.ScoreSnapshot(None, None, None, None)
   343→    prev_dim_scores = (
   344→        runtime.state.get("dimension_scores", {}) if not path_changed else {}
   345→    )
   346→
   347→    if runtime.lang and runtime.lang.zone_map is not None:
   348→        runtime.state["zone_distribution"] = runtime.lang.zone_map.counts()
   349→    _persist_scan_coverage(runtime.state, runtime.lang)
```

```
    43→def _add_scan_parser(sub) -> None:
    44→    p_scan = sub.add_parser("scan", help="Run all detectors, update state, show diff")
    45→    p_scan.add_argument("--path", type=str, default=None)
    46→    p_scan.add_argument("--state", type=str, default=None)

    52→    p_scan.add_argument(

    54→    )
    55→    p_scan.add_argument(
    56→        "--profile",
    57→        choices=["objective", "full", "ci"],
    58→        default=None,
```

```
    p_status.add_argument("--json", action="store_true")
```

```
   161→def compute_health_breakdown(
   162→    dimension_scores: dict, *, score_key: str = "score"
   163→) -> dict[str, object]:
   164→    """Return pool averages and weighted contribution breakdown for score transparency."""
   165→    if not dimension_scores:
   166→        return {
   167→            "overall_score": 100.0,
   168→            "mechanical_fraction": 1.0,
   169→            "subjective_fraction": 0.0,
   170→            "mechanical_avg": 100.0,
   171→            "subjective_avg": None,
   172→            "entries": [],
   173→        }
   174→
   175→    mech_sum = 0.0
   176→    mech_weight = 0.0
   177→    subj_sum = 0.0
   178→    subj_weight = 0.0
   179→    mechanical_rows: list[dict[str, float | str]] = []
   180→    subjective_rows: list[dict[str, float | str]] = []
   181→
   182→    for name, data in dimension_scores.items():
   183→        score = float(data.get(score_key, data.get("score", 0.0)))
   184→        is_subjective = "subjective_assessment" in data.get("detectors", {})
   185→        if is_subjective:
   186→            configured = max(0.0, _subjective_dimension_weight(name, data))
   187→            effective = configured
   188→            subj_sum += score * effective
   189→            subj_weight += effective
   190→            subjective_rows.append(
   191→                {
   192→                    "name": str(name),
   193→                    "score": score,
   194→                    "configured_weight": configured,
   195→                    "effective_weight": effective,
   196→                }
   197→            )
   198→            continue
   199→
   200→        checks = float(data.get("checks", 0) or 0)
   201→        sample_factor = min(1.0, checks / MIN_SAMPLE) if checks > 0 else 0.0
   202→        configured = max(0.0, _mechanical_dimension_weight(name))
   203→        effective = configured * sample_factor
   204→        mech_sum += score * effective
   205→        mech_weight += effective
   206→        mechanical_rows.append(
   207→            {
   208→                "name": str(name),
   209→                "score": score,
   210→                "checks": checks,
   211→                "sample_factor": sample_factor,
   212→                "configured_weight": configured,
   213→                "effective_weight": effective,
   214→            }
   215→        )
   216→
   217→    mech_avg = (mech_sum / mech_weight) if mech_weight > 0 else 100.0
   218→    subj_avg = (subj_sum / subj_weight) if subj_weight > 0 else None
   219→
   220→    if subj_avg is None:
   221→        mechanical_fraction = 1.0
   222→        subjective_fraction = 0.0
   223→        overall_score = round(mech_avg, 1)
   224→    elif mech_weight == 0:
   225→        mechanical_fraction = 0.0
   226→        subjective_fraction = 1.0
   227→        overall_score = round(subj_avg, 1)
   228→    else:
   229→        mechanical_fraction = MECHANICAL_WEIGHT_FRACTION
   230→        subjective_fraction = SUBJECTIVE_WEIGHT_FRACTION
   231→        overall_score = round(
   232→            mech_avg * mechanical_fraction + subj_avg * subjective_fraction,
   233→            1,
   234→        )
   235→
   236→    entries: list[dict[str, float | str]] = []
   237→
   238→    for row in mechanical_rows:
   239→        pool_share = (
   240→            float(row["effective_weight"]) / mech_weight if mech_weight > 0 else 0.0
   241→        )
   242→        per_point = mechanical_fraction * pool_share
   243→        score = float(row["score"])
   244→        entries.append(
   245→            {
   246→                "name": str(row["name"]),
   247→                "pool": "mechanical",
   248→                "score": score,
   249→                "checks": float(row["checks"]),
   250→                "sample_factor": float(row["sample_factor"]),
   251→                "configured_weight": float(row["configured_weight"]),
   252→                "effective_weight": float(row["effective_weight"]),
   253→                "pool_share": pool_share,
   254→                "overall_per_point": per_point,
   255→                "overall_contribution": per_point * score,
   256→                "overall_drag": per_point * (100.0 - score),
   257→            }
   258→        )
   259→
   260→    for row in subjective_rows:
   261→        pool_share = (
   262→            float(row["effective_weight"]) / subj_weight if subj_weight > 0 else 0.0
   263→        )
   264→        per_point = subjective_fraction * pool_share
   265→        score = float(row["score"])
   266→        entries.append(
   267→            {
   268→                "name": str(row["name"]),
   269→                "pool": "subjective",
   270→                "score": score,
   271→                "checks": 0.0,
   272→                "sample_factor": 1.0,
   273→                "configured_weight": float(row["configured_weight"]),
   274→                "effective_weight": float(row["effective_weight"]),
   275→                "pool_share": pool_share,
   276→                "overall_per_point": per_point,
   277→                "overall_contribution": per_point * score,
   278→                "overall_drag": per_point * (100.0 - score),
   279→            }
   280→        )
   281→
   282→    return {
   283→        "overall_score": overall_score,
   284→        "mechanical_fraction": mechanical_fraction,
   285→        "subjective_fraction": subjective_fraction,
   286→        "mechanical_avg": mech_avg,
   287→        "subjective_avg": subj_avg,
   288→        "entries": entries,
   289→    }
   290→
   291→
```

```
1→"""State scoring, statistics, and suppression accounting."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from copy import deepcopy
     6→
     7→__all__ = [
     8→    "suppression_metrics",
     9→]
    10→
    11→from desloppify.core._internal.coercions import coerce_confidence
    12→from desloppify.engine._scoring.policy.core import matches_target_score
    13→from desloppify.engine._state.filtering import path_scoped_findings
    14→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel, ensure_state_defaults
    15→from desloppify.languages._framework.base.types import ScanCoverageRecord
    16→
    17→_EMPTY_COUNTERS = ("open", "fixed", "auto_resolved", "wontfix", "false_positive")
    18→_SUBJECTIVE_TARGET_RESET_THRESHOLD = 2
    19→
    20→
    21→def _resolve_lang_from_state(state: StateModel) -> str | None:
    22→    """Best-effort language detection from state (scan_history > lang_capabilities)."""
    23→    history = state.get("scan_history")
    24→    if isinstance(history, list):
    25→        for entry in reversed(history):
    26→            if isinstance(entry, dict):
    27→                lang = entry.get("lang")
    28→                if isinstance(lang, str) and lang.strip():
    29→                    return lang.strip().lower()
    30→    capabilities = state.get("lang_capabilities")
    31→    if isinstance(capabilities, dict) and len(capabilities) == 1:
    32→        only_lang = next(iter(capabilities.keys()))
    33→        if isinstance(only_lang, str) and only_lang.strip():
    34→            return only_lang.strip().lower()
    35→    return None
    36→
    37→
    38→def _count_findings(findings: dict) -> tuple[dict[str, int], dict[int, dict[str, int]]]:
    39→    """Tally per-status counters and per-tier breakdowns."""
    40→    counters = dict.fromkeys(_EMPTY_COUNTERS, 0)
    41→    tier_stats: dict[int, dict[str, int]] = {}
    42→
    43→    for finding in findings.values():
    44→        status = finding["status"]
    45→        tier = finding.get("tier", 3)
    46→        counters[status] = counters.get(status, 0) + 1
    47→        tier_counter = tier_stats.setdefault(tier, dict.fromkeys(_EMPTY_COUNTERS, 0))
    48→        tier_counter[status] = tier_counter.get(status, 0) + 1
    49→
    50→    return counters, tier_stats
    51→
    52→
    53→def _coerce_subjective_score(value: dict | float | int | str | None) -> float:
    54→    """Normalize a subjective assessment score payload to a 0-100 float."""
    55→    raw = value.get("score", 0) if isinstance(value, dict) else value
    56→    try:
    57→        score = float(raw)
    58→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
    59→        score = 0.0
    60→    return max(0.0, min(100.0, score))
    61→
    62→
    63→def _subjective_target_matches(
    64→    subjective_assessments: dict, *, target: float
    65→) -> list[str]:
    66→    """Return dimension keys whose subjective score matches the target band."""
    67→    matches = [
    68→        dimension
    69→        for dimension, payload in subjective_assessments.items()
    70→        if matches_target_score(_coerce_subjective_score(payload), target)
    71→    ]
    72→    return sorted(matches)
    73→
    74→
    75→def _subjective_integrity_baseline(target: float | None) -> dict[str, object]:
    76→    """Create baseline subjective-integrity metadata for scan/reporting output."""
    77→    return {
    78→        "status": "disabled" if target is None else "pass",
    79→        "target_score": None if target is None else round(float(target), 2),
    80→        "matched_count": 0,
    81→        "matched_dimensions": [],
    82→        "reset_dimensions": [],
    83→    }
    84→
    85→
    86→def _apply_subjective_integrity_policy(
    87→    subjective_assessments: dict,
    88→    *,
    89→    target: float,
    90→) -> tuple[dict, dict[str, object]]:
    91→    """Apply anti-gaming penalties for subjective scores clustered on the target."""
    92→    normalized_target = max(0.0, min(100.0, float(target)))
    93→    matched_dimensions = _subjective_target_matches(
    94→        subjective_assessments,
    95→        target=normalized_target,
    96→    )
    97→    meta = _subjective_integrity_baseline(normalized_target)
    98→    meta["matched_count"] = len(matched_dimensions)
    99→    meta["matched_dimensions"] = matched_dimensions
   100→
```

```
303→def compute_score_bundle(
   304→    findings: dict,

   306→    *,
   307→    subjective_assessments: dict | None = None,
   308→    allowed_subjective_dimensions: set[str] | None = None,
   309→) -> ScoreBundle:

   319→    strict_scores = by_mode["strict"]

   325→        if "subjective_assessment" not in data.get("detectors", {})

   331→        verified_strict_dimension_scores=verified_strict_scores,
   332→        overall_score=compute_health_score(lenient_scores),
   333→        objective_score=compute_health_score(mechanical_lenient_scores),
   334→        strict_score=compute_health_score(strict_scores),
   335→        verified_strict_score=compute_health_score(verified_strict_scores),
   336→    )
   337→
   338→
   339→def compute_score_impact(
   340→    dimension_scores: dict,
   341→    potentials: dict[str, int],
   342→    detector: str,
   343→    issues_to_fix: int,
   344→) -> float:
   345→    """Estimate score improvement from fixing N issues in a detector."""
   346→    target_dim = None
   347→    for dim in DIMENSIONS:
   348→        if detector in dim.detectors:
   349→            target_dim = dim
   350→            break
   351→    if target_dim is None or target_dim.name not in dimension_scores:
   352→        return 0.0
   353→
   354→    potential = potentials.get(detector, 0)
   355→    if potential <= 0:
   356→        return 0.0
   357→
   358→    dim_data = dimension_scores[target_dim.name]
   359→    old_score = compute_health_score(dimension_scores)
   360→
   361→    det_data = dim_data["detectors"].get(detector)
   362→    if not det_data:
   363→        return 0.0
   364→
   365→    old_weighted = det_data["weighted_failures"]
   366→    new_weighted = max(0.0, old_weighted - issues_to_fix * 1.0)
   367→
   368→    total_potential = 0
   369→    total_new_weighted_failures = 0.0
   370→    for det in target_dim.detectors:
   371→        det_values = dim_data["detectors"].get(det)
   372→        if not det_values:
   373→            continue
   374→        total_potential += det_values["potential"]
   375→        if det == detector:
   376→            total_new_weighted_failures += new_weighted
   377→        else:
   378→            total_new_weighted_failures += det_values["weighted_failures"]
   379→    if total_potential <= 0:
   380→        return 0.0
   381→
   382→    new_dim_score = (
   383→        max(
   384→            0.0,
   385→            (total_potential - total_new_weighted_failures) / total_potential,
   386→        )
   387→        * 100
   388→    )
   389→
   390→    simulated = {k: dict(v) for k, v in dimension_scores.items()}
   391→    simulated[target_dim.name] = {**dim_data, "score": round(new_dim_score, 1)}
   392→    new_score = compute_health_score(simulated)
   393→
   394→    return round(new_score - old_score, 1)
   395→
   396→
   397→def get_dimension_for_detector(detector: str) -> Dimension | None:
   398→    """Look up which dimension a detector belongs to."""
   399→    policy = DETECTOR_SCORING_POLICIES.get(detector)
   400→    if not policy or policy.dimension is None:
   401→        return None
   402→    return DIMENSIONS_BY_NAME.get(policy.dimension)
```

```
    70→def _ts_treesitter_phases() -> list[DetectorPhase]:
    71→    """Cherry-pick tree-sitter phases that complement TS's own detectors.
    72→
    73→    TS already has its own smells and unused detection — only add cohesion
    74→    which TS lacks.  (Signature analysis is added separately since it's
    75→    backend-agnostic and doesn't require tree-sitter.)
    76→    """
    77→    from desloppify.languages._framework.treesitter import is_available
    78→
    79→    if not is_available():
    80→        return []
    81→
    82→    from desloppify.languages._framework.treesitter._specs import TREESITTER_SPECS
    83→
    84→    spec = TREESITTER_SPECS.get("typescript")
    85→    if spec is None:
    86→        return []
    87→
    88→    return [
    89→        make_cohesion_phase(spec),
    90→    ]
    91→

    94→    ts_test_coverage_hooks.has_testable_logic,
    95→    ts_test_coverage_hooks.resolve_import_spec,
    96→    ts_test_coverage_hooks.map_test_to_source,
    97→)

    99→
   100→register_lang_hooks("typescript", test_coverage=ts_test_coverage_hooks)
```

```
    12→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
```

## Comprehensive Scorecard/Badge Generation Analysis

### 1. **Where the Scorecard PNG is Generated**

The scorecard PNG is generated by the `generate_scorecard()` function in:
- **Main file**: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/output/scorecard.py` (lines 43-126)

The function uses PIL (Pillow) for rendering:
- Uses `PIL.Image` to create the RGB image
- Uses `PIL.ImageDraw` to draw shapes and text
- Defers PIL imports until needed (lines 45-47) since PIL is an optional dependency

### 2. **Data Flow into the Scorecard**

The scorecard receives:

**From `state` dict:**
- `overall_score`: Main health score (via `get_overall_score(state)` from `state.py`)
- `strict_score`: Conservative variant (via `get_strict_score(state)`)
- `dimension_scores`: Dictionary of dimension names → {score, strict, checks, issues, tier, detectors}

**Metadata:**
- `project_name`: Resolved from GitHub CLI, git remote, or folder name (via `resolve_project_name()` in `meta.py`)
- `package_version`: From installed metadata or `pyproject.toml` (via `resolve_package_version()` in `meta.py`)

**Dimensions processing:**
```python
active_dims = prepare_scorecard_dimensions(state)  # Extracts from dimension_scores
```

This function (in `dimensions.py`, lines 180-207):
1. Extracts all dimensions from `state["dimension_scores"]`
2. Collapses elegance dimensions (High/Mid/Low elegance → single "Elegance" row)
3. Filters out unassessed subjective placeholders (score=0, issues=0)
4. Limits to max 20 rows with language-specific priority ordering
5. Sorts with "File health" first, then alphabetically

### 3. **How badge_path CLI Flag is Wired**

**CLI Definition** (`parser_groups.py`, lines 71-77):
```python
p_scan.add_argument(
    "--badge-path",
    type=str,
    default=None,
    metavar="PATH",
    help="Output path for scorecard image (default: scorecard.png)",
)
```

**Flow through execution**:
1. **scan.py** calls `emit_scorecard_badge(args, config, state)` (in `scan_artifacts.py`)
2. **scan_artifacts.py** (lines 85-176):
   - Loads scorecard helpers lazily via importlib
   - Calls `get_badge_config(args, config)` to resolve the path
3. **scorecard.py** `get_badge_config()` (lines 129-158):
   - Checks CLI args first: `getattr(args, "badge_path", None)`
   - Falls back to config: `cfg.get("badge_path")`
   - Falls back to env var: `os.environ.get("DESLOPPIFY_BADGE_PATH", "scorecard.png")`
   - Resolves relative paths against PROJECT_ROOT
   - Returns tuple: `(output_path, disabled)`

**Disable flag**: `--no-badge` sets `args.no_badge=True`, which skips generation entirely

### 4. **Current Scorecard Layout/Structure**

The rendered scorecard is **landscape (wide) format**, 780×~180px (at SCALE=1; rendered at 2x internally):

**Three-panel layout:**

1. **Left Panel** (25% width):
   - Version: "v0.7.6" (small, monospace)
   - Title: "DESLOPPIFY SCORE" (serif, bold, large)
   - Horizontal divider with diamond ornament
   - Main score: "90.8" (serif, bold, 42pt, color-coded)
   - Strict score: "strict 90.8" (serif, right-aligned, pastel color, smaller)
   - Project pill: "peteromallet/desloppify" (rounded rectangle, bordered)

2. **Vertical Divider** (2% width):
   - Vertical rule with diamond ornament at center
   - Color: BORDER with ACCENT diamond

3. **Right Panel** (73% width):
   - Two-column grid of dimensions (max 20 total)
   - Each dimension row shows:
     - **Name** (left, truncated with ellipsis if needed)
     - **Health score** (center-right, bold monospace, color-coded)
     - **Strict score** (right, small monospace, muted color)
   - Rows alternate between BG_TABLE and BG_ROW_ALT backgrounds

**Color Palette** (theme.py, lines 100-109):
```python
BG = (247, 240, 228)           # Beige background
BG_SCORE = (240, 232, 217)     # Left panel background
BG_TABLE = (240, 233, 220)     # Table background
BG_ROW_ALT = (234, 226, 212)   # Alternating row shade
TEXT = (58, 48, 38)            # Dark brown text
DIM = (138, 122, 102)          # Dimmed text (version, labels)
BORDER = (192, 176, 152)       # Border/divider lines
ACCENT = (148, 112, 82)        # Diamond ornaments
FRAME = (172, 152, 126)        # Outer frame
```

**Score Color Coding** (theme.py, lines 16-34):
- **≥90**: Deep sage green (68, 120, 68)
- **70-90**: Olive green (120, 140, 72)
- **<70**: Yellow-green (145, 155, 80)
- **Muted variant** (for strict column): Pastel peach/apricot/coral family

**Layout Constants** (all scaled by SCALE=2):
- Row height: 20px
- Image width: 780px
- Divider x-position: 260px (left panel cutoff)
- Frame inset: 5px (double frame)
- Rows per column: ceil(dimension_count / 2)

### 5. **Multi-Language & Per-Language Breakdown**

**Current Design:**
- **No per-language split in scorecard** — the image shows aggregated metrics
- The scorecard displays dimensions from whichever language was **most recently scanned** or has the **most findings**

**Language Detection** (dimensions.py, lines 16-45 `resolve_scorecard_lang()`):
1. Checks `scan_history` for most recent scan's language
2. Falls back to `lang_capabilities` if only one language is registered
3. Falls back to counting findings by language (uses language with most findings)
4. Returns None if no language detected

**Language-Specific Dimension Ordering** (dimension_policy.py):
- Python, TypeScript, and C# each have preferred dimension orders for truncation
- When scorecard exceeds 20 rows, mechanical dimensions (File health, Code quality, etc.) are kept first
- Then subjective dimensions are added in language-specific priority order
- This ensures "important" dimensions for each language appear on the scorecard

**Example**: 
- Python prioritizes: Elegance, Abstraction fit, Error consistency, etc.
- TypeScript prioritizes: Elegance, Abstraction fit, API coherence, Auth consistency, etc.

---

## Key Findings Summary

| Aspect | Detail |
|--------|--------|
| **Generation** | `generate_scorecard(state, output_path)` in `scorecard.py` |
| **PIL Usage** | Deferred imports; Image, ImageDraw, ImageFont for rendering |
| **Data Source** | `dimension_scores` dict from scan state; main/strict scores from state functions |
| **CLI Wiring** | `--badge-path PATH`, `--no-badge`, env var `DESLOPPIFY_BADGE_PATH` |
| **Config Chain** | CLI args → config dict → env vars → default "scorecard.png" |
| **Layout** | Landscape 780px wide, left panel (scores/metadata) + divider + right panel (2-column dimension table) |
| **Dimensions Shown** | Max 20 rows from `dimension_scores`, elegance-collapsed, filtered for real data |
| **Language Support** | Detects active language; uses language-specific dimension priority for truncation |
| **Colors** | Warm earth tones (beige, brown); score-based green/olive/yellow-green coding |
| **Font Loading** | Cross-platform TTF loading with fallbacks (macOS/Linux/DejaVu) |

The scorecard is **fully dynamically generated** from scan state — no hardcoded dimensions or single-language assumptions in the rendering logic itself.

## Comprehensive Exploration Report: Desloppify Scan Command Flow

Based on my thorough exploration of the desloppify codebase, here's a detailed breakdown of the scan command architecture and reporting system:

---

### 1. Scan Command Handler Location & Orchestration

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/scan/scan.py`

The `cmd_scan()` function (lines 113-205) is the main entry point. It orchestrates the full scan lifecycle:

```python
def cmd_scan(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    """Run all detectors, update persistent state, show diff."""
    runtime = prepare_scan_runtime(args)
    orchestrator = ScanOrchestrator(
        runtime,
        run_scan_generation_fn=run_scan_generation,
        merge_scan_results_fn=merge_scan_results,
        resolve_noise_snapshot_fn=resolve_noise_snapshot,
        persist_reminder_history_fn=persist_reminder_history,
    )
```

**Orchestration phases:**
1. **Runtime prep** (`prepare_scan_runtime`) - Load state, config, language settings, handle subjective resets
2. **Generation** (`orchestrator.generate()`) - Run all detectors
3. **Merge** (`orchestrator.merge()`) - Merge findings, compute scores, update state
4. **Noise resolution** (`orchestrator.noise_snapshot()`) - Calculate hidden findings
5. **Reporting** - Multiple reporting calls (shown below)
6. **Narrative** (`show_post_scan_analysis()`) - Compute coaching narrative
7. **Persistence** - Save query payload and reminders

### 4. Scoring System Architecture

**Main Scoring Files:**
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/scoring.py` - Facade
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_scoring/results/core.py` - Core compute functions
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_scoring/policy/core.py` - Policies and dimensions

**Core Score Computation Flow:**

1. **Dimension Scoring** (`compute_dimension_scores_by_mode()`):
   - Computes per-dimension scores for three modes: **lenient**, **strict**, **verified_strict**
   - For each dimension, aggregates detectors into:
     - `checks`: potential issues
     - `issues`: actual findings
     - `weighted_failures`: mode-specific weighted failures
   - Formula: `dim_score = (checks - weighted_failures) / checks * 100`
   - Appends subjective dimensions via `append_subjective_dimensions()`

2. **Health Breakdown** (`compute_health_breakdown()`):
   - **Mechanical dimensions** (objective): weighted by sample factor (checks/MIN_SAMPLE)
   - **Subjective dimensions**: unweighted by sample
   - Two-pool blend: `overall_score = mech_avg * 0.6 + subj_avg * 0.4`
   - Returns contribution breakdown for each dimension (drag analysis)

3. **Score Bundle** (`compute_score_bundle()`):
   - Computes **all four canonical scores** in one pass:
     - **overall**: all dimensions (mech + subj) in lenient mode
     - **objective**: mechanical dimensions only in lenient mode
     - **strict**: all dimensions in strict mode (includes wontfix/ignored)
     - **verified_strict**: all dimensions in verified_strict mode (only verified findings count)

**Score Retrieval** (`state.py`):
```python
class ScoreSnapshot(NamedTuple):
    overall: float | None
    objective: float | None
    strict: float | None
    verified: float | None

def score_snapshot(state: StateModel) -> ScoreSnapshot:
    """Load all four canonical scores from *state* in one call."""
```

**What this means:**
- This block is printed **only to agent environments** (Claude, Gemini, Codex, Cursor)
- Detected via `_is_agent_environment()` checking env vars: `CLAUDE_CODE`, `DESLOPPIFY_AGENT`, `GEMINI_CLI`, `CODEX_SANDBOX*`, `CURSOR_TRACE_ID`
- The contract mandates:
  1. Always show the canonical 4-score trio (overall, objective, strict, verified)
  2. Show ALL dimension scores in a markdown table
  3. Include both mechanical and subjective dimensions
  4. Goal is to maximize strict scores (the lenient score can be inflated by wontfix)

### 7. Path Scoping in Scan Command

**Argparse Setup** (`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/cli_support/parser_groups.py` lines 43-85):

```python
def _add_scan_parser(sub) -> None:
    p_scan = sub.add_parser("scan", help="Run all detectors, update state, show diff")
    p_scan.add_argument("--path", type=str, default=None)
    p_scan.add_argument("--state", type=str, default=None)

    p_scan.add_argument("--skip-slow", action="store_true")
    p_scan.add_argument("--profile", choices=["objective", "full", "ci"])
    p_scan.add_argument("--force-resolve", action="store_true")
    p_scan.add_argument("--no-badge", action="store_true")
    p_scan.add_argument("--badge-path", type=str)
    p_scan.add_argument("--lang-opt", action="append")
```

**Path Resolution Flow** (`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/cli.py` lines 61-86):

```python
def _resolve_default_path(args) -> None:
    """Fill args.path from detected language or default source path."""
    if getattr(args, "path", None) is not None:
        return  # User provided explicit path
    if getattr(args, "command", None) == "review":
        try:
            saved_path = load_state(state_file).get("scan_path")
            if saved_path:
                args.path = str((PROJECT_ROOT / saved_path).resolve())
                return
        except: pass
    lang = resolve_lang(args)
    args.path = str(PROJECT_ROOT / lang.default_src) if lang else str(DEFAULT_PATH)
```

**Path Storage** (`scan_workflow.py` lines 229-243):

The path is stored in state as `scan_path` during merge, and subsequent commands respect this scope:

```python
    args=args,
    state_path=state_file,
    state=state,
    path=path,  # <-- Scoped path
    ...
)
```

### Summary

The scan command flow is a carefully orchestrated pipeline:

```
user invokes scan
    ↓
parse args, resolve path (--path or default)
    ↓
prepare_scan_runtime (load state, config, lang)
    ↓
ScanOrchestrator.generate() → findings, potentials, metrics
    ↓
ScanOrchestrator.merge() → merge with prior state, compute scores
    ↓
show_diff_summary() → +new / -resolved / ↻reopened
    ↓
show_score_delta() → all 4 scores with deltas
    ↓
show_scorecard_subjective_measures() → dimension table with health/strict
    ↓
show_score_model_breakdown() → contribution analysis
    ↓
show_dimension_deltas() / show_low_dimension_hints()
    ↓
show_score_integrity() → wontfix debt, ignore patterns
    ↓
show_concern_count()
    ↓
show_post_scan_analysis()
    ├── _coverage_reduction_warnings()
    ├── compute_narrative() → phase, headline, actions, strategy, reminders
    ├── print headline + ranked actions
    ├── print plan fields (why_now, primary_action, verification_step)
    ├── _print_filtered_reminders()
    ├── _show_subjective_score_nudge()
    └── review findings nudge
    ↓
_print_llm_summary() → agent instruction block with ALL scores
    ↓
emit_scorecard_badge()
    ↓
auto_update_skill()
    ↓
write_query() → persist all metadata to .desloppify/query.json
```

The "score reporting contract" block (`_print_llm_summary`, lines 255-260) mandates that agents must present all four canonical scores plus all dimension scores in a markdown table, as this is crucial for the agent to make informed decisions about next steps.

## Comprehensive Multi-Language Handling Analysis

Based on my thorough exploration of the desloppify codebase, here's a detailed understanding of how multi-language handling works:

---

### **1. Language Detection and Registration**

**Language Registry Architecture:**
- **Location**: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/languages/_framework/registry_state.py`
- Languages are registered at import time via the `@register_lang(name)` decorator (in `/languages/__init__.py`)
- **Registry state** is a global mutable dict (`_registry`) that stores `LangConfig` instances keyed by language name
- **Plugins** discovered from:
  - Built-in packages: `/languages/{python,typescript,go,rust,java,...}/` (32 language directories found)
  - Single-file plugins: `/languages/plugin_*.py` naming convention
  - User plugins: `.desloppify/plugins/*.py`

**Auto-Detection Mechanism:**
- File: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/helpers/lang.py`
- `auto_detect_lang_name()` → `auto_detect_lang(project_root)` in `resolution.py`
- **Detection markers**: each language plugin declares `detect_markers` (e.g., `pyproject.toml` for Python, `package.json` for TypeScript)
- **Fallback strategy**: if multiple config files present (py + ts), counts actual source files in each language's extensions and picks the dominant one
- No multi-language scan in a single invocation — picks ONE language

---

### **2. State Storage Per Language**

**File Naming Convention:**
- Location: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/.desloppify/`
- **Format**: `state-{lang}.json` (e.g., `state-python.json`, `state-typescript.json`, `state-bash.json`)
- **Observed files** (from `.desloppify/`): state-bash.json, state-csharp.json, state-dart.json, state-docker.json, state-gdscript.json, state-go.json, state-java.json, state-python.json (10.4MB), state-ruby.json, state-rust.json, etc.
- **Atomic writes**: temp file + `os.replace()` with `.json.bak` backups

**State Resolution Logic** (`state_path()` in `/commands/helpers/state.py`):
1. Explicit `--state` flag takes precedence
2. If language detected/specified → `.desloppify/state-{lang_name}.json`
3. Fallback for non-scan commands: if only ONE `state-*.json` file exists, use it (allows legacy workflows)
4. Scan command always respects language mapping (no cross-lang merges)

---

### **3. The `--lang` CLI Flag**

**Top-level parser** (`/cli_support/parser.py` lines 89-94):
```python
parser.add_argument(
    "--lang",
    type=str,
    default=None,
    help=f"Language to scan ({lang_help}). Auto-detected if omitted.",
)
```

**How it works:**
- `--lang python scan --path desloppify` → forces Python language plugin, scans `desloppify/` with Python rules
- Global flag on main parser, applies to ALL commands (scan, status, show, etc.)
- Resolution chain in `resolve_lang()`:
  1. Check `args.lang` (explicit CLI flag)
  2. Fallback to `auto_detect_lang_name()` if `--lang` not provided
  3. Raises `LangResolutionError` if language name doesn't exist

**Example from usage**:
```
desloppify --lang python scan --path scripts/desloppify
desloppify --lang typescript tree --focus src/components
```

---

### **4. Multi-Language Scan Support**

**Current Status**: ❌ **NO per-language split in single scan invocation**

- Each `scan` invocation processes ONE language only
- No `--by-language` flag exists
- No `--all-languages` flag
- The tool picks the "dominant" language via auto-detection and scans only that
- **Workaround**: Run multiple scans with explicit `--lang` flag:
  ```bash
  desloppify --lang python scan --path src
  desloppify --lang typescript scan --path src
  ```
- Each creates/updates its own `state-{lang}.json`

---

### **5. StateModel Type and Field Structure**

**Type Definition**: `/engine/_state/schema.py`

```python
class StateModel(TypedDict, total=False):
    # Canonical scores (always present)
    overall_score: Required[float]              # Weighted aggregate
    objective_score: Required[float]            # Dimension-based (no subjective)
    strict_score: Required[float]               # Mechanical only
    verified_strict_score: Required[float]      # Strict with attestation
    
    # Dimension breakdown (optional, filled by scan)
    dimension_scores: dict[str, DimensionScore] # Key: dimension name (e.g., "Complexity", "Security")
    
    # Core state
    findings: dict[str, Finding]                # All detected issues
    stats: StateStats                           # Counters by status/tier
    scan_history: list[ScanHistoryEntry]       # Previous scan snapshots
    
    # Metadata
    version: Required[int]
    created: Required[str]
    last_scan: Required[str | None]
    scan_count: Required[int]
    scan_path: str                              # Relative path scanned
    scan_coverage: dict[str, ScanCoverageRecord] # Per-language coverage metadata
    
    # Subjective assessments
    subjective_integrity: SubjectiveIntegrity   # Anti-gaming metadata
    subjective_assessments: dict[str, SubjectiveAssessment] # Dimension scores from review
    
    # Other
    zone_distribution: dict[str, int]           # Zone counts (test/config/generated/etc)
    score_confidence: dict[str, Any]
    potentials: dict[str, int]
    codebase_metrics: dict[str, Any]
```

**DimensionScore structure**:
```python
class DimensionScore(TypedDict, total=False):
    score: float                    # Lenient score (0-100)
    strict: float                   # Alias for strict_score
    strict_score: float             # Mechanical + attestation score
    verified_strict_score: float    # Verified strict score
    checks: int                     # Total checks run
    issues: int                     # Open issues
    tier: int                       # Priority tier (1-5)
    detectors: dict[str, Any]       # Per-detector breakdown
    carried_forward: bool           # If skipped due to --skip-slow
```

```python
def cmd_status(args):
    runtime = command_runtime(args)  # Loads state for detected/specified language
    state = runtime.state
    config = runtime.config
    
    scores = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)  # Returns ScoreSnapshot(overall, objective, strict, verified)
    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {})  # Dict[str, DimensionScore]
    
    # Output components:
    show_dimension_table(state, dim_scores)       # Shows all dimensions
    show_score_model_breakdown(state, dim_scores) # Objective vs strict
    show_focus_suggestion(dim_scores, state)      # Recommends next fix area
    show_review_summary(state)                    # Review progress
    compute_narrative(state)                      # LLM coaching
```

### **7. Language Plugin Registration System**

**Example: Python Plugin** (`/languages/python/__init__.py`):
```python
@register_lang("python")  # Decorator auto-instantiates and validates
class PythonConfig(LangConfig):
    name: str = "python"
    default_src: Path = Path("src")
    file_finder: FileFinder = find_py_files
    function_extractor: FunctionExtractor = extract_py_functions
    phases: list[DetectorPhase] = [
        phase_smells,
        phase_structural,
        phase_unused,
        phase_coupling,
        ...
    ]
    zone_rules: list[ZoneRule] = PY_ZONE_RULES
    detect_markers: list[str] = ["pyproject.toml", "setup.py", "setup.cfg", "go.mod", ...]
```

**Discovery Flow**:
1. `desloppify` imports `from desloppify.languages import available_langs`
2. `available_langs()` calls `load_all()` if not yet loaded
3. `load_all()` scans `/languages/{*,plugin_*.py}` and imports all
4. Each import triggers `@register_lang` decorator → stores `LangConfig` instance in `registry_state._registry`
5. `get_lang(name)` retrieves from registry

**Validation**: Each plugin is validated on registration:
- Structure validation: required dirs/files exist (commands/, detectors/, phases.py, etc.)
- Contract validation: LangConfig has required methods (phase execution, etc.)

### **8. Dimension Scores Computation and Storage**

**Where computed** (`/engine/_state/scoring.py` lines 337-386):

```python
def _compute_stats_and_scores(state, findings, merged, subjective_assessments, ...):
    # 1. Compute score bundle from findings + subjective assessments
    bundle = compute_score_bundle(
        findings,
        merged,
        subjective_assessments=subjective_assessments,
        allowed_subjective_dimensions=allowed_subjective,
    )
    
    # 2. Extract three score modes
    lenient_scores = bundle.dimension_scores
    strict_scores = bundle.strict_dimension_scores
    verified_strict_scores = bundle.verified_strict_dimension_scores
    
    # 3. Store in state
    state["dimension_scores"] = {
        name: {
            "score": lenient_scores[name]["score"],
            "strict_score": strict_scores[name]["score"],
            "verified_strict_score": verified_strict_scores[name]["score"],
            "checks": lenient_scores[name]["checks"],
            "issues": lenient_scores[name]["issues"],
            "tier": lenient_scores[name]["tier"],
            "detectors": lenient_scores[name].get("detectors", {}),
        }
        for name in lenient_scores
    }
    
    # 4. Aggregate to four canonical scores
    state.update(_aggregate_scores(state["dimension_scores"], compute_health_score))
    # → sets: overall_score, objective_score, strict_score, verified_strict_score
```

**Dimension list** comes from scoring policies (`/engine/_scoring/policy/core.py`):
- Standard dimensions: Complexity, Coupling, Consistency, Duplication, Hygiene, Refactoring, Security, Smells, Structure, Testing
- Subjective dimensions (per language in `intelligence/review/dimensions/lang.py`)

---

### **9. Scan Workflow and Score Updates**

**Main scan flow** (`/commands/scan/scan.py`):
1. `prepare_scan_runtime()` → loads language, config, state
2. `run_scan_generation()` → runs detector phases, returns findings
3. `merge_scan_results()` → upserts findings, saves previous scores
4. `_compute_stats_and_scores()` → **populates dimension_scores in state**
5. `save_state()` → writes to `state-{lang}.json`

**Key insight**: Dimension scores are **computed fresh on every scan** from current findings, then stored in the StateModel. Previous dimensions from `--skip-slow` are carried forward if not recomputed.

---

| Aspect | Implementation |
|--------|-----------------|
| **Language Detection** | Auto-detect by config file markers + file count; no multi-lang scan per invocation |
| **State File Naming** | `state-{lang}.json` per language in `.desloppify/` directory |
| **--lang Flag** | Global CLI flag, can override auto-detection |
| **Multi-Language Support** | ❌ Not available; run multiple scans with different `--lang` values |
| **Canonical Scores** | `overall_score`, `objective_score`, `strict_score`, `verified_strict_score` (4 fields) |
| **dimension_scores** | Dict[dimension_name → {score, strict_score, verified_strict_score, checks, issues, tier, detectors}] |
| **Status Output** | Reads state for detected/explicit language; prints scores + dimension table + narrative coaching |
| **Plugin Registry** | Global `_registry` dict; ~32 languages built-in; discovered via `@register_lang` decorator |

---

### **Key Files Reference**

| File | Purpose |
|------|---------|
| `/cli.py` | Main entry: parse args, resolve language, dispatch commands |
| `/languages/__init__.py` | Public API: `register_lang`, `available_langs`, `auto_detect_lang`, `get_lang` |
| `/languages/_framework/registry_state.py` | Global mutable registry dict; lang instance storage |
| `/languages/_framework/resolution.py` | `auto_detect_lang()`, `get_lang()`, language instantiation |
| `/languages/_framework/discovery.py` | `load_all()` plugin discovery from `/languages/` directories |
| `/app/commands/helpers/lang.py` | `resolve_lang()`, `auto_detect_lang_name()`, `resolve_lang_settings()` |
| `/app/commands/helpers/state.py` | `state_path()` — resolves `state-{lang}.json` file paths |
| `/engine/_state/schema.py` | StateModel TypedDict with all four scores + dimension_scores |
| `/engine/_state/scoring.py` | `_compute_stats_and_scores()` — populates dimension_scores in state |
| `/app/commands/status_cmd.py` | Status command; reads state and displays scores |
| `/app/commands/scan/scan_workflow.py` | Scan orchestration; calls scoring to populate state |
| `/scoring.py` | Facade for objective dimension scoring (`compute_dimension_scores`, `compute_health_score`) |

This architecture enables language-isolated scanning with language-specific state files, clear dimension-based scoring, and per-language narrative coaching.

> DEVELOPER

Design an implementation plan for per-language scorecards in the desloppify codebase. The user specifically asked for the plan to think about how the information can be "beautiful and elegant." This will be saved as a .md design document, not implemented yet.

## Current Architecture

### Scorecard Rendering
- `desloppify/app/output/scorecard.py` — `generate_scorecard(state, output_path)` renders a single PNG
- Layout: 780px wide landscape, warm earth tones (beige BG, sage/olive score colors, brown text)
- Three panels: Left (score + project name + version) | Vertical divider with diamond ornament | Right (2-column dimension table)
- Dimensions: max 20, elegance-collapsed, sorted with "File health" first
- Uses PIL/Pillow with deferred imports (optional dependency)
- Modular parts in `scorecard_parts/`: theme.py, draw.py, left_panel.py, ornaments.py, dimensions.py, dimension_policy.py, meta.py, projection.py

### State Architecture
- Per-language state files already exist: `.desloppify/state-{lang}.json`  (e.g., state-python.json, state-typescript.json)
- Each state has: overall_score, objective_score, strict_score, verified_strict_score, dimension_scores dict
- `state_path(args)` in helpers/state.py resolves state files
- `_sole_existing_lang_state_file()` finds single state files

### Scan Flow
- `cmd_scan(args)` in scan.py is the main handler
- Each scan targets ONE language only (no multi-lang per invocation)
- Badge emitted at end via `emit_scorecard_badge(args, config, state)` in scan_artifacts.py
- `get_badge_config(args, config)` resolves path from CLI → config → env → default "scorecard.png"
- Current badge path template has no `{lang}` substitution

### CLI
- `--badge-path PATH` flag on scan command
- `--no-badge` disables generation
- `--lang` is a global top-level flag
- No `--by-language` flag exists yet

## Feature Request (GitHub Issue #140)
Generate per-language scorecard images for mixed-language repos. When multiple `state-{lang}.json` files exist, produce `scorecard-{lang}.png` for each language alongside the aggregate `scorecard.png`.

## Design Constraints
1. The user asked us to think about beautiful/elegant information presentation
2. Per-language scorecards should be individual PNG files (not one giant combined image)
3. The aggregate scorecard should still work as before for single-language repos
4. The feature should work as a post-scan artifact — reading existing state files for all languages
5. Must not break existing single-language workflows
6. Must handle the case where only 1 language state exists (no per-language needed)

## Key Design Questions to Address

1. **Where to trigger multi-lang scorecard generation**: After a normal scan (emit per-lang badges alongside aggregate), or as a separate command/flag?

2. **How to produce the aggregate scorecard**: When multiple languages exist, how do we compute the "combined" score? Options:
   a. Simple average of per-language overall scores
   b. Weighted by file count (state has potentials/findings counts)
   c. Just show the current language's scorecard as before (no true aggregate)

3. **Per-language scorecard visual design**: How should a per-language scorecard differ from the current one?
   - Should show language name prominently (e.g., "PYTHON SCORE" instead of "DESLOPPIFY SCORE")
   - Same warm earth-tone palette
   - Same dimension table layout
   - Maybe a subtle language-specific accent?

4. **Badge path templating**: How to name per-language files?
   - `scorecard-python.png`, `scorecard-typescript.png`
   - Template: `--badge-path "scorecard-{lang}.png"` or auto-derive from base path

5. **CLI integration**: 
   - `--by-language` flag on scan to also emit per-lang badges
   - OR always emit per-lang when multiple state files exist
   - OR separate `desloppify scorecards` command

## My Recommendation

### Approach: `generate_scorecard` gains a `lang_label` parameter

The simplest, most elegant approach:

1. **New function**: `generate_multilang_scorecards(output_dir, project_root)` that:
   - Discovers all `state-{lang}.json` files
   - For each, calls `generate_scorecard(state, output_path, lang_label="Python")` 
   - Also generates aggregate if 2+ languages exist

2. **Modify `generate_scorecard`** to accept optional `lang_label` parameter:
   - When set, left panel shows "PYTHON SCORE" instead of "DESLOPPIFY SCORE"
   - When set, the language name appears in the project pill area
   - No other visual changes needed — the dimension table already adapts to whatever's in state

3. **Integration point**: `emit_scorecard_badge` in scan_artifacts.py gains per-lang emission alongside the current badge

4. **Badge path**: Auto-derive from base: if base is `scorecard.png`, per-lang is `scorecard-python.png`

Please produce a detailed implementation plan with specific file changes, function signatures, and the visual design for the per-language scorecard variation.

```
    43→def generate_scorecard(state: dict, output_path: str | Path) -> Path:
    44→    """Render a landscape scorecard PNG from scan state. Returns the output path."""
    45→    image_mod = importlib.import_module("PIL.Image")  # deferred: optional dependency
    46→    image_draw_mod = importlib.import_module("PIL.ImageDraw")  # deferred: optional dependency
    47→    scorecard_draw_mod = importlib.import_module("desloppify.app.output.scorecard_parts.draw")  # deferred: depends on PIL
    48→
    49→    output_path = Path(output_path)
    50→
    51→    main_score = get_overall_score(state) or 0
    52→    strict_score = get_strict_score(state) or 0
    53→
    54→    project_name = resolve_project_name(PROJECT_ROOT)
    55→    package_version = resolve_package_version(
    56→        PROJECT_ROOT,
    57→        version_getter=importlib_metadata.version,
    58→        package_not_found_error=importlib_metadata.PackageNotFoundError,
    59→    )
    60→
    61→    # Layout — landscape (wide), File health first
    62→    active_dims = prepare_scorecard_dimensions(state)
    63→    row_count = len(active_dims)
    64→    row_h = scale(20)
    65→    width = scale(780)
    66→    divider_x = scale(260)
    67→    frame_inset = scale(5)
    68→
    69→    cols = 2
    70→    rows_per_col = (row_count + cols - 1) // cols
    71→    table_content_h = scale(14) + scale(4) + scale(6) + rows_per_col * row_h
    72→    content_h = max(table_content_h + scale(28), scale(150))
    73→    height = scale(12) + content_h
    74→
    75→    img = image_mod.new("RGB", (width, height), BG)
    76→    draw = image_draw_mod.Draw(img)
    77→
    78→    # Double frame
    79→    draw.rectangle((0, 0, width - 1, height - 1), outline=FRAME, width=scale(2))
    80→    draw.rectangle(
    81→        (frame_inset, frame_inset, width - frame_inset - 1, height - frame_inset - 1),
    82→        outline=BORDER,
    83→        width=1,
    84→    )
    85→
    86→    content_top = frame_inset + scale(1)
    87→    content_bot = height - frame_inset - scale(1)
    88→    content_mid_y = (content_top + content_bot) // 2
    89→
    90→    # Left panel: title + score + project name
    91→    scorecard_draw_mod.draw_left_panel(
    92→        draw,
    93→        main_score,
    94→        strict_score,
    95→        project_name,
    96→        package_version,
    97→        lp_left=frame_inset + scale(11),
    98→        lp_right=divider_x - scale(11),
    99→        lp_top=content_top + scale(4),
   100→        lp_bot=content_bot - scale(4),
   101→    )
   102→
   103→    # Vertical divider with ornament
   104→    scorecard_draw_mod.draw_vert_rule_with_ornament(
   105→        draw,
   106→        divider_x,
   107→        content_top + scale(12),
   108→        content_bot - scale(12),
   109→        content_mid_y,
   110→        BORDER,
   111→        ACCENT,
   112→    )
   113→
   114→    # Right panel: dimension table
   115→    scorecard_draw_mod.draw_right_panel(
   116→        draw,
   117→        active_dims,
   118→        row_h,
   119→        table_x1=divider_x + scale(11),
   120→        table_x2=width - frame_inset - scale(11),
   121→        table_top=content_top + scale(4),
   122→        table_bot=content_bot - scale(4),
   123→    )
   124→    output_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   125→    img.save(str(output_path), "PNG", optimize=True)
   126→    return output_path
   127→
   128→
   129→def get_badge_config(args, config: dict | None = None) -> tuple[Path | None, bool]:
   130→    """Resolve badge output path and whether badge generation is disabled.
   131→
   132→    Returns (output_path, disabled). Checks CLI args, then config, then env vars.
   133→    """
   134→    cfg = config or {}
   135→    disabled = getattr(args, "no_badge", False)
   136→    if not disabled:
   137→        disabled = not cfg.get("generate_scorecard", True)
   138→    if not disabled:
   139→        disabled = os.environ.get("DESLOPPIFY_NO_BADGE", "").lower() in (
   140→            "1",
   141→            "true",
   142→            "yes",
   143→        )
   144→    if disabled:
   145→        return None, True
   146→
   147→    path_str = (
   148→        getattr(args, "badge_path", None)
   149→        or cfg.get("badge_path")
   150→        or os.environ.get("DESLOPPIFY_BADGE_PATH", "scorecard.png")
   151→    )
   152→    path = Path(path_str)
   153→    # On Windows, "/tmp/foo.png" is root-anchored but drive-relative.
   154→    # Treat any rooted path as user-intended absolute-like input.
   155→    is_root_anchored = bool(path.root)
   156→    if not path.is_absolute() and not is_root_anchored:
   157→        path = PROJECT_ROOT / path
   158→    return path, False
   159→
   160→
   161→def _scorecard_ignore_warning(state: dict) -> str | None:
   162→    """Return an ignore-suppression warning line for scorecard context."""
   163→    info = state.get("ignore_integrity", {}) if isinstance(state, dict) else {}
   164→    if not isinstance(info, dict):
   165→        return None
   166→    ignored = int(info.get("ignored", 0) or 0)
   167→    if ignored <= 0:
   168→        return None
   169→
   170→    suppressed_pct = float(info.get("suppressed_pct", 0.0) or 0.0)
   171→    rounded = round(suppressed_pct)
   172→    level = "high" if suppressed_pct >= 50 else "moderate"
   173→    return (
   174→        f"Ignore suppression is {rounded}% ({level}) "
   175→        f"across {ignored} findings."
   176→    )
   177→
   178→
   179→__all__ = [
   180→    "ACCENT",

   182→    "BG_ROW_ALT",
   183→    "BG_SCORE",
   184→    "BG_TABLE",
   185→    "BORDER",
```

```
    71→def _load_scorecard_helpers():
    72→    """Load scorecard helper callables lazily via importlib.
    73→
    74→    Deferred: scorecard depends on PIL (optional dependency).

    85→def emit_scorecard_badge(
    86→    args, config: dict[str, object], state: dict[str, object]
    87→) -> tuple[Path | None, OutputResult]:
    88→    """Generate a scorecard image badge and print usage hints."""
    89→    generate_scorecard, get_badge_config = _load_scorecard_helpers()
    90→    if not callable(generate_scorecard) or not callable(get_badge_config):
    91→        explicit_badge_request = bool(

   102→            )

   105→                status="error",
   106→                message="scorecard support not installed",
   107→                error_kind="scorecard_dependency_missing",

   116→        )

   125→

   136→

   139→    except ValueError:

   141→
   142→    readme_has_badge = False
   143→    for readme_name in ("README.md", "readme.md", "README.MD"):
   144→        readme_path = PROJECT_ROOT / readme_name
```

```
1→"""Visual style primitives shared by scorecard rendering modules."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import importlib
     6→import logging
     7→
     8→from desloppify.core.fallbacks import log_best_effort_failure
     9→
    10→# Render at 2x for retina/high-DPI crispness
    11→SCALE = 2
    12→
    13→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    14→
    15→
    16→def score_color(score: float, *, muted: bool = False) -> tuple[int, int, int]:
    17→    """Color-code a score: deep sage >= 90, mustard 70-90, dusty rose < 70.
    18→
    19→    muted=True returns a desaturated variant for secondary display (strict column).
    20→    """
    21→    if score >= 90:
    22→        base = (68, 120, 68)  # deep sage
    23→    elif score >= 70:
    24→        base = (120, 140, 72)  # olive green
    25→    else:
    26→        base = (145, 155, 80)  # yellow-green
    27→    if not muted:
    28→        return base
    29→    # Pastel orange shades for strict column
    30→    if score >= 90:
    31→        return (195, 160, 115)  # light sandy peach
    32→    if score >= 70:
    33→        return (200, 148, 100)  # warm apricot
    34→    return (195, 125, 95)  # soft coral
    35→
    36→
    37→def load_font(
    38→    size: int, *, serif: bool = False, bold: bool = False, mono: bool = False
    39→):
    40→    """Load a font with cross-platform fallback."""
    41→    image_font_mod = importlib.import_module("PIL.ImageFont")  # deferred: optional dependency
    42→
    43→    size = size * SCALE
    44→    candidates = []
    45→    if mono:
    46→        candidates = [
    47→            "/System/Library/Fonts/SFNSMono.ttf",
    48→            "/usr/share/fonts/truetype/dejavu/DejaVuSansMono.ttf",
    49→            "/usr/share/fonts/truetype/liberation/LiberationMono-Regular.ttf",
    50→        ]
    51→    elif serif and bold:
    52→        candidates = [
    53→            "/System/Library/Fonts/Supplemental/Georgia Bold.ttf",
    54→            "/System/Library/Fonts/NewYork.ttf",
    55→            "/usr/share/fonts/truetype/dejavu/DejaVuSerif-Bold.ttf",
    56→            "/usr/share/fonts/truetype/liberation/LiberationSerif-Bold.ttf",
    57→        ]
    58→    elif serif:
    59→        candidates = [
    60→            "/System/Library/Fonts/Supplemental/Georgia.ttf",
    61→            "/System/Library/Fonts/NewYork.ttf",
    62→            "/usr/share/fonts/truetype/dejavu/DejaVuSerif.ttf",
    63→            "/usr/share/fonts/truetype/liberation/LiberationSerif-Regular.ttf",
    64→        ]
    65→    elif bold:
    66→        candidates = [
    67→            "/System/Library/Fonts/SFCompact.ttf",
    68→            "/System/Library/Fonts/HelveticaNeue.ttc",
    69→            "/usr/share/fonts/truetype/dejavu/DejaVuSans-Bold.ttf",
    70→        ]
    71→    else:
    72→        candidates = [
    73→            "/System/Library/Fonts/SFCompact.ttf",
    74→            "/System/Library/Fonts/HelveticaNeue.ttc",
    75→            "/usr/share/fonts/truetype/dejavu/DejaVuSans.ttf",
    76→        ]
    77→    for path in candidates:
    78→        try:
    79→            return image_font_mod.truetype(path, size)
    80→        except OSError as exc:
    81→            log_best_effort_failure(
    82→                logger, f"load scorecard font candidate {path}", exc
    83→            )
    84→            continue
    85→    return image_font_mod.load_default()
    86→
    87→
    88→def scale(v: int | float) -> int:
    89→    """Scale a layout value."""
    90→    return int(v * SCALE)
    91→
    92→
    93→def fmt_score(score: float) -> str:
    94→    """Format score without .0 for whole numbers."""
    95→    if score == int(score):
    96→        return f"{int(score)}"
    97→    return f"{score:.1f}"
    98→
    99→
   100→# -- Palette used by all drawing functions --
   101→BG = (247, 240, 228)
   102→BG_SCORE = (240, 232, 217)
   103→BG_TABLE = (240, 233, 220)
   104→BG_ROW_ALT = (234, 226, 212)
   105→TEXT = (58, 48, 38)
   106→DIM = (138, 122, 102)
   107→BORDER = (192, 176, 152)
   108→ACCENT = (148, 112, 82)
   109→FRAME = (172, 152, 126)
   110→
```

```
1→"""Scorecard panel drawing helpers used by the renderer."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.app.output.scorecard_parts.left_panel import draw_left_panel
     6→from desloppify.app.output.scorecard_parts.ornaments import (
     7→    draw_ornament,
     8→    draw_rule_with_ornament,
     9→    draw_vert_rule_with_ornament,
    10→)
    11→from desloppify.app.output.scorecard_parts.theme import (
    12→    BG_ROW_ALT,
    13→    BG_TABLE,
    14→    BORDER,
    15→    TEXT,
    16→    fmt_score,
    17→    load_font,
    18→    scale,
    19→    score_color,
    20→)
    21→
    22→
    23→def draw_right_panel(
    24→    draw,
    25→    active_dims: list,
    26→    row_h: int,
    27→    table_x1: int,
    28→    table_x2: int,
    29→    table_top: int,
    30→    table_bot: int,
    31→) -> None:
    32→    """Draw the right panel: two separate dimension tables side by side."""
    33→    font_row = load_font(11, mono=True)
    34→    font_strict = load_font(9, mono=True)
    35→    row_count = len(active_dims)
    36→
    37→    cols = 2
    38→    rows_per_col = (row_count + cols - 1) // cols
    39→    table_width = table_x2 - table_x1
    40→    grid_gap = scale(8)
    41→    grid_width = (table_width - grid_gap) // cols
    42→
    43→    for col_index in range(cols):
    44→        grid_x1 = table_x1 + col_index * (grid_width + grid_gap)
    45→        grid_x2 = grid_x1 + grid_width
    46→        draw.rounded_rectangle(
    47→            (grid_x1, table_top, grid_x2, table_bot),
    48→            radius=scale(4),
    49→            fill=BG_TABLE,
    50→            outline=BORDER,
    51→            width=1,
    52→        )
    53→
    54→        name_col_width = scale(132)
    55→        value_col_gap = scale(4)
    56→        value_col_width = scale(34)
    57→        total_content_width = (
    58→            name_col_width
    59→            + value_col_gap
    60→            + value_col_width
    61→            + value_col_gap
    62→            + value_col_width
    63→        )
    64→        block_left = grid_x1 + (grid_width - total_content_width) // 2
    65→        name_col_x = block_left
    66→        health_col_x = name_col_x + name_col_width + value_col_gap
    67→        strict_col_x = health_col_x + value_col_width + value_col_gap + scale(4)
    68→
    69→        rows_this_col = min(rows_per_col, row_count - col_index * rows_per_col)
    70→        content_height = rows_this_col * row_h
    71→        content_top = (table_top + table_bot) // 2 - content_height // 2
    72→
    73→        sample_bbox = draw.textbbox((0, 0), "Xg", font=font_row)
    74→        row_text_height = sample_bbox[3] - sample_bbox[1]
    75→        row_text_offset = sample_bbox[1]
    76→        start_idx = col_index * rows_per_col
    77→
    78→        for row_index in range(rows_this_col):
    79→            dim_idx = start_idx + row_index
    80→            if dim_idx >= row_count:
    81→                break
    82→            name, data = active_dims[dim_idx]
    83→            band_top = content_top + row_index * row_h
    84→            band_bottom = band_top + row_h
    85→            if row_index % 2 == 1:
    86→                draw.rectangle(
    87→                    (grid_x1 + 1, band_top, grid_x2 - 1, band_bottom), fill=BG_ROW_ALT
    88→                )
    89→            text_y = band_top + (row_h - row_text_height) // 2 - row_text_offset + scale(1)
    90→            score = data.get("score", 100)
    91→            strict = data.get("strict", score)
    92→
    93→            max_name_width = name_col_width - scale(2)
    94→            while (
    95→                name
    96→                and draw.textlength(name + "\u2026", font=font_row) > max_name_width
    97→            ):
    98→                name = name[:-1]
    99→            if draw.textlength(name, font=font_row) > max_name_width:
   100→                name = name.rstrip() + "\u2026"
   101→
   102→            draw.text((name_col_x, text_y), name, fill=TEXT, font=font_row)
   103→            draw.text(
   104→                (health_col_x, text_y),
   105→                f"{fmt_score(score)}%",
   106→                fill=score_color(score),
   107→                font=font_row,
   108→            )
   109→
   110→            strict_text = f"{fmt_score(strict)}%"
   111→            strict_bbox = draw.textbbox((0, 0), strict_text, font=font_strict)
   112→            strict_text_height = strict_bbox[3] - strict_bbox[1]
   113→            strict_y = band_top + (row_h - strict_text_height) // 2 - strict_bbox[1]
   114→            draw.text(
   115→                (strict_col_x, strict_y),
   116→                strict_text,
   117→                fill=score_color(strict, muted=True),
   118→                font=font_strict,
   119→            )
   120→
   121→__all__ = [

   123→    "draw_right_panel",
   124→    "draw_ornament",
   125→    "draw_rule_with_ornament",

   127→]
```

```
1→"""Dimension projection policy for scorecard rendering."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.app.output.scorecard_parts.dimension_policy import (
     6→    _DEFAULT_ELEGANCE_COMPONENTS,
     7→    _ELEGANCE_COMPONENTS_BY_LANG,
     8→    _SUBJECTIVE_SCORECARD_ORDER_BY_LANG,
     9→    _SUBJECTIVE_SCORECARD_ORDER_DEFAULT,
    10→)
    11→from desloppify.app.output.scorecard_parts.dimension_policy import (
    12→    _SCORECARD_MAX_DIMENSIONS as SCORECARD_MAX_DIMENSIONS,
    13→)
    14→
    15→
    16→def resolve_scorecard_lang(state: dict) -> str | None:
    17→    """Best-effort current scan language key for scorecard display policy."""
    18→    history = state.get("scan_history")
    19→    if isinstance(history, list):
    20→        for entry in reversed(history):
    21→            if not isinstance(entry, dict):
    22→                continue
    23→            lang = entry.get("lang")
    24→            if isinstance(lang, str) and lang.strip():
    25→                return lang.strip().lower()
    26→
    27→    capabilities = state.get("lang_capabilities")
    28→    if isinstance(capabilities, dict) and len(capabilities) == 1:
    29→        only_lang = next(iter(capabilities.keys()))
    30→        if isinstance(only_lang, str) and only_lang.strip():
    31→            return only_lang.strip().lower()
    32→
    33→    findings = state.get("findings")
    34→    if isinstance(findings, dict):
    35→        counts: dict[str, int] = {}
    36→        for finding in findings.values():
    37→            if not isinstance(finding, dict):
    38→                continue
    39→            lang = finding.get("lang")
    40→            if isinstance(lang, str) and lang.strip():
    41→                key = lang.strip().lower()
    42→                counts[key] = counts.get(key, 0) + 1
    43→        if counts:
    44→            return max(counts, key=counts.get)
    45→    return None
    46→
    47→
    48→def is_unassessed_subjective_placeholder(data: dict) -> bool:
    49→    return (
    50→        "subjective_assessment" in data.get("detectors", {})
    51→        and data.get("score", 0) == 0
    52→        and data.get("issues", 0) == 0
    53→    )
    54→
    55→
    56→def collapse_elegance_dimensions(
    57→    active_dims: list[tuple[str, dict]],
    58→    *,
    59→    lang_key: str | None,
    60→) -> list[tuple[str, dict]]:
    61→    """Collapse High/Mid/Low elegance rows into one aggregate display row."""
    62→    component_names = set(
    63→        _ELEGANCE_COMPONENTS_BY_LANG.get(lang_key or "", _DEFAULT_ELEGANCE_COMPONENTS)
    64→    )
    65→    elegance_rows = [
    66→        (name, data) for name, data in active_dims if name in component_names
    67→    ]
    68→    if not elegance_rows:
    69→        return active_dims
    70→
    71→    remaining_rows = [
    72→        (name, data) for name, data in active_dims if name not in component_names
    73→    ]
    74→    count = len(elegance_rows)
    75→    score_avg = round(
    76→        sum(float(data.get("score", 100.0)) for _, data in elegance_rows) / count, 1
    77→    )
    78→    strict_avg = round(
    79→        sum(
    80→            float(data.get("strict", data.get("score", 100.0)))
    81→            for _, data in elegance_rows
    82→        )
    83→        / count,
    84→        1,
    85→    )
    86→    checks_total = sum(int(data.get("checks", 0)) for _, data in elegance_rows)
    87→    issues_total = sum(int(data.get("issues", 0)) for _, data in elegance_rows)
    88→    tier = max(int(data.get("tier", 4)) for _, data in elegance_rows)
    89→    placeholder_flags = [
    90→        bool(
    91→            data.get("detectors", {})
    92→            .get("subjective_assessment", {})
    93→            .get("placeholder")
    94→        )
    95→        for _, data in elegance_rows
    96→    ]
    97→
    98→    label = "Elegance"
    99→    if any(name.lower() == label.lower() for name, _ in remaining_rows):
   100→        label = "Elegance (combined)"
   101→
   102→    pass_rate = round(score_avg / 100.0, 4)
   103→    return [
   104→        *remaining_rows,
   105→        (
   106→            label,
   107→            {
   108→                "score": score_avg,
   109→                "strict": strict_avg,
   110→                "checks": checks_total,
   111→                "issues": issues_total,
   112→                "tier": tier,
   113→                "detectors": {
   114→                    "subjective_assessment": {
   115→                        "potential": checks_total,
   116→                        "pass_rate": pass_rate,
   117→                        "issues": issues_total,
   118→                        "weighted_failures": round(checks_total * (1 - pass_rate), 4),
   119→                        "components": [name for name, _ in elegance_rows],
   120→                        "placeholder": any(placeholder_flags),
   121→                    }
   122→                },
   123→            },
   124→        ),
   125→    ]
   126→
   127→
   128→def limit_scorecard_dimensions(
   129→    active_dims: list[tuple[str, dict]],
   130→    *,
   131→    lang_key: str | None,
   132→    max_rows: int = SCORECARD_MAX_DIMENSIONS,
   133→) -> list[tuple[str, dict]]:
   134→    """Limit scorecard rows with language-specific subjective priority."""
   135→    if len(active_dims) <= max_rows:
   136→        return active_dims
   137→
   138→    mechanical = [
   139→        (name, data)
   140→        for name, data in active_dims
   141→        if "subjective_assessment" not in data.get("detectors", {})
   142→    ]
   143→    subjective = [
   144→        (name, data)
   145→        for name, data in active_dims
   146→        if "subjective_assessment" in data.get("detectors", {})
   147→    ]
   148→    if len(mechanical) >= max_rows:
   149→        return mechanical[:max_rows]
   150→
   151→    budget = max_rows - len(mechanical)
   152→    preferred_order = _SUBJECTIVE_SCORECARD_ORDER_BY_LANG.get(
   153→        lang_key or "",
   154→        _SUBJECTIVE_SCORECARD_ORDER_DEFAULT,
   155→    )
   156→
   157→    remaining = {name: (name, data) for name, data in subjective}
   158→    selected: list[tuple[str, dict]] = []
   159→    for name in preferred_order:
   160→        row = remaining.pop(name, None)
   161→        if row is None:
   162→            continue
   163→        selected.append(row)
   164→        if len(selected) >= budget:
   165→            break
   166→
   167→    if len(selected) < budget and remaining:
   168→        extras = sorted(
   169→            remaining.values(),
   170→            key=lambda item: (
   171→                float(item[1].get("strict", item[1].get("score", 100.0))),
   172→                item[0],
   173→            ),
   174→        )
   175→        selected.extend(extras[: budget - len(selected)])
   176→
   177→    return [*mechanical, *selected]
   178→
   179→
   180→def prepare_scorecard_dimensions(state: dict) -> list[tuple[str, dict]]:
   181→    """Prepare scorecard rows (active, elegance-collapsed, capped).
   182→
   183→    Dimensions are derived dynamically from what the scoring engine produced
   184→    in ``dimension_scores`` — no per-language hard-coded dimension list.
   185→    Unassessed subjective placeholders (score 0, no issues) are excluded so
   186→    the scorecard only shows dimensions that have real data.
   187→    """
   188→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {})
   189→    if not isinstance(dim_scores, dict):
   190→        return []
   191→
   192→    all_dims = [
   193→        (name, data) for name, data in dim_scores.items() if isinstance(data, dict)
   194→    ]
   195→
   196→    [REDACTED](state)
   197→    all_dims = collapse_elegance_dimensions(all_dims, lang_key=lang_key)
   198→
   199→    # Show dimensions with real data; skip unassessed subjective placeholders.
   200→    active_dims = [
   201→        (name, data)
   202→        for name, data in all_dims
   203→        if data.get("checks", 0) > 0 and not is_unassessed_subjective_placeholder(data)
   204→    ]
   205→    active_dims = limit_scorecard_dimensions(active_dims, lang_key=lang_key)
   206→    active_dims.sort(key=lambda x: (0 if x[0] == "File health" else 1, x[0]))
   207→    return active_dims
   208→
   209→
   210→__all__ = [
   211→    "SCORECARD_MAX_DIMENSIONS",
   212→    "collapse_elegance_dimensions",
   213→    "prepare_scorecard_dimensions",
   214→    "limit_scorecard_dimensions",
   215→    "resolve_scorecard_lang",
   216→]
   217→
```

```
1→"""Ornamental line primitives for the scorecard renderer."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.app.output.scorecard_parts.theme import scale
     6→
     7→
     8→def draw_ornament(draw, cx: int, cy: int, size: int, fill) -> None:
     9→    """Draw a small diamond ornament centered at (cx, cy)."""
    10→    draw.polygon(
    11→        [
    12→            (cx, cy - size),
    13→            (cx + size, cy),
    14→            (cx, cy + size),
    15→            (cx - size, cy),
    16→        ],
    17→        fill=fill,
    18→    )
    19→
    20→
    21→def draw_rule_with_ornament(
    22→    draw,
    23→    y: int,
    24→    x1: int,
    25→    x2: int,
    26→    cx: int,
    27→    line_fill,
    28→    ornament_fill,
    29→) -> None:
    30→    """Draw a horizontal rule with a diamond ornament in the center."""
    31→    gap = scale(8)
    32→    draw.rectangle((x1, y, cx - gap, y + 1), fill=line_fill)
    33→    draw.rectangle((cx + gap, y, x2, y + 1), fill=line_fill)
    34→    draw_ornament(draw, cx, y, scale(3), ornament_fill)
    35→
    36→
    37→def draw_vert_rule_with_ornament(
    38→    draw,
    39→    x: int,
    40→    y1: int,
    41→    y2: int,
    42→    cy: int,
    43→    line_fill,
    44→    ornament_fill,
    45→) -> None:
    46→    """Draw a vertical rule with a diamond ornament in the center."""
    47→    gap = scale(8)
    48→    draw.rectangle((x, y1, x + 1, cy - gap), fill=line_fill)
    49→    draw.rectangle((x, cy + gap, x + 1, y2), fill=line_fill)
    50→    draw_ornament(draw, x, cy, scale(3), ornament_fill)
    51→
    52→
    53→__all__ = [
    54→    "draw_ornament",
    55→    "draw_rule_with_ornament",
    56→    "draw_vert_rule_with_ornament",
    57→]
    58→
```
