```
1→"""Metadata helpers for scorecard rendering."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import logging
     6→import re
     7→import subprocess
     8→from collections.abc import Callable
     9→from pathlib import Path
    10→
    11→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    12→
    13→
    14→def resolve_project_name(project_root: Path) -> str:
    15→    """Resolve owner/repo display name from GitHub CLI, git remote, or folder."""
    16→    try:
    17→        name = subprocess.check_output(
    18→            ["gh", "repo", "view", "--json", "nameWithOwner", "-q", ".nameWithOwner"],
    19→            cwd=str(project_root),
    20→            stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL,
    21→            text=True,
    22→            timeout=5,
    23→        ).strip()
    24→        if "/" in name:
    25→            return name
    26→    except (
    27→        subprocess.CalledProcessError,
    28→        FileNotFoundError,
    29→        subprocess.TimeoutExpired,
    30→    ) as exc:
    31→        logger.debug("gh repo view failed, falling back to git remote: %s", exc)
    32→
    33→    try:
    34→        url = subprocess.check_output(
    35→            ["git", "config", "--get", "remote.origin.url"],
    36→            cwd=str(project_root),
    37→            stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL,
    38→            text=True,
    39→            timeout=5,
    40→        ).strip()
    41→        if url.startswith("git@") and ":" in url:
    42→            path = url.split(":")[-1]
    43→        else:
    44→            path = "/".join(url.split("/")[-2:])
    45→        return path.removesuffix(".git")
    46→    except (
    47→        subprocess.CalledProcessError,
    48→        FileNotFoundError,
    49→        IndexError,
    50→        subprocess.TimeoutExpired,
    51→    ):
    52→        return project_root.name
    53→
    54→
    55→def resolve_package_version(
    56→    project_root: Path,
    57→    *,
    58→    version_getter: Callable[[str], str],
    59→    package_not_found_error: type[Exception],
    60→) -> str:
    61→    """Resolve package version from installed metadata or local pyproject."""
    62→    try:
    63→        return version_getter("desloppify")
    64→    except package_not_found_error as exc:
    65→        logger.debug("Package metadata lookup failed, trying pyproject.toml: %s", exc)
    66→
    67→    pyproject_path = project_root / "pyproject.toml"
    68→    try:
    69→        text = pyproject_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    70→        match = re.search(r'^\s*version\s*=\s*"([^"]+)"\s*$', text, re.MULTILINE)
    71→        if match:
    72→            return match.group(1)
    73→    except OSError as exc:
    74→        logger.debug("Failed to read pyproject.toml for version: %s", exc)
    75→
    76→    return "unknown"
    77→
```

```
1→"""Canonical scorecard projection helpers shared by command/reporting surfaces."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import re
     6→
     7→from desloppify.engine.planning.dimension_rows import (
     8→    scorecard_dimension_rows,
     9→)
    10→from desloppify.scoring import DISPLAY_NAMES
    11→
    12→_COMPONENT_TO_DIMENSION: dict[str, str] = {
    13→    "abstraction leverage": "abstraction_fitness",
    14→    "indirection cost": "abstraction_fitness",
    15→    "interface honesty": "abstraction_fitness",
    16→}
    17→
    18→
    19→def dimension_cli_key(dimension_name: str) -> str:
    20→    """Map a display name to a review --dimensions CLI key."""
    21→    cleaned = dimension_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
    22→    lowered = cleaned.lower()
    23→    if lowered in _COMPONENT_TO_DIMENSION:
    24→        return _COMPONENT_TO_DIMENSION[lowered]
    25→    for key, display in DISPLAY_NAMES.items():
    26→        if display.lower() == lowered:
    27→            return key
    28→    return re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9]+", "_", lowered).strip("_")
    29→
    30→
    31→def scorecard_dimension_cli_keys(name: str, data: dict) -> list[str]:
    32→    """Map scorecard subjective display names to review --dimensions keys."""
    33→    if name in ("Elegance", "Elegance (combined)"):
    34→        components = (
    35→            data.get("detectors", {})
    36→            .get("subjective_assessment", {})
    37→            .get("components", [])
    38→        )
    39→        if isinstance(components, list) and components:
    40→            keys = [dimension_cli_key(component) for component in components]
    41→        else:
    42→            keys = [
    43→                "high_level_elegance",
    44→                "mid_level_elegance",
    45→                "low_level_elegance",
    46→            ]
    47→    elif name in ("Abstraction fit", "Abstraction fit (combined)"):
    48→        components = (
    49→            data.get("detectors", {})
    50→            .get("subjective_assessment", {})
    51→            .get("components", [])
    52→        )
    53→        if isinstance(components, list) and components:
    54→            keys = [dimension_cli_key(component) for component in components]
    55→        else:
    56→            keys = ["abstraction_fitness"]
    57→    else:
    58→        keys = [dimension_cli_key(name)]
    59→
    60→    seen: set[str] = set()
    61→    ordered: list[str] = []
    62→    for key in keys:
    63→        if not key or key in seen:
    64→            continue
    65→        ordered.append(key)
    66→        seen.add(key)
    67→    return ordered
    68→
    69→
    70→def scorecard_subjective_entries(
    71→    state: dict,
    72→    *,
    73→    dim_scores: dict | None = None,
    74→) -> list[dict]:
    75→    """Return scorecard-subjective entries with score/strict/placeholder metadata."""
    76→    rows = scorecard_dimension_rows(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
    77→    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments") or {}
    78→    subjective_display_names = {display.lower() for display in DISPLAY_NAMES.values()}
    79→    subjective_display_names.update({"elegance", "elegance (combined)"})
    80→
    81→    entries: list[dict] = []
    82→    for name, data in rows:
    83→        detectors = data.get("detectors", {})
    84→        lowered_name = str(name).strip().lower()
    85→        is_subjective = (
    86→            "subjective_assessment" in detectors
    87→            or lowered_name in subjective_display_names
    88→        )
    89→        if not is_subjective:
    90→            continue
    91→        score = float(data.get("score", 0.0))
    92→        strict = float(data.get("strict", score))
    93→        assessment_meta = detectors.get("subjective_assessment", {})
    94→        placeholder = bool(
    95→            assessment_meta.get("placeholder")
    96→            or (
    97→                data.get("score", 0) == 0
    98→                and data.get("issues", 0) == 0
    99→                and data.get("checks", 0) == 0
   100→            )
   101→        )
   102→        [REDACTED]("dimension_key", "")
   103→        stale = bool(
   104→            dim_key
   105→            and isinstance(assessments.get(dim_key), dict)
   106→            and assessments[dim_key].get("needs_review_refresh")
   107→        )
   108→        entries.append(
   109→            {
   110→                "name": name,
   111→                "score": score,
   112→                "strict": strict,
   113→                "checks": int(data.get("checks", 0) or 0),
   114→                "issues": int(data.get("issues", 0) or 0),
   115→                "tier": int(data.get("tier", 4) or 4),
   116→                "placeholder": placeholder,
   117→                "stale": stale,
   118→                "dimension_key": dim_key,
   119→                "cli_keys": scorecard_dimension_cli_keys(name, data),
   120→            }
   121→        )
   122→    return entries
   123→
   124→
   125→def scorecard_dimensions_payload(
   126→    state: dict,
   127→    *,
   128→    dim_scores: dict | None = None,
   129→) -> list[dict]:
   130→    """Serialize scorecard rows for JSON/query outputs."""
   131→    subjective_by_name = {
   132→        entry["name"]: entry
   133→        for entry in scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
   134→    }
   135→    payload: list[dict] = []
   136→    for name, data in scorecard_dimension_rows(state, dim_scores=dim_scores):
   137→        score = float(data.get("score", 0.0))
   138→        strict = float(data.get("strict", score))
   139→        subjective = name in subjective_by_name
   140→        entry = {
   141→            "name": name,
   142→            "score": score,
   143→            "strict": strict,
   144→            "checks": int(data.get("checks", 0) or 0),
   145→            "issues": int(data.get("issues", 0) or 0),
   146→            "tier": int(
   147→                data.get("tier", 4 if subjective else 3) or (4 if subjective else 3)
   148→            ),
   149→            "subjective": subjective,
   150→        }
   151→        if subjective:
   152→            sub = subjective_by_name[name]
   153→            entry["placeholder"] = bool(sub.get("placeholder"))
   154→            entry["cli_keys"] = list(sub.get("cli_keys", []))
   155→        payload.append(entry)
   156→    return payload
   157→
```

```
1→"""State-path and scan-gating helpers for command modules."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from pathlib import Path
     6→
     7→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.lang import auto_detect_lang_name
     8→from desloppify.core._internal.text_utils import PROJECT_ROOT
     9→from desloppify.utils import colorize
    10→
    11→
    12→def _sole_existing_lang_state_file() -> Path | None:
    13→    """Return the only existing language-specific state file, if unambiguous."""
    14→    state_dir = PROJECT_ROOT / ".desloppify"
    15→    if not state_dir.exists():
    16→        return None
    17→    candidates = sorted(path for path in state_dir.glob("state-*.json") if path.is_file())
    18→    if len(candidates) == 1:
    19→        return candidates[0]
    20→    return None
    21→
    22→
    23→def _allow_lang_state_fallback(args) -> bool:
    24→    """Whether command can safely fallback to the sole existing lang state file."""
    25→    # Scan should always honor detected/explicit language mapping to avoid cross-lang merges.
    26→    return getattr(args, "command", None) != "scan"
    27→
    28→
    29→def state_path(args) -> Path | None:
    30→    """Get state file path from args, or None for default."""
    31→    path_arg = getattr(args, "state", None)
    32→    if path_arg:
    33→        return Path(path_arg)
    34→    lang_name = getattr(args, "lang", None)
    35→    if not lang_name:
    36→        lang_name = auto_detect_lang_name(args)
    37→    if lang_name:
    38→        resolved = PROJECT_ROOT / ".desloppify" / f"state-{lang_name}.json"
    39→        if resolved.exists() or not _allow_lang_state_fallback(args):
    40→            return resolved
    41→        fallback = _sole_existing_lang_state_file()
    42→        if fallback is not None:
    43→            return fallback
    44→        return resolved
    45→
    46→    if _allow_lang_state_fallback(args):
    47→        fallback = _sole_existing_lang_state_file()
    48→        if fallback is not None:
    49→            return fallback
    50→    return None
    51→
    52→
    53→def require_completed_scan(state: dict) -> bool:
    54→    """Return True when the state contains at least one completed scan."""
    55→    has_completed_scan = bool(state.get("last_scan"))
    56→    if not has_completed_scan:
    57→        print(colorize("No scans yet. Run: desloppify scan", "yellow"))
    58→    return has_completed_scan
    59→
```

```
1→"""State persistence and migration routines."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import json
     6→import logging
     7→import shutil
     8→import sys
     9→from pathlib import Path
    10→
    11→__all__ = [
    12→    "load_state",
    13→    "save_state",
    14→]
    15→
    16→from desloppify.core._internal.text_utils import is_numeric
    17→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import (
    18→    CURRENT_VERSION,
    19→    STATE_FILE,
    20→    StateModel,
    21→    empty_state,
    22→    ensure_state_defaults,
    23→    json_default,
    24→    validate_state_invariants,
    25→)
    26→from desloppify.engine._state.scoring import _recompute_stats
    27→from desloppify.file_discovery import safe_write_text
    28→
    29→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    30→
    31→
    32→def _load_json(path: Path) -> dict[str, object]:
    33→    data = json.loads(path.read_text())
    34→    if not isinstance(data, dict):
    35→        raise ValueError("state file root must be a JSON object")
    36→    return data
    37→
    38→
    39→def _normalize_loaded_state(data: dict[str, object]) -> dict[str, object]:
    40→    normalized = ensure_state_defaults(data)
    41→    validate_state_invariants(normalized)
    42→    return normalized
    43→
    44→
    45→def load_state(path: Path | None = None) -> StateModel:
    46→    """Load state from disk, or return empty state on missing/corruption."""
    47→    state_path = path or STATE_FILE
    48→    if not state_path.exists():
    49→        return empty_state()
    50→
    51→    try:
    52→        data = _load_json(state_path)
    53→    except (json.JSONDecodeError, UnicodeDecodeError, OSError, ValueError) as ex:
    54→        backup = state_path.with_suffix(".json.bak")
    55→        if backup.exists():
    56→            try:
    57→                backup_data = _load_json(backup)
    58→                print(
    59→                    f"  ⚠ State file corrupted ({ex}), loaded from backup.",
    60→                    file=sys.stderr,
    61→                )
    62→                return _normalize_loaded_state(backup_data)
    63→            except (
    64→                json.JSONDecodeError,
    65→                UnicodeDecodeError,
    66→                OSError,
    67→                ValueError,
    68→            ) as backup_ex:
    69→                logger.debug("Backup state load failed from %s: %s", backup, backup_ex)
    70→
    71→        print(f"  ⚠ State file corrupted ({ex}). Starting fresh.", file=sys.stderr)
    72→        rename_failed = False
    73→        try:
    74→            state_path.rename(state_path.with_suffix(".json.corrupted"))
    75→        except OSError as rename_ex:
    76→            rename_failed = True
    77→            logger.debug(
    78→                "Failed to rename corrupted state file %s: %s", state_path, rename_ex
    79→            )
    80→        if rename_failed:
    81→            logger.debug(
    82→                "Corrupted state file retained at original path: %s", state_path
    83→            )
    84→        return empty_state()
    85→
    86→    version = data.get("version", 1)
    87→    if version > CURRENT_VERSION:
    88→        print(
    89→            "  ⚠ State file version "
    90→            f"{version} is newer than supported ({CURRENT_VERSION}). "
    91→            "Some features may not work correctly.",
    92→            file=sys.stderr,
    93→        )
    94→
    95→    return _normalize_loaded_state(data)
    96→
    97→
    98→def _coerce_integrity_target(value: object) -> float | None:
    99→    if not is_numeric(value):
   100→        return None
   101→    return max(0.0, min(100.0, float(value)))
   102→
   103→
   104→def _resolve_integrity_target(
   105→    state: StateModel,
   106→    explicit_target: float | None,
   107→) -> float | None:
   108→    target = _coerce_integrity_target(explicit_target)
   109→    if target is not None:
   110→        return target
   111→
   112→    integrity = state.get("subjective_integrity")
   113→    if not isinstance(integrity, dict):
   114→        return None
   115→    return _coerce_integrity_target(integrity.get("target_score"))
   116→
   117→
   118→def save_state(
   119→    state: StateModel,
   120→    path: Path | None = None,
   121→    *,
   122→    subjective_integrity_target: float | None = None,
   123→) -> None:
   124→    """Recompute stats/score and save to disk atomically."""
   125→    ensure_state_defaults(state)
   126→    _recompute_stats(
   127→        state,
   128→        scan_path=state.get("scan_path"),
   129→        subjective_integrity_target=_resolve_integrity_target(
   130→            state,
   131→            subjective_integrity_target,
   132→        ),
   133→    )
   134→    validate_state_invariants(state)
   135→
   136→    state_path = path or STATE_FILE
   137→    state_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   138→
   139→    content = json.dumps(state, indent=2, default=json_default) + "\n"
   140→
   141→    if state_path.exists():
   142→        backup = state_path.with_suffix(".json.bak")
   143→        try:
   144→            shutil.copy2(str(state_path), str(backup))
   145→        except OSError as backup_ex:
   146→            logger.debug(
   147→                "Failed to create state backup %s: %s",
   148→                state_path.with_suffix(".json.bak"),
   149→                backup_ex,
   150→            )
   151→
   152→    try:
   153→        safe_write_text(state_path, content)
   154→    except OSError as ex:
   155→        print(f"  Warning: Could not save state: {ex}", file=sys.stderr)
   156→        raise
   157→
```

```
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.score import target_strict_score_from_config

    51→from desloppify.core.query import write_query
    52→from desloppify.utils import colorize
    53→
    54→
    55→def _print_scan_header(lang_label: str) -> None:
    56→    """Print the scan header line."""
    57→    print(colorize(f"\nDesloppify Scan{lang_label}\n", "bold"))
    58→
    59→
    60→def _print_scan_complete_banner() -> None:
    61→    """Print scan completion hint banner."""
    62→    lines = [
    63→        colorize("  Scan complete", "bold"),
    64→        colorize("  " + "─" * 50, "dim"),
    65→    ]
    66→    print("\n".join(lines))
    67→
    68→
    69→def _show_scan_visibility(noise, effective_include_slow: bool) -> None:
    70→    """Print fast-scan and noise budget visibility hints."""
    71→    if not effective_include_slow:
    72→        print(colorize("  * Fast scan — slow phases (duplicates) skipped", "yellow"))
    73→    if noise.budget_warning:
    74→        print(colorize(f"  * {noise.budget_warning}", "yellow"))
    75→    if noise.hidden_total:
    76→        global_label = (
    77→            f", {noise.global_noise_budget} global"
    78→            if noise.global_noise_budget > 0
    79→            else ""
    80→        )
    81→        print(
    82→            colorize(
    83→                f"  * Noise budget: {noise.noise_budget}/detector{global_label} "
    84→                f"({noise.hidden_total} findings hidden in show output: "
    85→                f"{_format_hidden_by_detector(noise.hidden_by_detector)})",
    86→                "dim",
    87→            )
    88→        )
    89→
    90→
    91→def _show_coverage_preflight(runtime) -> None:
    92→    """Print preflight warnings when scan coverage confidence is reduced."""
    93→    warnings = getattr(runtime, "coverage_warnings", []) or []
    94→    if not isinstance(warnings, list) or not warnings:
    95→        return
    96→
    97→    for entry in warnings:
    98→        if not isinstance(entry, dict):
    99→            continue
   100→        summary = str(entry.get("summary", "")).strip()
   101→        impact = str(entry.get("impact", "")).strip()
   102→        remediation = str(entry.get("remediation", "")).strip()
   103→        detector = str(entry.get("detector", "")).strip() or "detector"
   104→
   105→        headline = summary or f"Coverage reduced for `{detector}`."
   106→        print(colorize(f"  * Coverage preflight: {headline}", "yellow"))
   107→        if impact:
   108→            print(colorize(f"    Repercussion: {impact}", "dim"))
   109→        if remediation:
   110→            print(colorize(f"    Fix: {remediation}", "dim"))
   111→
   112→
   113→def cmd_scan(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   114→    """Run all detectors, update persistent state, show diff."""
   115→    runtime = prepare_scan_runtime(args)
   116→    orchestrator = ScanOrchestrator(
   117→        runtime,
   118→        run_scan_generation_fn=run_scan_generation,
   119→        merge_scan_results_fn=merge_scan_results,
   120→        resolve_noise_snapshot_fn=resolve_noise_snapshot,
   121→        persist_reminder_history_fn=persist_reminder_history,
   122→    )
   123→    _print_scan_header(runtime.lang_label)
   124→    if runtime.reset_subjective_count > 0:
   125→        print(
   126→            colorize(
   127→                "  * Subjective reset "
   128→                f"{runtime.reset_subjective_count} subjective dimensions to 0",
   129→                "yellow",
   130→            )
   131→        )
   132→    if runtime.expired_manual_override_count > 0:
   133→        print(
   134→            colorize(
   135→                "  * Expired provisional manual-override assessments: "
   136→                f"{runtime.expired_manual_override_count} dimension(s) reset to 0. "
   137→                "Use trusted `review --run-batches --runner codex --parallel --scan-after-import` to replace them.",
   138→                "yellow",
   139→            )
   140→        )
   141→    _show_coverage_preflight(runtime)
   142→
   143→    findings, potentials, codebase_metrics = orchestrator.generate()
   144→    merge = orchestrator.merge(findings, potentials, codebase_metrics)
   145→    _print_scan_complete_banner()
   146→
   147→    noise = orchestrator.noise_snapshot()
   148→
   149→    show_diff_summary(merge.diff)
   150→    show_score_delta(
   151→        runtime.state,
   152→        merge.prev_overall,
   153→        merge.prev_objective,
   154→        merge.prev_strict,
   155→        merge.prev_verified,
   156→    )
   157→    _show_scan_visibility(noise, runtime.effective_include_slow)
   158→    show_scorecard_subjective_measures(runtime.state)
   159→    show_score_model_breakdown(runtime.state)
   160→
   161→    target_value = target_strict_score_from_config(runtime.config, fallback=95.0)
   162→
   163→    new_dim_scores = runtime.state.get("dimension_scores", {})
   164→    if new_dim_scores and merge.prev_dim_scores:
   165→        show_dimension_deltas(merge.prev_dim_scores, new_dim_scores)
   166→    if new_dim_scores:
   167→        show_low_dimension_hints(new_dim_scores)
   168→        show_subjective_paths_section(
   169→            runtime.state,
   170→            new_dim_scores,
   171→            threshold=target_value,
   172→            target_strict_score=target_value,
   173→        )
   174→
   175→    show_score_integrity(runtime.state, merge.diff)
   176→    show_concern_count(runtime.state, lang_name=runtime.lang.name if runtime.lang else None)
   177→    warnings, narrative = show_post_scan_analysis(
   178→        merge.diff,
   179→        runtime.state,
   180→        runtime.lang,
   181→        target_strict_score=target_value,
   182→    )
   183→    orchestrator.persist_reminders(narrative)
   184→
   185→    write_query(
   186→        build_scan_query_payload(
   187→            runtime.state,
   188→            runtime.config,
   189→            runtime.profile,
   190→            merge.diff,
   191→            warnings,
   192→            narrative,
   193→            merge,
   194→            noise,
   195→        ),
   196→        query_file=QUERY_FILE,
   197→    )
   198→
   199→    badge_emit = emit_scorecard_badge(args, runtime.config, runtime.state)
   200→    if isinstance(badge_emit, tuple):
   201→        badge_path, _badge_result = badge_emit
   202→    else:  # Backward-compatible shape for monkeypatched tests.
   203→        badge_path = badge_emit
   204→    _print_llm_summary(runtime.state, badge_path, narrative, merge.diff)
   205→    auto_update_skill()
   206→
   207→
   208→__all__ = [
   209→    "cmd_scan",
   210→]
   211→
```

```
   343→
   344→def _mk_dim(
   345→    score: float,
   346→    *,
   347→    strict: float | None = None,
   348→    checks: int = 10,
   349→    issues: int = 0,
   350→    subjective: bool = True,
   351→) -> dict:
   352→    detectors = (
   353→        {"subjective_assessment": {"issues": issues}}
   354→        if subjective
   355→        else {"structural": {}}
   356→    )
   357→    return {
   358→        "score": score,
   359→        "strict": score if strict is None else strict,
   360→        "checks": checks,
   361→        "issues": issues,
   362→        "tier": 4 if subjective else 3,
   363→        "detectors": detectors,
   364→    }
   365→
   366→
   367→class TestScorecardDimensionPolicy:
   368→    def test_resolve_scorecard_lang_prefers_latest_scan_history(self):
   369→        state = {
   370→            "scan_history": [
   371→                {"lang": "typescript"},
   372→                {"lang": "python"},
   373→            ]
   374→        }
   375→        assert resolve_scorecard_lang(state) == "python"
   376→
   377→    def test_collapse_elegance_dimensions_averages_components(self):
   378→        dims = [
   379→            ("Naming quality", _mk_dim(90, strict=90)),
   380→            ("High elegance", _mk_dim(80, strict=70, issues=1)),
   381→            ("Mid elegance", _mk_dim(60, strict=50, issues=2)),
   382→            ("Low elegance", _mk_dim(100, strict=90, issues=0)),
   383→        ]
   384→        collapsed = collapse_elegance_dimensions(dims, lang_key="python")
   385→        names = [name for name, _ in collapsed]
   386→        assert "High elegance" not in names
   387→        assert "Mid elegance" not in names
   388→        assert "Low elegance" not in names
   389→        assert "Elegance" in names
   390→        combined = dict(collapsed)["Elegance"]
   391→        assert combined["score"] == 80.0
   392→        assert combined["strict"] == 70.0
   393→        assert combined["issues"] == 3
   394→
   395→    def test_limit_scorecard_dimensions_python_drops_highest_score_extra(self):
   396→        """When budget forces truncation, extras are sorted ascending by score
   397→        so the lowest-score (worst) dimensions are shown first."""
   398→        active_dims = [
   399→            ("File health", _mk_dim(99, subjective=False)),
   400→            ("Code quality", _mk_dim(95, subjective=False)),
   401→            ("Duplication", _mk_dim(98, subjective=False)),
   402→            ("Security", _mk_dim(97, subjective=False)),
   403→            ("Test health", _mk_dim(96, subjective=False)),
   404→            ("Naming quality", _mk_dim(80)),
   405→            ("Error consistency", _mk_dim(81)),
   406→            ("Abstraction fit", _mk_dim(82)),
   407→            ("Logic clarity", _mk_dim(83)),
   408→            ("AI generated debt", _mk_dim(84)),
   409→            ("Type safety", _mk_dim(85)),
   410→            ("Contracts", _mk_dim(86)),
   411→            ("Elegance", _mk_dim(87)),
   412→        ]
   413→        limited = limit_scorecard_dimensions(
   414→            active_dims, lang_key="python", max_rows=12
   415→        )
   416→        names = {name for name, _ in limited}
   417→        assert len(limited) == 12
   418→        # Contracts has the highest score among non-preferred extras, so it's
   419→        # dropped first when budget is exhausted.
   420→        assert "Contracts" not in names
   421→        assert "Type safety" in names
   422→
```

## Per-Language Scorecards -- Implementation Plan

### 1. Overview of Current Architecture

The scorecard system has a clean, modular structure:

- **`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/output/scorecard.py`** -- Top-level `generate_scorecard(state, output_path)` orchestrator. Imports PIL lazily. Computes layout geometry, then delegates to `scorecard_parts/draw.py` for the three visual regions (left panel, vertical divider, right panel dimension table).

- **`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/output/scorecard_parts/left_panel.py`** -- Draws the left panel. The title "DESLOPPIFY SCORE" is hardcoded at line 160 inside `_left_panel_measurements()`. The project name appears in a rounded "pill" at the bottom. The version string sits above the title.

- **`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/output/scorecard_parts/theme.py`** -- Palette constants (BG, TEXT, ACCENT, etc.), `score_color()`, `load_font()`, `scale()`, `fmt_score()`. The warm earth-tone palette: beige BG `(247,240,228)`, sage score colors, brown text `(58,48,38)`, border `(192,176,152)`, accent `(148,112,82)`.

- **`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/output/scorecard_parts/draw.py`** -- `draw_right_panel()` renders the 2-column dimension table. `draw_left_panel` is re-exported from left_panel.py.

- **`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/output/scorecard_parts/dimensions.py`** -- `prepare_scorecard_dimensions(state)` extracts, collapses elegance rows, limits to 20 rows, sorts with "File health" first.

- **`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/scan/scan_artifacts.py`** -- `emit_scorecard_badge(args, config, state)` is called at line 199 of scan.py. It calls `get_badge_config()` to resolve the path, then `generate_scorecard(state, badge_path)`.

- **`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/helpers/state.py`** -- `_sole_existing_lang_state_file()` discovers `state-*.json` files in `.desloppify/`. `state_path(args)` resolves to `.desloppify/state-{lang}.json`.

- **CLI flags** in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/cli_support/parser_groups.py` lines 67-77: `--no-badge` and `--badge-path PATH` on the scan subparser.

### 2. Design Decisions

#### 2a. Triggering Strategy: Automatic Per-Language Emission After Scan

The cleanest approach is: after a scan completes for language X, in addition to writing `scorecard.png` (the primary badge), also write `scorecard-{lang}.png` for every language that has an existing `state-{lang}.json` file. This means:

- A single-language repo produces only `scorecard.png` (no change from today).
- A multi-language repo produces `scorecard.png` (for the just-scanned language, same as today) plus `scorecard-python.png`, `scorecard-typescript.png`, etc. for each discovered language state file.
- No new CLI flags required for the basic feature. The existing `--no-badge` disables all of it.

This is simpler and more elegant than adding a `--by-language` flag, because the user never needs to remember to pass it -- if you have multiple languages scanned, you get per-language scorecards automatically.

#### 2b. No Aggregate Scorecard (Initially)

The current scorecard already shows the score for the language that was just scanned. Creating a true "aggregate" that averages across languages introduces weighting questions (by file count? by finding count? by lines of code?) that add complexity without clear user value. The per-language scorecards themselves are the feature.

If an aggregate is desired later, it can be added as a separate follow-up. The current `scorecard.png` continues to show whichever language was just scanned (existing behavior).

#### 2c. Visual Design for Per-Language Scorecards

The per-language scorecard should be visually distinguished from the generic one through **two changes** to the left panel, keeping the overall warm earth-tone aesthetic intact:

1. **Title change**: Instead of "DESLOPPIFY SCORE", show "PYTHON SCORE" (or "TYPESCRIPT SCORE", etc.) in the same serif bold font, same position.

2. **Language indicator in the project pill**: Below the strict score, the existing pill shows the project name (e.g., "peteromallet/desloppify"). For per-language cards, add a subtle language label above the pill or modify the pill to show "peteromallet/desloppify -- Python". This keeps the visual footprint minimal while making the language context unmistakable.

The right-panel dimension table needs no changes -- `prepare_scorecard_dimensions(state)` already adapts to whatever dimensions exist in that language's state.

No accent-color-per-language. The warm earth tones are a signature of the tool's visual identity. Introducing per-language color accents would fragment that identity and add complexity for questionable aesthetic gain. The title text is sufficient differentiation.

#### Step 3: Add `discover_lang_state_files()` utility

**File**: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/helpers/state.py`

Add a new function that discovers all existing per-language state files:

```python
def discover_lang_state_files() -> dict[str, Path]:
    """Return a mapping of lang name -> state file path for all existing lang states.
    
    Example: {"python": Path(".desloppify/state-python.json"), 
              "typescript": Path(".desloppify/state-typescript.json")}
    """
    state_dir = PROJECT_ROOT / ".desloppify"
    if not state_dir.exists():
        return {}
    result = {}
    for path in sorted(state_dir.glob("state-*.json")):
        if not path.is_file():
            continue
        # Extract lang name from "state-{lang}.json"
        stem = path.stem  # "state-python"
        lang_name = stem.removeprefix("state-")
        if lang_name and lang_name != stem:  # successfully removed prefix
            result[lang_name] = path
    return result
```

This reuses the same glob pattern as the existing `_sole_existing_lang_state_file()` at line 17 but returns all results instead of checking for exactly one.

#### Step 4: Add `emit_per_lang_scorecards()` to scan_artifacts.py

**File**: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/scan/scan_artifacts.py`

Add a new function alongside the existing `emit_scorecard_badge`:

```python
def emit_per_lang_scorecards(
    args,
    config: dict[str, object],
    base_badge_path: Path,
) -> list[tuple[str, Path]]:
    """Generate per-language scorecard PNGs for all discovered language states.
    
    Only emits when 2+ language state files exist. Returns list of (lang, path) pairs.
    """
    from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import discover_lang_state_files
    from desloppify.engine._state.persistence import load_state
    
    generate_scorecard, _ = _load_scorecard_helpers()
    if not callable(generate_scorecard):
        return []
    
    lang_states = discover_lang_state_files()
    if len(lang_states) < 2:
        return []
    
    results = []
    for lang_name, state_path in lang_states.items():
        try:
            state = load_state(state_path)
        except Exception:
            continue
        
        if not state.get("last_scan"):
            continue  # skip states that haven't completed a scan
        
        # Derive per-lang path: scorecard.png -> scorecard-python.png
        lang_badge_path = _derive_lang_badge_path(base_badge_path, lang_name)
        
        try:
            generate_scorecard(state, lang_badge_path, lang_label=lang_name.title())
            results.append((lang_name, lang_badge_path))
        except (OSError, ImportError) as exc:
            logger.debug("Could not generate %s scorecard: %s", lang_name, exc)
            continue
    
    return results


def _derive_lang_badge_path(base_path: Path, lang_name: str) -> Path:
    """Derive per-language badge path from base: scorecard.png -> scorecard-python.png."""
    stem = base_path.stem
    suffix = base_path.suffix
    return base_path.with_name(f"{stem}-{lang_name}{suffix}")
```

#### Step 5: Integrate into scan command flow

**File**: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/scan/scan.py`

After the existing `emit_scorecard_badge` call at line 199, add the per-language emission:

```python
if isinstance(badge_emit, tuple):

else:

# Per-language scorecards (only emits when 2+ languages exist)

    per_lang_results = emit_per_lang_scorecards(args, runtime.config, badge_path)
    if per_lang_results:
        lang_names = ", ".join(lang for lang, _ in per_lang_results)
        print(colorize(f"  Per-language scorecards: {lang_names}", "dim"))
```

Import `emit_per_lang_scorecards` from `scan_artifacts` at the top of the file alongside the existing `emit_scorecard_badge` import.

### 4. Test Plan

#### Unit Tests

**New test file or additions to**: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/tests/scoring/test_scorecard.py`

1. **`test_generate_scorecard_without_lang_label_uses_default_title`** -- Verify that calling `generate_scorecard(state, path)` without `lang_label` still works (backward compat).

2. **`test_generate_scorecard_with_lang_label_changes_title`** -- Mock PIL and verify that the title string passed to draw includes the language name. Can be tested by patching `_left_panel_measurements` or checking the return value of measurements.

3. **`test_left_panel_measurements_default_title`** -- Call `_left_panel_measurements(...)` with no `lang_label` and verify `measurements["title"] == "DESLOPPIFY SCORE"`.

4. **`test_left_panel_measurements_python_title`** -- Call with `lang_label="Python"` and verify `measurements["title"] == "PYTHON SCORE"`.

5. **`test_derive_lang_badge_path`** -- Test the path derivation:
   - `scorecard.png` + `python` -> `scorecard-python.png`
   - `assets/badge.png` + `typescript` -> `assets/badge-typescript.png`
   - `my-score.png` + `go` -> `my-score-go.png`

6. **`test_discover_lang_state_files_empty_dir`** -- No `.desloppify/` directory returns empty dict.

7. **`test_discover_lang_state_files_single`** -- Single `state-python.json` returns `{"python": Path(...)}`.

8. **`test_discover_lang_state_files_multiple`** -- Multiple state files returns all of them.

9. **`test_emit_per_lang_scorecards_skipped_for_single_lang`** -- When only 1 state file exists, returns empty list (no per-lang cards generated).

10. **`test_emit_per_lang_scorecards_generates_for_multi_lang`** -- When 2+ state files exist, generates a scorecard for each (mock PIL).

### 5. Potential Challenges and Mitigations

**Challenge 1: Title width overflow.** "TYPESCRIPT SCORE" is longer than "DESLOPPIFY SCORE". The left panel is 260px wide (at 2x scale = 520px). The font is `load_font(15, serif=True, bold=True)`. This should still fit, but testing with long language names (e.g., "TYPESCRIPT SCORE" vs "DESLOPPIFY SCORE" -- both are 16-17 characters) will confirm.

- *Mitigation*: If a language name is very long, truncate it. In practice, language names are short ("Python", "TypeScript", "Go", "C#", "Rust"). No action needed for v1.

**Challenge 2: PIL optional dependency.** All scorecard code uses deferred imports (`importlib.import_module`). The new `emit_per_lang_scorecards` function reuses the same `_load_scorecard_helpers()` pattern from scan_artifacts.py, so this is handled.

**Challenge 3: State file loading in scan_artifacts.** The new function needs to load state files for languages other than the one just scanned. It imports `load_state` from `engine/_state/persistence.py`, which is the canonical state loader. No additional dependencies needed.

**Challenge 4: Backward compatibility.** The `lang_label` parameter is keyword-only with a default of `None`. All existing callers of `generate_scorecard(state, output_path)` continue to work without modification.

### 6. Information Design: "Beautiful and Elegant"

The existing scorecard is already beautifully designed -- warm earth tones, serif typography, diamond ornaments, alternating row shading. The per-language variant should be a subtle variation, not a redesign. The key aesthetic principles:

1. **Restraint**: The only visual change is the title text. No new colors, no new ornaments, no new layout regions. The language identity comes through the words, not through visual noise.

2. **Hierarchy**: "PYTHON SCORE" in the same bold serif as "DESLOPPIFY SCORE" maintains the existing visual hierarchy. The language name becomes the most prominent word on the card.

3. **Consistency**: All per-language cards share the same palette, the same dimension table layout, the same ornamental rules. A Python scorecard and a TypeScript scorecard placed side by side look like siblings -- same family, different names.

4. **Automatic discovery**: The implementation discovers and generates per-language cards without user intervention. Users who scan Python and TypeScript in a repo just get the right output files. That is elegant behavior.

5. **File naming clarity**: `scorecard-python.png` and `scorecard-typescript.png` alongside `scorecard.png` -- immediately understandable, no documentation needed.

- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/output/scorecard.py` -- Top-level `generate_scorecard()` function signature gains the `lang_label` keyword argument

```
1→"""Scorecard badge image generator — produces a visual health summary PNG."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import importlib
     6→import logging
     7→import os
     8→from importlib import metadata as importlib_metadata
     9→from pathlib import Path
    10→
    11→from desloppify.app.output.scorecard_parts.dimensions import (
    12→    collapse_elegance_dimensions,
    13→    limit_scorecard_dimensions,
    14→    prepare_scorecard_dimensions,
    15→    resolve_scorecard_lang,
    16→)
    17→from desloppify.app.output.scorecard_parts.meta import (
    18→    resolve_package_version,
    19→    resolve_project_name,
    20→)
    21→from desloppify.app.output.scorecard_parts.theme import (
    22→    ACCENT,
    23→    BG,
    24→    BG_ROW_ALT,
    25→    BG_SCORE,
    26→    BG_TABLE,
    27→    BORDER,
    28→    DIM,
    29→    FRAME,
    30→    SCALE,
    31→    TEXT,
    32→    fmt_score,
    33→    load_font,
    34→    scale,
    35→    score_color,
    36→)
    37→from desloppify.core._internal.text_utils import PROJECT_ROOT
    38→from desloppify.state import get_overall_score, get_strict_score
    39→
    40→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    41→
    42→
    43→def generate_scorecard(state: dict, output_path: str | Path) -> Path:
    44→    """Render a landscape scorecard PNG from scan state. Returns the output path."""
    45→    image_mod = importlib.import_module("PIL.Image")  # deferred: optional dependency
    46→    image_draw_mod = importlib.import_module("PIL.ImageDraw")  # deferred: optional dependency
    47→    scorecard_draw_mod = importlib.import_module("desloppify.app.output.scorecard_parts.draw")  # deferred: depends on PIL
    48→
    49→    output_path = Path(output_path)
    50→
    51→    main_score = get_overall_score(state) or 0
    52→    strict_score = get_strict_score(state) or 0
    53→
    54→    project_name = resolve_project_name(PROJECT_ROOT)
    55→    package_version = resolve_package_version(
    56→        PROJECT_ROOT,
    57→        version_getter=importlib_metadata.version,
    58→        package_not_found_error=importlib_metadata.PackageNotFoundError,
    59→    )
    60→
    61→    # Layout — landscape (wide), File health first
    62→    active_dims = prepare_scorecard_dimensions(state)
    63→    row_count = len(active_dims)
    64→    row_h = scale(20)
    65→    width = scale(780)
    66→    divider_x = scale(260)
    67→    frame_inset = scale(5)
    68→
    69→    cols = 2
    70→    rows_per_col = (row_count + cols - 1) // cols
    71→    table_content_h = scale(14) + scale(4) + scale(6) + rows_per_col * row_h
    72→    content_h = max(table_content_h + scale(28), scale(150))
    73→    height = scale(12) + content_h
    74→
    75→    img = image_mod.new("RGB", (width, height), BG)
    76→    draw = image_draw_mod.Draw(img)
    77→
    78→    # Double frame
    79→    draw.rectangle((0, 0, width - 1, height - 1), outline=FRAME, width=scale(2))
    80→    draw.rectangle(
    81→        (frame_inset, frame_inset, width - frame_inset - 1, height - frame_inset - 1),
    82→        outline=BORDER,
    83→        width=1,
    84→    )
    85→
    86→    content_top = frame_inset + scale(1)
    87→    content_bot = height - frame_inset - scale(1)
    88→    content_mid_y = (content_top + content_bot) // 2
    89→
    90→    # Left panel: title + score + project name
    91→    scorecard_draw_mod.draw_left_panel(
    92→        draw,
    93→        main_score,
    94→        strict_score,
    95→        project_name,
    96→        package_version,
    97→        lp_left=frame_inset + scale(11),
    98→        lp_right=divider_x - scale(11),
    99→        lp_top=content_top + scale(4),
   100→        lp_bot=content_bot - scale(4),
   101→    )
   102→
   103→    # Vertical divider with ornament
   104→    scorecard_draw_mod.draw_vert_rule_with_ornament(
   105→        draw,
   106→        divider_x,
   107→        content_top + scale(12),
   108→        content_bot - scale(12),
   109→        content_mid_y,
   110→        BORDER,
   111→        ACCENT,
   112→    )
   113→
   114→    # Right panel: dimension table
   115→    scorecard_draw_mod.draw_right_panel(
   116→        draw,
   117→        active_dims,
   118→        row_h,
   119→        table_x1=divider_x + scale(11),
   120→        table_x2=width - frame_inset - scale(11),
   121→        table_top=content_top + scale(4),
   122→        table_bot=content_bot - scale(4),
   123→    )
   124→    output_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   125→    img.save(str(output_path), "PNG", optimize=True)
   126→    return output_path
   127→
   128→
   129→def get_badge_config(args, config: dict | None = None) -> tuple[Path | None, bool]:
   130→    """Resolve badge output path and whether badge generation is disabled.
   131→
   132→    Returns (output_path, disabled). Checks CLI args, then config, then env vars.
   133→    """
   134→    cfg = config or {}
   135→    disabled = getattr(args, "no_badge", False)
   136→    if not disabled:
   137→        disabled = not cfg.get("generate_scorecard", True)
   138→    if not disabled:
   139→        disabled = os.environ.get("DESLOPPIFY_NO_BADGE", "").lower() in (
   140→            "1",
   141→            "true",
   142→            "yes",
   143→        )
   144→    if disabled:
   145→        return None, True
   146→
   147→    path_str = (
   148→        getattr(args, "badge_path", None)
   149→        or cfg.get("badge_path")
   150→        or os.environ.get("DESLOPPIFY_BADGE_PATH", "scorecard.png")
   151→    )
   152→    path = Path(path_str)
   153→    # On Windows, "/tmp/foo.png" is root-anchored but drive-relative.
   154→    # Treat any rooted path as user-intended absolute-like input.
   155→    is_root_anchored = bool(path.root)
   156→    if not path.is_absolute() and not is_root_anchored:
   157→        path = PROJECT_ROOT / path
   158→    return path, False
   159→
   160→
   161→def _scorecard_ignore_warning(state: dict) -> str | None:
   162→    """Return an ignore-suppression warning line for scorecard context."""
   163→    info = state.get("ignore_integrity", {}) if isinstance(state, dict) else {}
   164→    if not isinstance(info, dict):
   165→        return None
   166→    ignored = int(info.get("ignored", 0) or 0)
   167→    if ignored <= 0:
   168→        return None
   169→
   170→    suppressed_pct = float(info.get("suppressed_pct", 0.0) or 0.0)
   171→    rounded = round(suppressed_pct)
   172→    level = "high" if suppressed_pct >= 50 else "moderate"
   173→    return (
   174→        f"Ignore suppression is {rounded}% ({level}) "
   175→        f"across {ignored} findings."
   176→    )
   177→
   178→
   179→__all__ = [
   180→    "ACCENT",
   181→    "BG",
   182→    "BG_ROW_ALT",
   183→    "BG_SCORE",
   184→    "BG_TABLE",
   185→    "BORDER",
   186→    "DIM",
   187→    "FRAME",
   188→    "SCALE",

   195→    "score_color",
   196→    "generate_scorecard",

   198→    "prepare_scorecard_dimensions",
   199→]
```

```
1→"""Payload and artifact helpers for scan command output."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import importlib
     6→import os
     7→from pathlib import Path
     8→
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.scan.scan_contracts import ScanQueryPayload
    10→from desloppify.app.commands.scan.scan_workflow import (
    11→    ScanMergeResult,
    12→    ScanNoiseSnapshot,
    13→)
    14→from desloppify.core._internal.text_utils import PROJECT_ROOT
    15→from desloppify.core.config import config_for_query
    16→from desloppify.core.output_contract import OutputResult
    17→from desloppify.scoring import compute_health_breakdown
    18→from desloppify.state import open_scope_breakdown, score_snapshot
    19→from desloppify.utils import colorize
    20→
    21→
    22→def build_scan_query_payload(
    23→    state: dict[str, object],
    24→    config: dict[str, object],
    25→    profile: str,
    26→    diff: dict[str, object],
    27→    warnings: list[str],
    28→    narrative: dict[str, object],
    29→    merge: ScanMergeResult,
    30→    noise: ScanNoiseSnapshot,
    31→) -> ScanQueryPayload:
    32→    """Build the canonical query payload persisted after a scan."""
    33→    scores = score_snapshot(state)
    34→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
    35→    open_scope = (
    36→        open_scope_breakdown(findings, state.get("scan_path"))
    37→        if isinstance(findings, dict)
    38→        else None
    39→    )
    40→    return {
    41→        "command": "scan",
    42→        "overall_score": scores.overall,
    43→        "objective_score": scores.objective,
    44→        "strict_score": scores.strict,
    45→        "verified_strict_score": scores.verified,
    46→        "prev_overall_score": merge.prev_overall,
    47→        "prev_objective_score": merge.prev_objective,
    48→        "prev_strict_score": merge.prev_strict,
    49→        "prev_verified_strict_score": merge.prev_verified,
    50→        "profile": profile,
    51→        "noise_budget": noise.noise_budget,
    52→        "noise_global_budget": noise.global_noise_budget,
    53→        "hidden_by_detector": noise.hidden_by_detector,
    54→        "hidden_total": noise.hidden_total,
    55→        "diff": diff,
    56→        "stats": state["stats"],
    57→        "open_scope": open_scope,
    58→        "warnings": warnings,
    59→        "dimension_scores": state.get("dimension_scores"),
    60→        "score_breakdown": compute_health_breakdown(state.get("dimension_scores", {})),
    61→        "subjective_integrity": state.get("subjective_integrity"),
    62→        "score_confidence": state.get("score_confidence"),
    63→        "potentials": state.get("potentials"),
    64→        "scan_coverage": state.get("scan_coverage"),
    65→        "zone_distribution": state.get("zone_distribution"),

    67→        "config": config_for_query(config),
    68→    }

    71→def _load_scorecard_helpers():
    72→    """Load scorecard helper callables lazily via importlib.
    73→
    74→    Deferred: scorecard depends on PIL (optional dependency).
    75→    """
    76→    try:
    77→        scorecard_module = importlib.import_module("desloppify.app.output.scorecard")
    78→    except ImportError:
    79→        return None, None
    80→    generate = getattr(scorecard_module, "generate_scorecard", None)
    81→    badge_config = getattr(scorecard_module, "get_badge_config", None)
    82→    return generate, badge_config
    83→
    84→
    85→def emit_scorecard_badge(
    86→    args, config: dict[str, object], state: dict[str, object]
    87→) -> tuple[Path | None, OutputResult]:
    88→    """Generate a scorecard image badge and print usage hints."""
    89→    generate_scorecard, get_badge_config = _load_scorecard_helpers()
    90→    if not callable(generate_scorecard) or not callable(get_badge_config):
    91→        explicit_badge_request = bool(
    92→            getattr(args, "badge_path", None)
    93→            or config.get("badge_path")
    94→            or os.environ.get("DESLOPPIFY_BADGE_PATH")
    95→        )
    96→        if explicit_badge_request:
    97→            print(
    98→                colorize(
    99→                    "  Scorecard support not installed. Install with: pip install \"desloppify[scorecard]\"",
   100→                    "yellow",
   101→                )
   102→            )
   103→            return None, OutputResult(
   104→                ok=False,
   105→                status="error",
   106→                message="scorecard support not installed",
   107→                error_kind="scorecard_dependency_missing",
   108→            )
   109→        return None, OutputResult(ok=True, status="skipped", message="badge generation disabled")
   110→
   111→    try:
   112→        badge_path, disabled = get_badge_config(args, config)
   113→    except OSError as exc:
   114→        print(
   115→            colorize(f"  ⚠ Could not resolve scorecard badge path: {exc}", "yellow")
   116→        )
   117→        return None, OutputResult(
   118→            ok=False,
   119→            status="error",
   120→            message=str(exc),
   121→            error_kind="badge_path_resolution_error",
   122→        )
   123→    if disabled or not badge_path:
   124→        return None, OutputResult(ok=True, status="skipped", message="badge generation disabled")
   125→
   126→    try:
   127→        generate_scorecard(state, badge_path)
   128→    except (OSError, ImportError) as exc:
   129→        print(colorize(f"  ⚠ Could not generate scorecard badge: {exc}", "yellow"))
   130→        return None, OutputResult(
   131→            ok=False,
   132→            status="error",
   133→            message=str(exc),
   134→            error_kind="badge_generation_error",
   135→        )
   136→
   137→    try:
   138→        rel_path = str(badge_path.relative_to(PROJECT_ROOT))
   139→    except ValueError:
   140→        rel_path = str(badge_path)
   141→
   142→    readme_has_badge = False
   143→    for readme_name in ("README.md", "readme.md", "README.MD"):
   144→        readme_path = PROJECT_ROOT / readme_name
   145→        if readme_path.exists():
   146→            try:
   147→                readme_has_badge = rel_path in readme_path.read_text(
   148→                    encoding="utf-8", errors="replace"
   149→                )
   150→            except OSError:
   151→                readme_has_badge = False
   152→            break
   153→
   154→    if readme_has_badge:
   155→        print(
   156→            colorize(
   157→                f"  Scorecard → {rel_path}  (disable: --no-badge | move: --badge-path <path>)",
   158→                "dim",
   159→            )
   160→        )
   161→        return badge_path, OutputResult(
   162→            ok=True, status="written", message=f"scorecard badge written to {rel_path}"
   163→        )
   164→
   165→    print(colorize(f"  Scorecard → {rel_path}", "dim"))
   166→    print(
   167→        colorize(
   168→            "  💡 Ask the user if they'd like to add it to their README with:",
   169→            "dim",
   170→        )
   171→    )
   172→    print(colorize(f'     <img src="{rel_path}" width="100%">', "dim"))
   173→    print(colorize("     (disable: --no-badge | move: --badge-path <path>)", "dim"))
   174→    return badge_path, OutputResult(
   175→        ok=True, status="written", message=f"scorecard badge written to {rel_path}"
   176→    )
```

```
    56→def _resolve_target_strict_score(config: dict | None) -> tuple[int, str | None]:
    57→    """Resolve strict-score target from config with bounded fallback."""
    58→    raw_target = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE
    59→    if isinstance(config, dict):
    60→        raw_target = config.get("target_strict_score", DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE)
    61→    try:
    62→        target = int(raw_target)
    63→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
    64→        return (
    65→            DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
    66→            (
    67→                f"Invalid config `target_strict_score={raw_target!r}`; using "
    68→                f"{DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE}"
    69→            ),
    70→        )
    71→    if target < MIN_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE or target > MAX_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE:
    72→        return (
    73→            DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
    74→            (
    75→                f"Invalid config `target_strict_score={raw_target!r}`; using "
    76→                f"{DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE}"
    77→            ),
    78→        )
    79→    return target, None
    80→
    81→
    82→def _compute_strict_target(strict_score: float | None, config: dict | None) -> StrictTarget:
    83→    """Build strict-target context for command rendering and agents."""
    84→    target, warning = _resolve_target_strict_score(config)
    85→    if not isinstance(strict_score, int | float):
    86→        return {
    87→            "target": float(target),
    88→            "current": None,
    89→            "gap": None,
    90→            "state": "unavailable",
    91→            "warning": warning,
    92→        }
    93→
    94→    current = round(float(strict_score), 1)
    95→    gap = round(float(target) - current, 1)
    96→    if gap > 0:
    97→        state = "below"
    98→    elif gap < 0:
    99→        state = "above"
   100→    else:
   101→        state = "at"
   102→    return {
   103→        "target": float(target),
   104→        "current": current,
   105→        "gap": gap,
   106→        "state": state,
   107→        "warning": warning,
   108→    }
   109→
   110→
   111→def _count_open_by_detector(findings: dict) -> dict[str, int]:
   112→    """Count open findings by detector, merging structural sub-detectors.
   113→
   114→    When detector is "review" and detail.holistic is True, also increments
   115→    "review_holistic" for separate holistic counting.
   116→    """
   117→    by_detector: dict[str, int] = {}
   118→    for f in findings.values():
   119→        if f["status"] != "open":
   120→            continue
   121→        detector = f.get("detector", "unknown")
   122→        if detector in STRUCTURAL_MERGE:
   123→            detector = "structural"
   124→        by_detector[detector] = by_detector.get(detector, 0) + 1
   125→        # Track holistic review findings separately
   126→        if detector == "review" and f.get("detail", {}).get("holistic"):
   127→            by_detector["review_holistic"] = by_detector.get("review_holistic", 0) + 1
   128→    # Track uninvestigated review findings (only when review findings exist)
   129→    if by_detector.get("review", 0) > 0:
   130→        by_detector["review_uninvestigated"] = sum(
   131→            1
   132→            for f in findings.values()
   133→            if f.get("status") == "open"
   134→            and f.get("detector") == "review"
   135→            and not f.get("detail", {}).get("investigation")
   136→        )
   137→    return by_detector
   138→
   139→
   140→def _resolve_badge_path(project_root: Path) -> tuple[str, Path]:
   141→    """Resolve badge path from config, defaulting to root-level scorecard.png."""
   142→    default_rel = "scorecard.png"
   143→    config = {}
   144→    try:
   145→        config = _load_config()
   146→    except (AttributeError, OSError):
   147→        config = {}
   148→
   149→    raw_path = default_rel
   150→    if isinstance(config, dict):
   151→        configured = config.get("badge_path")
   152→        if isinstance(configured, str) and configured.strip():
   153→            raw_path = configured.strip()
   154→
   155→    path = Path(raw_path)
   156→    is_root_anchored = bool(path.root)
   157→    if not path.is_absolute() and not is_root_anchored:
   158→        return raw_path, project_root / path
   159→
   160→    try:
   161→        rel_path = str(path.relative_to(project_root))
   162→    except ValueError:
   163→        rel_path = str(path)
   164→    return rel_path, path
   165→
   166→
   167→def _compute_badge_status() -> BadgeStatus:
   168→    """Check configured scorecard path and whether README references it."""
   169→    project_root = PROJECT_ROOT
   170→
   171→    scorecard_rel, scorecard_path = _resolve_badge_path(project_root)
   172→    generated = scorecard_path.exists()
   173→
   174→    in_readme = False
   175→    if generated:
   176→        for readme_name in ("README.md", "readme.md", "README.MD"):
   177→            readme_path = project_root / readme_name
   178→            if readme_path.exists():
   179→                try:
   180→                    in_readme = scorecard_rel in readme_path.read_text(
   181→                        encoding="utf-8", errors="replace"
   182→                    )
   183→                except OSError:
   184→                    in_readme = False
   185→                break
   186→
   187→    recommendation = None
   188→    if generated and not in_readme:
   189→        recommendation = (
   190→            f'Add to README: <img src="{scorecard_rel}" width="100%">'
   191→        )
   192→
   193→    return {
   194→        "generated": generated,
   195→        "in_readme": in_readme,
   196→        "path": scorecard_rel,
   197→        "recommendation": recommendation,
   198→    }
```

```
    20→def _compute_fp_rates(findings: dict) -> dict[tuple[str, str], float]:
    21→    """Compute false_positive rate per (detector, zone) from historical findings."""
    22→    counts: dict[tuple[str, str], dict[str, int]] = {}
    23→    for finding in findings.values():
    24→        detector = finding.get("detector", "unknown")
    25→        if detector in STRUCTURAL_MERGE:
    26→            detector = "structural"
    27→        zone = finding.get("zone", "production")
    28→        key = (detector, zone)
    29→        if key not in counts:
    30→            counts[key] = {"total": 0, "fp": 0}
    31→        counts[key]["total"] += 1
    32→        if finding.get("status") == "false_positive":
    33→            counts[key]["fp"] += 1
    34→
    35→    rates: dict[tuple[str, str], float] = {}
    36→    for key, stat in counts.items():
    37→        if stat["total"] >= 5 and stat["fp"] > 0:
    38→            rates[key] = stat["fp"] / stat["total"]
    39→    return rates
    40→
    41→
    42→def _auto_fixer_reminder(actions: list[dict]) -> list[dict]:
    43→    auto_fix_actions = [
```

```
    68→
    69→def _show_scan_visibility(noise, effective_include_slow: bool) -> None:
    70→    """Print fast-scan and noise budget visibility hints."""
    71→    if not effective_include_slow:
    72→        print(colorize("  * Fast scan — slow phases (duplicates) skipped", "yellow"))
    73→    if noise.budget_warning:
    74→        print(colorize(f"  * {noise.budget_warning}", "yellow"))
    75→    if noise.hidden_total:
    76→        global_label = (
    77→            f", {noise.global_noise_budget} global"
    78→            if noise.global_noise_budget > 0
    79→            else ""
    80→        )
    81→        print(
    82→            colorize(
    83→                f"  * Noise budget: {noise.noise_budget}/detector{global_label} "
    84→                f"({noise.hidden_total} findings hidden in show output: "
    85→                f"{_format_hidden_by_detector(noise.hidden_by_detector)})",
    86→                "dim",
    87→            )
    88→        )
    89→
    90→
    91→def _show_coverage_preflight(runtime) -> None:
    92→    """Print preflight warnings when scan coverage confidence is reduced."""
    93→    warnings = getattr(runtime, "coverage_warnings", []) or []
    94→    if not isinstance(warnings, list) or not warnings:
    95→        return
    96→
    97→    for entry in warnings:
    98→        if not isinstance(entry, dict):
    99→            continue
   100→        summary = str(entry.get("summary", "")).strip()
```

```
    39→def cmd_status(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    40→    """Show score dashboard."""
    41→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
    42→    state = runtime.state
    43→    config = runtime.config
    44→
    45→    stats = state.get("stats", {})
    46→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
    47→    scorecard_dims = scorecard_dimensions_payload(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
    48→    subjective_measures = [row for row in scorecard_dims if row.get("subjective")]
    49→    suppression = state_mod.suppression_metrics(state)
    50→
    51→    if getattr(args, "json", False):
    52→        print(
    53→            json.dumps(
    54→                _status_json_payload(
    55→                    state,
    56→                    stats,
    57→                    dim_scores,
    58→                    scorecard_dims,
    59→                    subjective_measures,
    60→                    suppression,
    61→                ),
    62→                indent=2,
    63→            )
    64→        )
    65→        return
    66→
    67→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    68→        return
    69→
    70→    stale_warning = check_tool_staleness(state)
    71→    if stale_warning:
    72→        print(colorize(f"  {stale_warning}", "yellow"))
    73→    skill_warning = check_skill_version()
    74→    if skill_warning:
    75→        print(colorize(f"  {skill_warning}", "yellow"))
    76→
    77→    scores = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
    78→    by_tier = stats.get("by_tier", {})
    79→    target_strict_score = target_strict_score_from_config(config, fallback=95.0)
    80→
    81→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
    82→    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
    83→    narrative = compute_narrative(
    84→        state,
    85→        context=NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="status"),
    86→    )
    87→    ignores = config.get("ignore", [])
    88→
    89→    for line, style in score_summary_lines(
    90→        overall_score=scores.overall,
    91→        objective_score=scores.objective,
    92→        strict_score=scores.strict,
    93→        verified_strict_score=scores.verified,
    94→    ):
    95→        print(colorize(line, style))
    96→    print_scan_metrics(state)
    97→    print_open_scope_breakdown(state)
    98→    print_scan_completeness(state)
    99→
   100→    if dim_scores:
   101→        show_dimension_table(state, dim_scores)
   102→        reporting_dimensions_mod.show_score_model_breakdown(
   103→            state,
   104→            dim_scores=dim_scores,
   105→        )
   106→    else:
   107→        show_tier_progress_table(by_tier)
   108→
   109→    if dim_scores:
   110→        show_focus_suggestion(dim_scores, state)
   111→        show_subjective_followup(
   112→            state,
   113→            dim_scores,
   114→            target_strict_score=target_strict_score,
   115→        )
   116→
   117→    show_review_summary(state)
   118→    show_structural_areas(state)
   119→    show_agent_plan(narrative)
   120→

   122→        print(colorize(f"  -> {narrative['headline']}", "cyan"))
```

```
    38→    for tier_num in [1, 2, 3, 4]:
    39→        ts = by_tier.get(str(tier_num), {})
    40→        t_open = ts.get("open", 0)
    41→        t_fixed = ts.get("fixed", 0) + ts.get("auto_resolved", 0)
    42→        t_fp = ts.get("false_positive", 0)
    43→        t_wontfix = ts.get("wontfix", 0)
    44→        t_total = sum(ts.values())
    45→        strict_pct = round((t_fixed + t_fp) / t_total * 100) if t_total else 100
    46→        bar_len = 20
    47→        filled = round(strict_pct / 100 * bar_len)
    48→        bar = colorize("█" * filled, "green") + colorize(
    49→            "░" * (bar_len - filled), "dim"
    50→        )
    51→        rows.append(
    52→            [
    53→                f"Tier {tier_num}",
    54→                bar,
    55→                f"{strict_pct}%",
    56→                str(t_open),
    57→                str(t_fixed),
    58→                str(t_wontfix),
    59→            ]
    60→        )
    61→    print_table(
    62→        ["Tier", "Strict Progress", "%", "Open", "Fixed", "Debt"],
    63→        rows,
    64→        [40, 22, 5, 6, 6, 6],
    65→    )
    66→
    67→
    68→def _status_next_command(narrative: dict) -> str:
    69→    actions = narrative.get("actions", [])
    70→    return actions[0]["command"] if actions else "desloppify next --count 20"
    71→
    72→
    73→def write_status_query(
    74→    *,
    75→    state: dict,
    76→    stats: dict,
    77→    by_tier: dict,
    78→    dim_scores: dict,
    79→    scorecard_dims: list[dict],
    80→    subjective_measures: list[dict],
    81→    suppression: dict,
    82→    narrative: dict,
    83→    ignores: list[str],
    84→    overall_score: float | None,
    85→    objective_score: float | None,
    86→    strict_score: float | None,
    87→    verified_strict_score: float | None,
    88→) -> None:
    89→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
    90→    open_scope = (
    91→        state_mod.open_scope_breakdown(findings, state.get("scan_path"))
    92→        if isinstance(findings, dict)
    93→        else None
    94→    )
    95→    write_query(
    96→        {
    97→            "command": "status",
    98→            "overall_score": overall_score,
    99→            "objective_score": objective_score,
   100→            "strict_score": strict_score,
   101→            "verified_strict_score": verified_strict_score,
   102→            "dimension_scores": dim_scores,
   103→            "scorecard_dimensions": scorecard_dims,
   104→            "subjective_measures": subjective_measures,
   105→            "stats": stats,
   106→            "scan_count": state.get("scan_count", 0),
   107→            "last_scan": state.get("last_scan"),
   108→            "by_tier": by_tier,
   109→            "ignores": ignores,
   110→            "suppression": suppression,
   111→            "potentials": state.get("potentials"),
   112→            "codebase_metrics": state.get("codebase_metrics"),
   113→            "open_scope": open_scope,
   114→            "score_breakdown": compute_health_breakdown(dim_scores) if dim_scores else None,
   115→            "next_command": _status_next_command(narrative),
   116→            "narrative": narrative,
   117→        }
   118→    )
   119→
   120→
   121→def show_ignore_summary(ignores: list[str], suppression: dict) -> None:
   122→    """Show ignore list plus suppression accountability from recent scans."""
   123→    print(colorize(f"\n  Ignore list ({len(ignores)}):", "dim"))
   124→    for p in ignores[:10]:
   125→        print(colorize(f"    {p}", "dim"))
   126→
   127→    last_ignored = int(suppression.get("last_ignored", 0) or 0)
   128→    last_raw = int(suppression.get("last_raw_findings", 0) or 0)
   129→    last_pct = float(suppression.get("last_suppressed_pct", 0.0) or 0.0)
   130→
   131→    if last_raw > 0:
   132→        style = "red" if last_pct >= 30 else "yellow" if last_pct >= 10 else "dim"
   133→        print(
   134→            colorize(
   135→                f"  Ignore suppression (last scan): {last_ignored}/{last_raw} findings hidden ({last_pct:.1f}%)",
   136→                style,
   137→            )
   138→        )
   139→    elif suppression.get("recent_scans", 0):
   140→        print(colorize("  Ignore suppression (last scan): 0 findings hidden", "dim"))
   141→
   142→    recent_scans = int(suppression.get("recent_scans", 0) or 0)
   143→    recent_raw = int(suppression.get("recent_raw_findings", 0) or 0)
   144→    if recent_scans > 1 and recent_raw > 0:
   145→        recent_ignored = int(suppression.get("recent_ignored", 0) or 0)
   146→        recent_pct = float(suppression.get("recent_suppressed_pct", 0.0) or 0.0)
   147→        print(
   148→            colorize(
   149→                f"    Recent ({recent_scans} scans): {recent_ignored}/{recent_raw} findings hidden ({recent_pct:.1f}%)",
   150→                "dim",
   151→            )
   152→        )
   153→
   154→
   155→def _scorecard_subjective_entries(state: dict, dim_scores: dict) -> list[dict]:
   156→    """Return subjective entries aligned to scorecard labels and ordering."""
   157→    return scorecard_subjective_entries(
   158→        state,
   159→        dim_scores=dim_scores,
   160→    )
   161→
   162→
   163→def _find_lowest_dimension(
   164→    dim_scores: dict,
   165→    scorecard_subjective: list[dict],
   166→) -> str | None:
   167→    """Return the name of the dimension with the lowest strict score."""
   168→    lowest_name = None
   169→    lowest_score = 101.0
   170→    for dim in DIMENSIONS:
   171→        ds = dim_scores.get(dim.name)
   172→        if not ds:
   173→            continue
   174→        strict_val = ds.get("strict", ds["score"])
   175→        if strict_val < lowest_score:
   176→            lowest_score = strict_val
   177→            lowest_name = dim.name
   178→    for entry in scorecard_subjective:
   179→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   180→        if strict_val < lowest_score:
   181→            lowest_score = strict_val
   182→            lowest_name = entry.get("name")
   183→    return lowest_name
   184→
   185→
   186→def _open_review_issue_counts(state: dict) -> dict[str, int]:
   187→    """Count open review findings grouped by subjective dimension key."""
   188→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   189→    if not isinstance(findings, dict):
   190→        return {}
   191→
   192→    counts: dict[str, int] = {}
   193→    for finding in findings.values():
   194→        if not isinstance(finding, dict):
   195→            continue
   196→        if finding.get("status") != "open" or finding.get("detector") != "review":
   197→            continue
   198→        detail = finding.get("detail", {})
   199→        dimension = ""
   200→        if isinstance(detail, dict):
   201→            dimension = str(detail.get("dimension", "")).strip()
   202→        if not dimension:
   203→            dimension = str(finding.get("dimension", "")).strip()
   204→        if not dimension:
   205→            continue
   206→        counts[dimension] = counts.get(dimension, 0) + 1
   207→    return counts
   208→
   209→
   210→def _render_objective_dimensions(
   211→    dim_scores: dict, *, lowest_name: str | None, bar_len: int
   212→) -> None:
   213→    """Print rows for objective (detector-based) dimensions."""
   214→    for dim in DIMENSIONS:
   215→        ds = dim_scores.get(dim.name)
   216→        if not ds:
   217→            continue
   218→        score_val = ds["score"]
   219→        strict_val = ds.get("strict", score_val)
   220→        checks = ds["checks"]
   221→
   222→        bar = dimension_bar(score_val, colorize_fn=colorize, bar_len=bar_len)
   223→
   224→        focus = colorize(" ←", "yellow") if dim.name == lowest_name else "  "
   225→        checks_str = f"{checks:>7,}"
   226→        action = dimension_action_type(dim.name)
   227→        print(
   228→            f"  {dim.name:<22} {checks_str}  {score_val:5.1f}%  {strict_val:5.1f}%  {bar}  T{dim.tier}  {action}{focus}"
   229→        )
   230→
   231→
   232→def _render_subjective_dimensions(
   233→    scorecard_subjective: list[dict],
   234→    *,
   235→    lowest_name: str | None,
   236→    bar_len: int,
   237→    review_issue_counts: dict[str, int],
   238→) -> None:
   239→    """Print rows for subjective (review-based) dimensions."""
   240→    if not scorecard_subjective:
   241→        return
   242→    print(
   243→        colorize(
   244→            "  ── Subjective Measures (matches scorecard.png) ──────────────────────",
   245→            "dim",
   246→        )
   247→    )
   248→    for entry in scorecard_subjective:
   249→        name = str(entry.get("name", "Unknown"))
   250→        score_val = float(entry.get("score", 0.0))
   251→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", score_val))
   252→        tier = 4
   253→
   254→        bar = dimension_bar(score_val, colorize_fn=colorize, bar_len=bar_len)
   255→
   256→        focus = colorize(" ←", "yellow") if name == lowest_name else "  "
   257→        checks_str = f"{'—':>7}"
   258→        stale_tag = colorize(" [stale]", "yellow") if entry.get("stale") else ""
   259→        placeholder_tag = (
   260→            colorize(" [unassessed]", "yellow") if entry.get("placeholder") else ""
   261→        )
   262→        dim_key = str(entry.get("dimension_key", "")).strip()
   263→        cli_keys = [
   264→            str(key).strip()
   265→            for key in entry.get("cli_keys", [])
   266→            if isinstance(key, str) and str(key).strip()
   267→        ]
   268→        if dim_key:
   269→            issue_count = int(review_issue_counts.get(dim_key, 0))
   270→        elif cli_keys:
   271→            issue_count = int(sum(review_issue_counts.get(key, 0) for key in cli_keys))
   272→        else:
   273→            issue_count = 0
   274→        issue_style = "yellow" if strict_val < 95.0 and issue_count == 0 else "dim"
   275→        issue_tag = colorize(f" [open issues: {issue_count}]", issue_style)
   276→        print(
   277→            f"  {name:<22} {checks_str}  {score_val:5.1f}%  {strict_val:5.1f}%  {bar}  T{tier}  {'review'}{focus}{stale_tag}"
   278→            f"{placeholder_tag}{issue_tag}"
   279→        )
   280→
   281→
   282→def _render_dimension_legend(scorecard_subjective: list[dict]) -> None:
   283→    """Print the legend footer and stale-dimension re-review hint."""
   284→    print(
   285→        colorize("  Health = open penalized | Strict = open + wontfix penalized", "dim")
   286→    )
   287→    print(
   288→        colorize(
   289→            "  Action: fix=auto-fixer | move=reorganize | refactor=manual rewrite | manual=review & fix",
   290→            "dim",
   291→        )
   292→    )
   293→    stale_keys = [
   294→        str(e.get("dimension_key"))
   295→        for e in scorecard_subjective
   296→        if e.get("stale") and e.get("dimension_key")
   297→    ]
   298→    if stale_keys:
   299→        n = len(stale_keys)
   300→        dims_arg = ",".join(stale_keys)
```
