1→"""Canonical scorecard projection helpers shared by command/reporting surfaces."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import re
     6→
     7→from desloppify.engine.planning.dimension_rows import (
     8→    scorecard_dimension_rows,
     9→)
    10→from desloppify.scoring import DISPLAY_NAMES
    11→
    12→_COMPONENT_TO_DIMENSION: dict[str, str] = {
    13→    "abstraction leverage": "abstraction_fitness",
    14→    "indirection cost": "abstraction_fitness",
    15→    "interface honesty": "abstraction_fitness",
    16→}
    17→
    18→
    19→def dimension_cli_key(dimension_name: str) -> str:
    20→    """Map a display name to a review --dimensions CLI key."""
    21→    cleaned = dimension_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
    22→    lowered = cleaned.lower()
    23→    if lowered in _COMPONENT_TO_DIMENSION:
    24→        return _COMPONENT_TO_DIMENSION[lowered]
    25→    for key, display in DISPLAY_NAMES.items():
    26→        if display.lower() == lowered:
    27→            return key
    28→    return re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9]+", "_", lowered).strip("_")
    29→
    30→
    31→def scorecard_dimension_cli_keys(name: str, data: dict) -> list[str]:
    32→    """Map scorecard subjective display names to review --dimensions keys."""
    33→    if name in ("Elegance", "Elegance (combined)"):
    34→        components = (
    35→            data.get("detectors", {})
    36→            .get("subjective_assessment", {})
    37→            .get("components", [])
    38→        )
    39→        if isinstance(components, list) and components:
    40→            keys = [dimension_cli_key(component) for component in components]
    41→        else:
    42→            keys = [
    43→                "high_level_elegance",
    44→                "mid_level_elegance",
    45→                "low_level_elegance",
    46→            ]
    47→    elif name in ("Abstraction fit", "Abstraction fit (combined)"):
    48→        components = (
    49→            data.get("detectors", {})
    50→            .get("subjective_assessment", {})
    51→            .get("components", [])
    52→        )
    53→        if isinstance(components, list) and components:
    54→            keys = [dimension_cli_key(component) for component in components]
    55→        else:
    56→            keys = ["abstraction_fitness"]
    57→    else:
    58→        keys = [dimension_cli_key(name)]
    59→
    60→    seen: set[str] = set()
    61→    ordered: list[str] = []
    62→    for key in keys:
    63→        if not key or key in seen:
    64→            continue
    65→        ordered.append(key)
    66→        seen.add(key)
    67→    return ordered
    68→
    69→
    70→def scorecard_subjective_entries(
    71→    state: dict,
    72→    *,
    73→    dim_scores: dict | None = None,
    74→) -> list[dict]:
    75→    """Return scorecard-subjective entries with score/strict/placeholder metadata."""
    76→    rows = scorecard_dimension_rows(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
    77→    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments") or {}
    78→    subjective_display_names = {display.lower() for display in DISPLAY_NAMES.values()}
    79→    subjective_display_names.update({"elegance", "elegance (combined)"})
    80→
    81→    entries: list[dict] = []
    82→    for name, data in rows:
    83→        detectors = data.get("detectors", {})
    84→        lowered_name = str(name).strip().lower()
    85→        is_subjective = (
    86→            "subjective_assessment" in detectors
    87→            or lowered_name in subjective_display_names
    88→        )
    89→        if not is_subjective:
    90→            continue
    91→        score = float(data.get("score", 0.0))
    92→        strict = float(data.get("strict", score))
    93→        assessment_meta = detectors.get("subjective_assessment", {})
    94→        placeholder = bool(
    95→            assessment_meta.get("placeholder")
    96→            or (
    97→                data.get("score", 0) == 0
    98→                and data.get("issues", 0) == 0
    99→                and data.get("checks", 0) == 0
   100→            )
```

1→"""Tool inventory helpers for narrative payloads."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from typing import Any
     6→
     7→from desloppify.intelligence.narrative._constants import DETECTOR_TOOLS
     8→from desloppify.intelligence.narrative.action_engine import supported_fixers
     9→from desloppify.intelligence.narrative.action_models import ToolFixer, ToolInventory
    10→from desloppify.state import StateModel
    11→
    12→
    13→def _move_reasons(by_detector: dict[str, int]) -> list[str]:
    14→    reasons: list[str] = []
    15→    if by_detector.get("orphaned", 0):
    16→        reasons.append(f"{by_detector['orphaned']} orphaned files")
    17→    if by_detector.get("coupling", 0):
    18→        reasons.append(f"{by_detector['coupling']} coupling violations")
    19→    if by_detector.get("single_use", 0):
    20→        reasons.append(f"{by_detector['single_use']} single-use files")
    21→    if by_detector.get("flat_dirs", 0):
    22→        reasons.append(f"{by_detector['flat_dirs']} flat directories")
    23→    if by_detector.get("naming", 0):
    24→        reasons.append(f"{by_detector['naming']} naming issues")
    25→    return reasons
    26→
    27→
    28→def _build_fixers(
    29→    by_detector: dict[str, int], state: StateModel, lang: str | None
    30→) -> list[ToolFixer]:
    31→    fixers: list[ToolFixer] = []
    32→    supported = supported_fixers(state, lang)
    33→
    34→    for detector, tool_info in DETECTOR_TOOLS.items():
    35→        if tool_info["action_type"] != "auto_fix":
    36→            continue
    37→        count = by_detector.get(detector, 0)
    38→        if count == 0:
    39→            continue
    40→
    41→        for fixer in tool_info["fixers"]:
    42→            if supported is not None and fixer not in supported:
    43→                continue
    44→            fixers.append(
    45→                {
    46→                    "name": fixer,
    47→                    "detector": detector,
    48→                    "open_count": count,
    49→                    "command": f"desloppify fix {fixer} --dry-run",
    50→                }
    51→            )
    52→
    53→    return fixers
    54→
    55→
    56→def compute_tools(
    57→    by_detector: dict[str, int],
    58→    state: StateModel,
    59→    lang: str | None,
    60→    badge: dict[str, Any],
    61→) -> ToolInventory:
    62→    move_keys = ["orphaned", "flat_dirs", "naming", "single_use", "coupling", "cycles"]
    63→    organizational_issues = sum(by_detector.get(detector, 0) for detector in move_keys)
    64→    reasons = _move_reasons(by_detector)
    65→
    66→    return {
    67→        "fixers": _build_fixers(by_detector, state, lang),
    68→        "move": {
    69→            "available": True,
    70→            "relevant": organizational_issues > 0,
    71→            "reason": " + ".join(reasons) if reasons else None,
    72→            "usage": "desloppify move <source> <dest> [--dry-run]",
    73→        },
    74→        "plan": {
    75→            "command": "desloppify plan",
    76→            "description": "Generate prioritized markdown cleanup plan",
    77→        },
    78→        "badge": badge,
    79→    }
    80→

    75→    ),
    76→    "finding_noise_global_budget": ConfigKey(
    77→        int,
    78→        0,
    79→        "Global cap for surfaced findings after per-detector budget (0 = unlimited)",
    80→    ),
    81→    "languages": ConfigKey(
    82→        dict, {}, "Language-specific settings {lang_name: {key: value}}"
    83→    ),
    84→}
    85→
    86→
    87→def default_config() -> dict[str, Any]:
    88→    """Return a config dict with all keys set to their defaults."""
    89→    return {k: copy.deepcopy(v.default) for k, v in CONFIG_SCHEMA.items()}
    90→
    91→
    92→def _coerce_target_strict_score(value: object) -> tuple[int, bool]:
    93→    """Coerce target strict score and report whether it is in range."""
    94→    try:
    95→        parsed = int(value)
    96→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
    97→        return MIN_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE, False
    98→    valid = MIN_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE <= parsed <= MAX_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE
    99→    return parsed, valid
   100→
   101→
   102→def load_config(path: Path | None = None) -> dict[str, Any]:
   103→    """Load config from disk, auto-migrating from state files if needed.
   104→
   105→    Fills missing keys with defaults. If no config.json exists, attempts
   106→    migration from state-*.json files.
   107→    """
   108→    p = path or CONFIG_FILE
   109→    if p.exists():
   110→        try:
   111→            config = json.loads(p.read_text())
   112→        except (json.JSONDecodeError, UnicodeDecodeError, OSError):
   113→            config = {}
   114→    else:
   115→        # First run — try migrating from state files
   116→        config = _migrate_from_state_files(p)
   117→
   118→    changed = False
   119→
   120→    # Fill missing keys with defaults
   121→    for key, schema in CONFIG_SCHEMA.items():
   122→        if key not in config:
   123→            config[key] = copy.deepcopy(schema.default)
   124→            changed = True
   125→        elif key == "badge_path":
   126→            try:
   127→                normalized = _validate_badge_path(str(config[key]))
   128→                if normalized != config[key]:
   129→                    config[key] = normalized
   130→                    changed = True
   131→            except ValueError:
   132→                config[key] = copy.deepcopy(schema.default)
   133→                changed = True
   134→
   135→    if changed and p.exists():
   136→        try:
   137→            save_config(config, p)
   138→        except OSError as exc:
   139→            log_best_effort_failure(logger, f"persist migrated config to {p}", exc)
   140→
   141→    return config
   142→
   143→
   144→def save_config(config: dict, path: Path | None = None) -> None:
   145→    """Save config to disk atomically."""
   146→    p = path or CONFIG_FILE
   147→    safe_write_text(p, json.dumps(config, indent=2) + "\n")
   148→
   149→
   150→def add_ignore_pattern(config: dict, pattern: str) -> None:
   151→    """Append a pattern to the ignore list (deduplicates)."""
   152→    ignores = config.setdefault("ignore", [])
   153→    if pattern not in ignores:
   154→        ignores.append(pattern)
   155→
   156→
   157→def set_ignore_metadata(config: dict, pattern: str, *, note: str, added_at: str) -> None:
   158→    """Record note + timestamp for an ignore pattern."""
   159→    meta = config.setdefault("ignore_metadata", {})
   160→    if not isinstance(meta, dict):
   161→        meta = {}
   162→        config["ignore_metadata"] = meta
   163→    meta[pattern] = {"note": note, "added_at": added_at}
   164→
   165→
   166→def _validate_badge_path(raw: str) -> str:
   167→    """Require badge_path to point to a filename (root or nested path)."""
   168→    value = raw.strip()
   169→    path = Path(value)
   170→    if (
   171→        not value
   172→        or value.endswith(("/", "\\"))
   173→        or path.name in {"", ".", ".."}
   174→    ):
   175→        raise ValueError(
   176→            "Expected file path for badge_path "
   177→            f"(example: scorecard.png or assets/scorecard.png), got: {raw}"
   178→        )
   179→    return value
   180→
```

def config_for_query(config: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Return a sanitized config dict suitable for query.json."""
    return {k: config.get(k, schema.default) for k, schema in CONFIG_SCHEMA.items()}


def _merge_config_value(config: dict, key: str, value: object) -> None:
    """Merge a config value into the target dict."""
    if key not in config:
        config[key] = copy.deepcopy(value)
        return
    if isinstance(value, list) and isinstance(config[key], list):
        for item in value:
            if item not in config[key]:
                config[key].append(item)
        return
    if isinstance(value, dict) and isinstance(config[key], dict):
        for dk, dv in value.items():
            if dk not in config[key]:
                config[key][dk] = copy.deepcopy(dv)
        return
```

## Comprehensive Scorecard & Badge Integration Points Report

Based on my thorough search of the desloppify codebase, here are **ALL** the places that touch scorecards, badge generation, and would need awareness of per-language badges:

---

## 1. CORE CONFIGURATION

### `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/core/config.py`
- **Line 44-49**: Two config keys in `CONFIG_SCHEMA`:
  - `"generate_scorecard"` (bool, default=True) — controls whether badge generation runs
  - `"badge_path"` (str, default="scorecard.png") — output path for the PNG
- **Line 125-133**: Badge path validation (`_validate_badge_path()`) ensures it's a filename, not a directory
- **Line 166-179**: `_validate_badge_path()` function — requires relative or absolute path to a file
- **Line 181+**: `config_for_query()` — exports sanitized config (includes both `generate_scorecard` and `badge_path`) to query.json

---

## 2. BADGE GENERATION & CLI INTEGRATION

### `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/output/scorecard.py`
**Critical hub for badge generation:**
- **Line 43-126**: `generate_scorecard(state, output_path)` — main function that:
  - Extracts overall/strict scores from state
  - Calls `prepare_scorecard_dimensions(state)` to get dimensions to display
  - Renders a PNG using PIL
  - Creates parent directories and saves atomically
- **Line 129-158**: `get_badge_config(args, config)` — resolver function that:
  - Checks `args.no_badge` (CLI flag) → disables if true
  - Checks `config.get("generate_scorecard")` → disables if false
  - Checks `DESLOPPIFY_NO_BADGE` env var → disables if "true"/"yes"/"1"
  - Resolves path from `args.badge_path` → `config.badge_path` → `DESLOPPIFY_BADGE_PATH` env var → default `"scorecard.png"`
  - Handles relative paths (resolved from PROJECT_ROOT) and absolute paths
- **Line 161-176**: `_scorecard_ignore_warning()` — generates warning text about ignore suppression for context
- **Line 179-199**: `__all__` export list includes `get_badge_config`, `generate_scorecard`, and dimension-handling functions

### CLI Arguments (Parser Groups)
- **`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/cli_support/parser_groups.py` (line 69-76)**:
  - `--no-badge` flag (bool) — skip scorecard image generation
  - `--badge-path <path>` argument — output path for scorecard image
  - Help text includes reference to `DESLOPPIFY_NO_BADGE` env var

---

## 3. SCAN COMMAND INTEGRATION

### `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/scan/scan_artifacts.py`
**Scanner integration point:**
- **Line 71-82**: `_load_scorecard_helpers()` — lazy-loads scorecard module (deferred because PIL is optional)
- **Line 85-176**: `emit_scorecard_badge()` — orchestrates badge generation after scan:
  - Loads helpers; if PIL missing, prints warning if user explicitly requested badge
  - Calls `get_badge_config(args, config)` to resolve path
  - Calls `generate_scorecard(state, badge_path)` to render PNG
  - Checks if README contains the badge path — if so, prints brief success message
  - If not in README, prints suggestion: `<img src="{rel_path}" width="100%">`
  - Returns tuple: (badge_path, OutputResult)
- **Line 154-176**: README detection — checks README.md/readme.md/README.MD for the relative path string

### `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/scan/scan.py`
- **Line 9-11**: Imports `emit_scorecard_badge` from scan_artifacts
- **Implicitly called** during `cmd_scan()` workflow (not shown in excerpt but confirmed by import)

---

## 4. SCORECARD RENDERING (Dimension Presentation)

### Per-Language Dimension Ordering
**`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/output/scorecard_parts/dimension_policy.py`**
- **Line 5**: `_SCORECARD_MAX_DIMENSIONS = 20` — hard cap on visible dimensions
- **Line 11-15**: `_ELEGANCE_COMPONENTS_BY_LANG` — per-language elegance component grouping (python/typescript/csharp all use same)
- **Line 21-40**: `_SUBJECTIVE_SCORECARD_ORDER_DEFAULT` — fallback ordering (40 dimension names)
- **Line 41-80**: `_SUBJECTIVE_SCORECARD_ORDER_BY_LANG` — **CRITICAL**: Language-specific priority ordering:
  - `"python"`: 10-item priority list
  - `"typescript"`: 11-item priority list (includes API coherence, Auth consistency, Stale migration)
  - `"csharp"`: 11-item priority list (same as typescript)
- **Line 81-87**: `_MECHANICAL_SCORECARD_DIMENSIONS` — objective dimensions (File health, Code quality, Duplication, Test health, Security)
- **Line 90-95**: `_compose_scorecard_dimensions()` — merges mechanical + subjective with language-specific priority; truncates to 20
- **Line 98-102**: `_SCORECARD_DIMENSIONS_BY_LANG` — final per-language dimension lists (python/typescript/csharp)

**`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/output/scorecard_parts/dimensions.py`**
- **Line 16-25**: `resolve_scorecard_lang(state)` — extracts most recent language from `state["scan_history"]`
- **Line 110-170**: `collapse_elegance_dimensions()` — merges High/Mid/Low elegance into single "Elegance" row
- **Line 129-205**: `prepare_scorecard_dimensions(state)` — main function that:
  - Calls `resolve_scorecard_lang(state)` to get current language
  - Calls `collapse_elegance_dimensions()` with language hint
  - Filters out placeholder/unassessed subjective entries
  - Calls `limit_scorecard_dimensions()` with language-specific ordering
  - Returns final (name, data) tuples for rendering
- **Line 131-158**: `limit_scorecard_dimensions()` — uses `_SUBJECTIVE_SCORECARD_ORDER_BY_LANG` to prioritize truncation

**`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/output/scorecard_parts/projection.py`**
- **Line 70-101**: `scorecard_subjective_entries()` — returns list of subjective dimension entries with metadata for query payload

  "narrative": {
    "tools": {
      "badge": {
        "generated": true,
        "in_readme": true,
        "path": "scorecard.png",
        "recommendation": null
      }
    },
    ...
  }
}
```

## CRITICAL INSIGHTS FOR MULTI-BADGE CHANGES

**When supporting per-language scorecard badges, these points MUST be updated:**

1. **`get_badge_config()` function** — currently returns single Path; needs to handle language parameter or return dict of paths
2. **`_SCORECARD_DIMENSIONS_BY_LANG`** — already language-aware; dimension selection is correct
3. **Narrative badge status** — `_compute_badge_status()` and `BadgeStatus` TypedDict assume single badge file; must expand to per-language
4. **ToolInventory.badge field** — currently single dict; may need list or dict keyed by language
5. **README detection** — checks for single path; must check for all generated paths
6. **Hardcoded text strings**:
   - "Scorecard dimensions (matches scorecard.png)" in scan_reporting_dimensions.py, status_parts/render.py, engine/planning/render.py
   - "Subjective Measures (matches scorecard.png)" in status_parts/render.py
7. **Query payload structure** — `config.badge_path` single string; needs expansion to map or array
8. **CLI args** — `--badge-path` is currently single path; may need language-specific variants
9. **Emit function** — `emit_scorecard_badge()` generates one file; needs to loop per language
10. **Test suite** — all badge_config tests assume single file and single path resolution

---

This is a complete map of all scorecard/badge integration points. The codebase is already partially aware of language differences (dimension ordering, elegance component grouping), but badge generation itself is entirely single-file. A per-language approach would require careful changes to the config schema, narrative types, and reporting output.
